- **D-119 Fundamentals transfer, share 0.75 (Jared approved 0.75 after the scan).** A lightsaber form's first session as a session's primary starts it at 0.75 x the character's highest form XP at that moment (FORM_TRANSFER_SHARE). It happens once per form (progression.formsStarted, progression slice v2; old saves count forms with XP as started), stays outside the 4.0x clamp like banked XP, applies to lightsaber forms only and never Force abilities, and creates no tier beat. Creation applies it to every form in the Padawan and Knight+ plans at the start of that phase. A never-trained form's basics drill opens with a teacher present (unlock "known or with a teacher"), which is how a new form is started. *Scan (docs-v2/calc):* share 0 / 0.5 / 0.6 / 0.70 / 0.75 / 0.8 / 0.9 give milestone 21 at -52 / -29 / -24 / -19 / -16 / -14 / -9%; 0.70 and above put every milestone within +/-20%.
- **D-120 Calibration re-run: proportional fill trimming and the rank-minimum reset (chosen in the build, from the D-51 rule Jared named).** *Trimming:* fill hours over the tiredness limit used to come off the last fill block, which skewed the Knight+ forms 71 : 29 instead of the plan's 2 : 1; the closed form then missed the day loop by -2.2 at 60 years (NPC test 2 allows +/-2). Trimming the largest block first fixed that but moved careers' main skills (Bounty hunter off by 5.8 in NPC test 7). Each 0.5 h now comes off the fill block with the largest remaining fraction of its planned hours: the shares hold, test 2 is within +/-1.2 and every archetype is within +/-2 (training-routines §4). *Results (K = 11.683, highest form):* 21: 4.2 y (-16%) | 41: 16.0 y | 61: 34.1 y (+6%) | 81: 56.7 y (+8%) | 101: 84.8 y (+9%). A 16-year-old Padawan has Shii-Cho 30.1, Soresu 34.2, Ataru 28.0. Y_avg recomputed. *Rank minimums (D-51 rule, read at 20.3):* Force Control 30 -> **34** (34.45), Force Sense 26 -> **31** (31.21), Willpower 48 -> **53** (53.94); highest form 39 (met at about 19.0), the Initiate Trials 24 / 14 (9.2 / 9.3) and Master 58 (35.2) stay. *Padawan selection (D-66 rule):* the window now opens at 10 (36 monthly rolls), divisor 940 (13.98% never chosen, seeded 14.11%).
- **D-121 Niman (Form VI) progression rules (Jared, 2026-11-17).** (1) Niman's fundamentals transfer is measured against the AVERAGE of the character's Form I, III, IV and V levels, not the highest form. Form V is the higher of Shien and Djem So, an untrained form counts as 0, and the 0.75 share applies to the XP of that average level (src/engine/forms.ts transferBaseXp, used in play, at creation and in the lifetime runner). (2) Synergy into Niman comes from each of Shii-Cho, Soresu, Ataru, Shien and Djem So at 0.10 with ceiling 0.6 of the source (relation nimanFoundation). This replaces the 0.05 foundation into Niman, so Makashi (Form II) no longer feeds it. (3) Niman drills have physical load 0.8 instead of 1.0 (still a physical block for ordering and the post-workout window). The lifetime test still passes; the Average does not train Niman.
- **D-122 Niman and Jar'Kai combat rules, recorded for M5 (Jared, 2026-11-17).** Niman matchups are 0 in every situation. Force abilities used while fighting in Niman get half the combat-integration penalty. Jar'Kai (dual blades, needs the equipment) requires Niman 30+, with 0.25 variant synergy between Jar'Kai and Niman. Recorded in architecture §15 ("Recorded for M5"); nothing is built before M5.

## 2026-11-17 — M4 (time advance)

D-123 is the plan Jared approved. Entries marked "chosen while Jared was away" were made during the build without him, under his standing instruction.

- **D-123 M4 plan (Jared approved, with two changes).** Plan file: C:\Users\dagge\.claude\plans\pasted-content-id-e6cf-m4-time-merry-mochi.md. *Changes by Jared:* (1) Obi-Wan's Clone Wars are ages 35-38 (Legends: born 57 BBY, war 22-19 BBY), about training years 32-35; Anakin's war from age 19. (2) Significant world events have a default daily chance of 1/150 in jumps (about 45% that a 90-day montage stops early); minor events 1/12. Both in config.
- **D-124 Order instruction stubs (chosen while Jared was away).** Without NPC generation (M5) and masters (M7), Order blocks need a teacher. Temple instructors teach as a teacher 10 levels ahead of the student in that skill (x1.05), a Padawan's master with no NPC named in the routine 30 levels ahead (x1.15), Temple peers are an even partner (x1.00, the D-40 "fill"), and teacher aptitude is 0. These are exactly the D-40 calibration factors, now produced by the real teacher-factor rule. Named stubs STUB_INSTRUCTOR_LEAD, STUB_MASTER_LEAD, STUB_TEACHER_APTITUDE (src/data/stubs.ts). A session's teacher is a `TrainingSource`: npc, partner, record, instructor, master, peers or solo.
- **D-125 Training records (chosen while Jared was away; Jared asked for holocron / archive learning).** src/data/trainingRecords.ts: Jedi Archives form records and lore texts (Jedi path), Sith archive records (Sith path), and two generic holocrons (any path). A record counts as a teacher with a fixed level per covered skill (progression-numbers §15): the teacher factor from its level, x0.9 for lightsaber forms (technique) and x1.0 otherwise, applied to the base factor. It opens a form's basics drill like a teacher, so the D-119 transfer applies. Archive form records teach at 35 and holocrons at 60-65 (design defaults; no Legends source gives numbers). Holocron possession is trusted until items exist (M8).
- **D-126 Self-study (chosen while Jared was away; Jared asked for it).** An unknown form's basics drill opens with no teacher. The session is solo (x0.90) and drills one rating below the normal pick (minimum Basic). The form is marked self-taught (progression.selfTaught, slice v3), and the lower rating holds for its sessions without teaching until a taught session (teacher, Order stub or record) clears it. The D-119 transfer applies to the first session as usual. Other locked activities stay locked (Juyo still needs the form known).
- **D-127 Combat XP built in M4 (chosen while Jared was away).** The lifetime test needs combat XP (combat-xp.md §5), so the combat branch of computeXp (D-55) is built now: src/engine/combatXp.ts implements §1-2 exactly, and the engine event `fightResolved {stakes, outcome, exchanges, forceUses}` credits it through progression, with fightsToday and combatXpToday in the progression slice (D-47), reset at midnight. Only the scripted lifetime fights emit it until duels (M5, stub STUB_SCRIPTED_COMBAT). *Found while testing:* with no training hours the D-104 clamp share of a skill used only in combat was 0, so its cap was 0 and every combat XP was discarded. A fight's exchange time (7.5 s each) now counts as its hours for the clamp share and the pace window (D-109 pattern), and as practice for rust removal. E, CI, the sparring ceiling and the bank stay in M5; the Average's yearly skirmish "never binds" (progression-numbers §12), so leaving the ceiling out does not move its calibration.
- **D-128 Routine defaults (chosen while Jared was away).** The default routine (training-routines §1): no priorities (the archetype default template: shares of the archetype weights before Knight, the D-41 0.5 split from Knight, or the character's focus weights from creation; the forms pool split by the form plan), standard effort, 6 training days a week, wake 06:00, 8 h sleep. The fill trains with Temple or unit peers (x1.00, the D-40 "fill"), outsiders alone (solo x0.90). The fill's 0.5 h rounding is carried from day to day (routines.fillCredit), exactly as the calc and the M3 runner did, so every share gets its hours on average; credit for a skill no longer in the shares is dropped. Training days spread evenly through the week (fractional days per week allowed, so a profile can train 300 of 368 days). Blocks run at standard intensity unless a priority sets one; push effort uses the push drill band (+10..+20).
- **D-129 The AI's routineRequested is a suggestion (chosen while Jared was away).** The narrative event stores a suggestion (skills, focus / regular / light, effort, span hint) that the player accepts or dismisses in the routine editor; it never changes the routine by itself and never affects the current turn. *Reason:* training-routines §1, "the player's own routine always decides".
- **D-130 The routine day loop runs real timed segments (chosen while Jared was away; refines the plan's "occupation routine").** src/state/advanceRoutine.ts runs each day as free time (rest) until bedtime, sleep to the wake time, sleepCompleted, then the scheduler's sessions one by one: resolveTraining (the story turn's code path) and advanceTime with occupation activity, with the conditional rests and the gaps around locked blocks as rest. So health and progression see real rest and sleep through their existing hooks, every §10 day-end hook runs after the day's sessions (midnight falls in the night), and no new occupation is needed. An interruption during the night lets the sleep finish, so the story resumes in the morning (Q48). *Measured:* the Average profile through this loop reaches 41 at 15.94 years with K = 11.683 unchanged (the M3 test's ±0.1 y holds), max day-end f 0.91, and the clamp never fires.
- **D-131 Engine-only rankSet (chosen while Jared was away).** The lifetime test needs Padawan and Knight at the profiles' ages while ranks stay fixed until M7. ranks gains an engine-only rankSet event; nothing in play emits it before M7 (promotionProposed and trials will).
- **D-132 The four-profile lifetime test through the real game (chosen while Jared was away; only profile inputs and scripted combat tuned).** tests/support/lifetime.ts builds a real GameState, sets the profile's routine per rank (routineSet), changes rank at the profile's ages (rankSet, D-131) and runs advanceByRoutine day by day; scripted fights enter as fightResolved. The M3 profile runner is deleted. Inputs (tests/lifetime/profiles.ts):
  - *Average:* unchanged (D-40); peacetime combat is the one yearly skirmish from Knight. Sparring from Padawan was tried and moved Adept to 15.77 (outside 16.0 ± 0.1), because the fights fed Soresu before it was the highest form; D-40 names only the skirmish.
  - *Obi-Wan:* push, 315 days, Padawan 13, Knight 25. Focus: the Initiate fill on Shii-Cho at intense intensity with Temple instructors; the Padawan plan keeps Shii-Cho as the solo form (Soresu 2 h + Shii-Cho 1 h); the Knight fill favors Soresu. Sparring 22 a year from 6 and 20 a year from 13 (combat 6-9%); the Clone Wars at ages 35-38 (D-123) with 24 serious duels a year (25%). *Why the Initiate inputs:* Adept is set before Padawan; with the default fill it came at 13.3 years (limit 12.96), and the highest form stalls about a year at the Padawan switch while Soresu restarts from the 0.75 transfer.
  - *Anakin:* prodigy combat and Force, push, 315 days, Padawan 9, Knight 20, single focus on Djem So (plan Djem So 3 h; focus shares Djem So 0.7), missions (skirmishes) 50 a year from 10, the Clone Wars 19-22 with 40 serious duels a year (war share 30-34%). His master uses the master stub (x1.15): without NPC generation the §12 "non-specialist master" (x1.05) cannot be modeled; the mission count absorbs it. At 60 missions a year he reached 61 at 7.99 years, a hair faster than 4.0x the Average (32.1 / 4 = 8.03), so 50 it is.
  - *Luke:* outsider, Force prodigy, push, 280 days, solo fill, Force Control (main specialty, weight 3 of 6, intense) and Force Sense with the Force holocron (D-125), Djem So and Force Speed at 1 each (focus shares Force Control 0.43). Force Speed is the body ability behind the late physical peak (§11a references 1 h/day); without it the peak fell at 83.
  - *Results (years of training; targets in brackets):* Average 4.17 (5.0) / 15.94 (16.0) / 32.95 (32.1) / 54.23 (52.7) / 80.9 (77.5). Obi-Wan 3.23 (3.4) / 10.39 (10.8) / 22.88 (21.7) / 36.95 (35.6) / 57.04 (52.4). Anakin 1.48 (1.3) / 4.24 (4.1) / 8.34 (8.2) / 13.73 (13.5) / 21.17 (19.9). Luke (Force Control) 1.77 (2.1) / 5.81 (6.9) / 12.30 (13.9) / 21.28 (22.8) / 32.39 (33.6); Force Control about 111 at 62 and still rising; effective physical peak at 65. No profile beats Anakin; no tier comes faster than 4.0x the Average; the clamp never fired.
  - *Readings chosen:* "no profile beats Anakin" is checked as every other profile needing at least 1.2 x Anakin's target per tier (Anakin's own test keeps him at or before it). progression-targets test 3 says "Force and effective physical peaks at 58-68" while progression-numbers test 3 says Force is "still rising at 60"; Force skills have no age cap, so the test checks the physical peak at 58-68 and Force still rising after 40 years and at 60.
  - *Y_avg re-measured.* The D-48 Y_avg table is the Average's yearly main-form XP, so it is re-measured from the real loop: 4: 580 | 8: 608 | 12: 545 | 16: 538 | 20: 562 | 24: 449 | 30: 407 | 40: 474 | 50: 463 | 60: 484 | 70: 444 | 80: 453 | 90: 254 | 98: 206 (the calc's 20: 526 and 24: 434 were 6-7% low).
- **D-133 The calc gains the centered state; phase paces re-measured (found in the build; root-cause fix).** Through the real loop the Average ran ahead of the closed form after Knighthood (60 years: 85.9 vs 82.5, NPC test 2 allows ±2; the Civilian +2.5). *Cause:* the game applies the centered state (x1.05 after 30+ minutes of meditation, through the next day, progression-numbers §17, D-108), and the Knight+ fill's Willpower meditation triggers it on most days; the calc (and the M3 runner) had no state of mind. docs-v2/calc/progression-sim.mjs now applies it; archetype-paces.mjs was re-run at the decided K = 11.683 (re-solving K with centered gives 11.694, a 0.1% change, and the src loop meets 16.0 ± 0.1 with 11.683, so K stays). Guardian Knight+ form pace 1.543 -> 1.590; all archetype paces copied into src/data/archetypes.ts. New NPC reference table: Padawan 16: 34.2 | Knight 30: 53.6 | Master 45: 68.4 | Elder 70: 87.8 | Obi-Wan check 80.2 | Anakin check 79.7 | trooper sergeant 50.3 | Sith warrior 75.5 (npc-generation.md). NPC test 2 now compares the closed form with the Average's training through the routine loop without its yearly skirmish (the closed form models training only; combat enters NPCs through war years): within ±2 at 10, 30 and 60 years.
- **D-134 Only jumps stop on an interruption (chosen while Jared was away).** TimeAdvance and TimeStep carry `jump` (montage and time skip, set by the routine day loop). World events roll only on jump days, and advanceTime stops on a hook's interruption only in a jump; in a story turn the hook's notice is enough (a deadline at midnight during a long scene does not cut the scene). Hooks may also return the RNG state after their rolls.
- **D-135 Thread escalation (chosen while Jared was away; answers 14-world-state question 4).** From the deadline: 1 when it is within 7 days, 2 on the deadline day, 3 when 3 days overdue (config THREAD_ESCALATION). Each step is the engine event threadEscalated (D-60), once per level. Levels 2 and 3 interrupt a jump once each; the jump stops in the morning. Threads without a deadline never escalate, and code never resolves or fails a thread (the story does, through threadResolved). This replaces the legacy rule that made every thread critical after 3 days (14-world-state bug 4). The D-54 "distracted" rule (an open deadline within 3 days) was already in progression.
- **D-136 Weighted world events (chosen while Jared was away; Jared set the chances, D-123).** src/data/worldEvents.config.ts and the world feature (slice v1: a log of the last 50 events, each marked told once a narration has carried it). Once per jump day: a significant event with chance 1/150, else a minor one with 1/12. Minor kinds: a message, rumors, galaxy news, an assigned duty (Jedi and Sith), a brief encounter with a known NPC, a teacher's remark. Significant kinds: a summons (Jedi and Sith), a mission offer (Knight and above, careers), a known NPC arrives with urgent news, trouble nearby, the situation around an open thread changes. The engine picks the kind and the NPC or thread involved (seeded); the AI narrates it. A significant event interrupts the jump; the story resumes that morning in moment pacing. Kinds and weights are design defaults. *Measured:* over 20,000 seeded days the rates match the config; 1 - (1 - 1/150)^90 = 45.3% of 90-day montages stop early.
- **D-137 Relationship drift and shared training (chosen while Jared was away; D-82 left drift to M4).** Teachers and training partners gain 0.2 points per hour of a shared routine session, up to 60 (engine event sharedTraining, emitted by the day loop). Without contact for 30 days a relationship drifts one point toward neutral every 30 days, stopping at ±20 (the edge of the neutral band). Design values in RELATIONSHIP_DRIFT.
- **D-138 Settling-in and staleness are derived; the Skip-ahead choice (chosen while Jared was away; D-18 approved the choice itself).** Neither is stored (amends architecture §2's pacing fields settlingIn and lastNoveltyDay): settling-in is the first 14 days after the first arrival at a major place (the campaign's starting place counts); staleness is 21+ days with no new NPC (firstMetDay), thread (openedDay) or visited place (firstVisitDay) and 21+ days at the current place, never while settling in. Locations gain currentSinceDay (slice v2; older saves count from the place's first visit). The state block tells the narrator ("Settling in: favor moment, no skip" / "STALE: the choices must include a forward jump"), and lists thread deadlines and untold world events. If the reply is stale and no choice jumps, the pipeline appends "Skip ahead: <top open thread>" (the nearest deadline, else the most recently active thread; plain "Skip ahead" with none) as a montage of 14 days, or up to the day before that thread's deadline, and logs it. Choices gain pacingHint montage / timeSkip and an optional spanDays.
- **D-139 Montage and time-skip turns (chosen while Jared was away).** A span picks its level: moment up to 15 minutes, scene up to 12 hours (12-24 hours clamp to a 12-hour scene, not a 1-day montage), montage up to 120 days, time skip beyond, clamped at 10 years; a requested level wins when the span fits its range. A jump requested without a span is 30 days (montage) or 90 days (time skip). Intent spans gain weeks, months (a twelfth of 368 days) and years. A jump runs the routine (the routine is the training; checks and a single activity do not apply), ends at the wake time on the target day (Q48), and its TurnDraft carries the montage report; the narration uses the montage or time-skip prompt, the report in the resolved block, and jump context caps (recentTurns 4,000, memory 6,000). Afterwards pacing is moment if a significant event interrupted (architecture §9), else scene, so the next turn's time is not read in jump-sized units. A jump always starts its own chapter (Q56), whatever its length. World events the narration was given are marked told after it.
- **D-140 Routine preview (chosen while Jared was away).** training-routines §5 is built by running the routine for 7 days on a copy of the state through the real day loop with no world rolls (src/state/routinePreview.ts), plus the typical training day's plan and its projected tiredness curve. Warnings: a day projected above f 1.0 (and fixed or locked blocks over the effort limit); trained skills not practiced this week that pass their rust grace period by its end; a teacher no longer ahead (the session note "no teaching benefit"); easy drills (challenge at or below the gap -20 value); refused sessions; unavailable teachers and trimmed fill. The drill-ceiling warning waits for combat experience (M5). Injury adjustments (training-routines §6 and test 7) wait for wounds (M5).
- **D-141 Routine and montage UI (chosen while Jared was away).** The Character tab gains a Routine card ("Routine and montage…") that opens a routine modal: effort, days a week, wake time, sleep hours, the Padawan's master, priorities (skill, focus / regular / light, and who teaches: alone, peers, Temple instructors, a known NPC as teacher or partner, an archive record or holocron), locked blocks, templates (save the routine in force, load, delete), the story's suggestion (accept / dismiss), the live preview (warnings, a typical day, a week's projected XP, a tiredness bar chart), "Save routine", and skip-ahead buttons (1 week, 1 month, 3 months, 6 months, 1 year, or a number of days; over 120 days is a time skip). Starting a jump saves an edited routine first. While the narration call runs, the feed plays the jump back day by day (about 4 seconds for any length; events appear on their day, an early stop is shown), then the turn shows the report card (dates, days, hours, XP and levels per skill, rust, relationships, events, adjustments, a day-by-day list). The intent prompt learns montage / timeSkip and the new span units; the contract tells the narrator about jump choices.
- **D-142 Montage spans on the calendar (Jared's bug: a "3 months" montage ran the wrong number of days).** *Root cause:* the editor's buttons sent fixed day counts (3 months = 92 days) and typed month spans were a flat 368/12 days, but months 1-8 have 31 days and 9-12 have 30, so a 3-month montage from month 1 day 1 stopped on month 3 day 31 instead of month 4 day 1. The report also counted the mornings planned (one fewer than the days that passed when a jump starts after the wake time). *Fix:* whole months and years now end on the same day of the month that many months later, clamped to the month's length (month 8 day 31 + 1 month = month 9 day 30), at the wake time (engine addCalendarMonths, turn jumpTargetDay). Days and weeks still count days; a fractional month or a span past the 10-year cap counts days. The buttons send 1 week, 1 / 3 / 6 months and 1 year as calendar spans, and the report's daysCompleted is the calendar days that passed.

## 2026-11-17 — M5 (combat and health)

- **D-143 M5 plan (Jared approved revision 2).** Plan file: C:\Users\dagge\.claude\plans\curried-stirring-piglet.md. *Jared's rulings:* (A) Order classes count as sparring for applied experience E: the Initiate forms class is peer sparring (+0.2 per session) *(amended by D-163: the instructors are a stronger partner, +0.4 a session, still up to 15)*, the Padawan master session master sparring (+0.4); sparring E stays capped at 15 (ceiling 40); profiles get real Padawan missions; K and the paces do not change. (B) Lifetime fights become real duels against generated opponents at the scripted gap, with wounds, healing and injury adjustments; a Dying profile is always stabilized; only fight counts, gaps and opponent kinds are retuned. (D) Duel call budget, see D-145. (E) Allies stay story-only; opponents can be marked engagedElsewhere (combatStarted, and an engine-checked opponentEngagement event); a full ally side is recorded under architecture §15. (F) Healing multipliers, see D-150. (G) The NPC generation seed is a hash of the campaign seed and the NPC's ID. (H) Form stamina, see D-147. (I) Sequences, see D-146. (J) Report only at step 9: each profile's main-form E at its Knight and Master milestones, the share of drill XP banked, and the milestone spread over 5 extra seeds (the gate stays on the fixed seed). (K) The duel panel and health card fit the iPad fixed-box layout; the duel log scrolls in its own box. (L) Recorded for M6, not built: Force Body (a fatigue debt held while active, failed upkeep costs Vitality or wounds, the debt lands at once at the end through the suppression crash helper; the only way around the sequence cap's fatigue reduction), Battlemind (like Force Valor; lowers the exertion rate and the age penalty, no debt, synergy with Vaapad and Juyo), Endure (Crucitorn) as pain resistance on the wound penalty; Force Valor stays Force load only; Jared supplies the numbers in M6. (M) End of production: a character-traits system, first entry Tactical Fighter (+1 to the sequence cap), on the architecture §15 future list.
- **D-144 Sparring modes and Sith academies (Jared).** Three modes chosen by rank, faction, era and setting (src/data/combat.config.ts SPARRING_MODES, SPARRING_DEFAULT_BY_RANK): *training saber / low-power setting* (default for Initiates and Sith acolytes, anyone may choose it): hits take Vitality only, no bypass, no wounds; the first side Spent loses. *Controlled live blades* (default for Jedi Padawans and above): a strike that would land ends the bout as a point to the striker, no damage; the accident: a strike margin of 16+ in an exchange where either side rolled a natural 1 lands as a real graze. *Sith academy*: live blades and real wounds; academy trials use lethal stakes; outside an academy a Sith spar uses the opponent's chosen stakes. Environment accidents in every mode: a hazard roll per bout from the terrain tags (usually minor, moderate or worse for a real fall); a flat floor about 1 in 200 bouts for a minor injury. Academies (SITH_ACADEMIES), checked against Docs/SWRPG lore: Korriban in the Jedi Civil War 3,959-3,956 BBY (Timeline :239; end year confirmed by Jared), the Trayus Academy on Malachor V 3,954-3,951 BBY (Timeline :266, :276; the academy is attested, its training is not: Sith academy rules as a design default, Jared), Korriban in the Great Galactic War 3,681-3,636 BBY (Timeline :322, :506; the academy era and end year by Jared), Korriban under the Brotherhood of Darkness about 1,010-1,000 BBY (Timeline :572-579, Characters_10 :62, :293; end at Ruusan confirmed). The Legacy-era One Sith on Korriban are left out (no file mentions an academy or live-blade training) and recorded under §15 for a Legacy-era campaign.
- **D-145 Duel call budget: quiet and narrated beats (Jared).** Quiet beats make no AI call: a +/-2 track swing, the every-5-exchanges stop, or a strike that misses or is blocked. They show the roll cards and one short line from code templates (src/data/combatLines.ts); the player picks a stance or tactic, or presses Fight on, which keeps resolving with the current stance until something significant happens. Narrated beats are the only ones that call the narrator: a hit that wounds, a maneuver that lands (disarm, knockdown, position), Spent, a Force action, a sequence, incapacitation, the end of the fight, or the player pressing Narrate. One call covers every exchange since the last narrated beat. Resolve to end plays out the rest with the current stance and narrates once. Target about 6-10 calls for a long Knight-level duel; a fixture test checks that a 150-exchange duel with default play uses 12 calls or fewer (DUEL_CALL_BUDGET).
- **D-146 Sequences: typed multi-step actions in combat (Jared).** The intent call may return `sequence: [step]` (at most 5 in the schema); each step is a stance or tactic, maneuver, Force action or strike, and may name a terrain object. The engine resolves the steps in order as exchanges, the opponent rolling every step, and all dice resolve before one narration call (a typed sequence uses one narration call, plus the intent call). Chain: a step's success passes its bonus to the next (advantage, an opening, or split defense +2 when a thrown object and a strike land together); building pressure +1 per successful step, up to +3 for the chain, stacking with the others. The chain stops at the first failed step or when the opponent reaches a strike, which becomes a narrated beat; the rest is dropped, not refunded. A natural 1 always fails a step. Fairness: against an equal opponent at the largest stacked bonus, no step may succeed more than 90% of the time (else the cap is lowered); a chain must always be able to fail. Length cap, in order: by rank (Padawan 2, Knight 3, Master 4; 5 when the main form and the Force skill used are both 80+; Initiates and acolytes 1; careers and outsiders 2, a design default), +1 when starting hidden, -1 at high mental fatigue (f_mental 0.8+), -2 when exhausted or past the limit (Spent, or a fatigue or Force meter above 1.0), never below 1. Terrain objects come from combatStarted terrain tags with a size class (Force load, hazard damage) and a seeded per-fight supply. Opponent adaptation: +1 defense per earlier use of the same pattern (Force ability + maneuver), up to +3; a pattern's first use counts as surprise for its opening bonus. Costs: normal exertion per step and Force load per Force step (health §2 backlash and collapse). All numbers in src/data/sequence.config.ts and src/data/terrain.ts, tunable.
- **D-147 Form matchups and stamina (built from the lore; stamina by Jared).** src/data/formMatchups.ts, from SW_Force_Abilities_Forms_v3_complete.md:176-202. A matchup is a net modifier (+/-1) on the form user's total: Shii-Cho +1 against several opponents; Makashi +1 one-on-one against a saber and -1 against several; Soresu +1 vs blasters; Ataru -1 in confined space; Shien +1 vs blasters (and the 8+ redirect); Djem So +1 against Soresu (design default); Sokan +1 on open terrain; Zero-G +1 in zero gravity; Saberstaff +1 against several opponents; Jar'Kai -1 in confined space; Niman 0 everywhere (D-122); Juyo, Vaapad, Tràkata and Dun Möch 0 (entries present). Exertion multiplier per form (Jared): Ataru x1.25, Soresu x0.85, Juyo and Vaapad x1.15, all others x1.0; tunable in the step 3 duel-length test.
- **D-148 Weapons and armor until M8 (STUB_WEAPONS, STUB_DUAL_BLADES).** src/data/weapons.ts: Jedi and Sith carry a lightsaber (Initiates and acolytes a training saber), soldiers a blaster rifle and medium armor, Mandalorians a pistol and heavy beskar (saber-resistant), smugglers and bounty hunters a pistol, civilians fight unarmed. Jar'Kai needs Niman 30+ and dual blades; nobody has dual blades until items, so Jar'Kai cannot be picked in a duel yet.
- **D-149 Health design defaults the spec leaves open.** Location penalties (health §5 gives targets, not numbers): per wound on its targets, minor 2%, moderate 5%, severe 10%, critical 15%, mortal 20% (arm and hand: forms, melee, Strength; leg: Agility; head: Force and mental skills; torso: everything; from Docs/SW_Injury_System_v1.md:72-81). Hazards (hazardOccurred, intent.risk, sparring accidents, thrown objects): D = base by type (fall 14, fire 12, explosion 25, crash 22, poison 10, exposure 8, impact 12) x severity (x0.5 / x1 / x2), with part of D bypassing Vitality (0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0), so severity 1 is a minor wound, 2 moderate and 3 severe for a fall; falls favour the legs. A healed torso wound's lasting injury is burns or lungs, a head wound's is head trauma (mental and Force 10%), a limb's is joint damage; severed limbs are a lost hand, arm or leg. Training the injured part (x0.5 healing) risks making it worse at 2% per day. Stabilizing is a moderate Medicine check (or the character's Force Heal (Self), or a present NPC healer). Prosthetics until items (stubProsthetic): a quality cybernetic for the Jedi, the Sith and Republic or Imperial soldiers, a basic prosthetic otherwise.
- **D-150 Healing multipliers (Jared).** Medical care x2 while under care; a bacta tank x4 for its hours and replaces medical care (they do not stack); a Force healer multiplies on top, x(1 + healer level / 50); training the injured part x0.5. The combined multiplier is capped at x6 (HEAL_MULT).
- **D-151 NPC generation inputs (design defaults).** src/data/npcGen.config.ts. Archetype by role (Jedi roles: Guardian 0.4, Consular 0.35, Sentinel 0.25; Sith roles: Warrior 0.6, Inquisitor 0.4; soldiers and officers: soldier; pilots and smugglers: smuggler; criminals: smuggler or bounty hunter; bounty hunters; Mandalorian soldiers and hunters: Mandalorian; everyone else: civilian). Rank from role for Jedi and Sith roles, none for careers. Default age when the AI gives none (Initiate 9, Padawan 16, Knight 30, Master 50, acolyte 16, Sith Lord 40, soldier 28, officer 38, else 35). Talents per npc-generation §2; the "everyone else" row (80 / 17 / 3 / 0.2) sums to 100.2, so "none" is 79.8. War years from the Great Galactic War (3,681-3,653 BBY) for combatant roles in its factions. Minion kinds (battle droid, trooper, elite trooper, thug, brawler, Sith acolyte) with grade chances; years by grade 2 / 5 / 10 / 15 / 20 at pace 1.0 (npc-generation §6). The minion count per group in combatStarted (1-12) is a new clamped AI amount (amends architecture §2).
- **D-152 Canon NPCs for 3,661 BBY (npc-generation §5).** src/data/canonNpcs.ts: 34 figures from SW_Characters_09_GreatGalacticWar_ColdWar.md (main sheets and the bench), keyed by the lore index canon ID so the D-81 match links them. Each sheet is a snapshot (mostly the figure's prime); the engine scales its levels to the current date by the closed-form growth ratio of the entry's archetype, so Satele Shan is a 38-year-old Knight in 3,661 rather than the sheet's Grand Master, and Malgus is slightly below his Sacking peak (the lore change-notes ask for exactly this). Vitiate, Lord Scourge and the Dread Masters are fixed (immortal or centuries-old; the justification is in the data). Sub-ability percentages set the sub-ability to base x percent; abilities not yet in the skill list stay in `unmapped` for the M6 catalog. Excluded: Kao Cen Darach (dead 3,681), Revan (in stasis), the eight class protagonists, Kira Carsen and Theron Shan (children or not yet active), the generic war-generation statline. *Questions for Jared:* most birth years are design defaults (the lore gives only Satele 3,699, Marr 3,702 and Vitiate 5,113); are Darth Vindican and Master Ngani Zho (both killed by Malgus, no date in the files) alive in 3,661; how old is Aric Jorgan in 3,661; the snapshot years of the prime sheets (3,643 for the Galactic War peaks, 3,653 for Malgus and Shae Vizla, 3,640 for Marr) are design defaults.
- **D-153 Flag registry built (flag-registry.md).** src/data/flags.ts lists every flag of §2, §4, §5 and §6 with its sources; param flags are written `<id>:<param>` (heroicAct:<fightId>). No rank-gating or engine flag allows `ai` (test).
- **D-154 Duel design defaults (rolls.md names these without numbers).** Stances: aggressive (attack +1, defense -1), balanced, defensive (defense +1, attack -1). Maneuver effects: position +1 while held, a knockdown starts the next exchange at advantage 2 for the striker, a disarm gives the disarmed side attack -4 until rearmed. Opponent fate when the player is incapacitated: spared in a spar or skirmish; in a serious fight captured or left, and in a lethal fight a killing strike, capture or left, rolled by the opponent's group (Sith 70 / 25 / 5, military 40 / 55 / 5, underworld 50 / 30 / 20, Jedi 0 / 90 / 10, others 30 / 40 / 30); a killing strike is a mortal wound, so the dying timer leaves room for a rescue. The player chooses spare, capture or kill for an incapacitated NPC. heroicAct: a won lethal fight against an opponent 10+ levels stronger, or ranked Sith Lord or Master and above. Recording review (progression-numbers §19): +5% XP for 3 days, at most once in 7, after a recorded fight (Temple halls and academy spars).
- **D-155 No special year; canon rosters by era (Jared, 2026-11-17).** 3,661 BBY was only Jared's old character's start date; nothing in the game favours it. *Canon:* src/data/canonNpcs.ts aggregates one roster file per lore era (src/data/canon/, matching Docs/SWRPG lore/SW_Characters_NN_*.md); each entry has a birth year, a death year when known, earlier roles with the year they end, unavailable ranges (stasis, prison, hiding) and a dated level snapshot. Who is present at a campaign date is computed (src/engine/canonRoster.ts): born, not dead before that year (the death year counts as present), not unavailable, and old enough for the role held then (ROLE_MIN_AGE, design defaults: Padawan 10, Knight 18, Master 31, acolyte 8, Sith Lord 18, soldier 18, officer 22, politician 25, official 20, others per npcGen.config.ts). Era 09 now spans 3,681-3,630 BBY and is complete: 46 figures, including those who die early (Kao Cen Darach 3,681, Darth Vindican) and those who appear late (the Galactic War heroes, Theron Shan, Kira Carsen, Revan after his stasis). Every year is marked `lore` or `estimate` (Jared kept the estimates). *D-152 answers (Jared):* Ngani Zho alive until about 3,643 (killed by Malgus in the Galactic War); Darth Vindican dies before 3,653, year uncertain (estimate 3,655); Aric Jorgan born about 3,675 (estimate), so never an active soldier before 3,657; the snapshot years stay as estimated. *Every era equal:* recorded under architecture §15 (one roster per era, same detail, added over time; the Old Republic came first only as Jared's preferred start). *No year-specific defaults:* the creation form has no default year (an era picker from the lore index fills the era's first year, or the player types a date; DEFAULT_START became DEFAULT_START_SECOND, 07:00); core-gm.md no longer names 3,661 or Zym's Temple, and the state block gives the Jedi Order's seat for the campaign date; the Temple in creation facts and the state block comes from src/data/orderSeats.ts, which covers every era (Tython, Coruscant to 3,653, Tython, Coruscant again, none after 19 BBY, Yavin 4 from 11 ABY, Ossus, none after the Massacre at Ossus), with the undated boundaries marked as estimates (return to Coruscant 3,600 BBY, Ossus from 36 ABY: questions for Jared). Tests use a neutral date (tests/support/dates.ts, TEST_YEAR -1032); tests that check lore content use LORE_TEST_YEAR -3661 as a lore fact (the Hydian Way blockade begins that year). The lifetime runner starts on the neutral date. character-creation.md §1 and phase1-answers.md are amended. A roster test checks presence and absence at 3,681, 3,653 and 3,640 BBY.
- **D-156 Duel length: pressure after a missed strike and half precognitive defense (Jared).** With rolls.md §3 exactly, the duel engine reproduced the win-rate table (49 / 60 / 69 / 84 / 93 / 96 / 99%) but duels ran about 1.8x too long (medians 63 / 65 / 60 / 53 / 44 / 35 / 24 against 34 / 33 / 34 / 29 / 24 / 20 / 13); exertion is symmetric and even with no precognitive defense a gap-0 duel took 47. *Rules:* a strike that misses or is blocked keeps the pressure, the track moving 1 step toward 0 instead of resetting (MISSED_STRIKE_STEP); precognitive defense is min(0.25, Force Sense / 400) instead of min(0.5, Force Sense / 200). *Found while testing (root causes, same decision):* (1) one level of difference changed the odds by 4 points (rolls test 5 allows 2.5) because a fractional level bonus tipped the integer band edges (+0.1 turned a margin-1 tie into a step); bands now read the margin rounded to the nearest point. (2) Position, matchups, footing and numbers were applied to attack rolls only; they are on the side's total (rolls §5), so they now count on attack and defense (stance and state of mind still split). *Measured, 10,000 duels per gap:* wins 49.4 / 61.1 / 68.4 / 83.2 / 91.2 / 95.4 / 98.6%, medians 37 / 35 / 36 / 31 / 27 / 23 / 17 (all within +/-3 points and +/-5 exchanges). Test 1 judges gaps 0-10 by the §3 band (30-40, Jared) and gaps 20+ by +/-5. Scenario table: straight 25.8% (23), surprise then an opening 33.8% (32), high ground 70.2% (68), reckless 80.1% (81); "then an opening" is read as the attacker holding at least +2 after the surprise strike, which the pressure rule already gives. *Deflection check (Jared asked, rolls §4):* a Jedi 50 (Soresu, Force Sense 50) against a named trooper with a blaster rifle and medium armor, 4,000 duels each, old precog vs new: trooper 30: wins 100.0 -> 99.9%, Jedi wounded 5.2 -> 5.9%; trooper 40: 99.5 -> 99.4%, 9.1 -> 10.6%; trooper 50: 98.5 -> 97.5%, 16.7 -> 18.5%. The ranged balance moves by 1-2 points. rolls.md §3 and §8 are amended.
- **D-157 Minions fold when Spent (Jared; health §9).** A minion (minion groups from combatStarted only) leaves the fight when Spent as well as on a moderate wound; named NPCs and the player keep the normal rule. The narration decides how a folded minion goes down (knocked out, disarmed, flees, killed); whether it counts as dead follows the fight's stakes. *Measured:* rolls test 3 (Jedi 70, Force Sense 70, Soresu vs a level-15 trooper minion, 47 Vitality) is still 100% with median 5 (three saber grazes Spend 47), so the target is set from the result: median 5 or fewer. Group fights (minions, the numbers modifier +1 per extra attacker up to +3): Jedi 50 vs 4 troopers of 30: 69% wins, median 66 exchanges; vs 8: 8%, 89; Jedi 70 vs 6 troopers of 40: 53%, 91. *For Jared:* groups are harsh on Jedi, because every extra trooper adds a numbers point and each must still be beaten one at a time; a lore check of the intended balance is recorded as an end-of-milestone question. health.md §9 and test 10 are amended.
- **D-158 Minion coordination, cut-down, beskar by era (Jared, 2026-11-18).** *Problem (measured):* minion groups were far too dangerous for Jedi; a Jedi at 50 against 10 troopers 30 levels weaker won 33% and was wounded 87% of the time, because each extra attacker added +1 numbers (max +3 = 30 levels) and the track reset for every new minion. *Rules:* (1) **Coordination per minion kind** (npcGen.config MINION_KINDS): the numbers bonus is +1 per extra attacker engaged x its coordination, rounded down, max +3 (named fighters count 1). B1 battle droids, militia, thugs 0; line troopers (clones, Republic troopers, stormtroopers) 0.5; elite squads (ARC, commandos, Imperial special forces, Havoc Squad-type) 1.0; Mandalorians 1.0 with their higher grades and heavy armor. Design defaults for Jared to check: brawlers 0, B2 super battle droids 0 (tougher: Vitality x1.2, heavy weapon and armor), droidekas 0.5 (Vitality x1.5, no shield model yet), beasts 0 (Vitality x1.8), Sith acolytes 0. Each kind also has a Vitality multiplier (B1 x0.6). (2) **Cut-down** (combat.config CUT_DOWN_MARGIN 6): against a minion, a build-up margin of 6+ is already a hit (quality by the margin), unless the minion wears saber-resistant armor (any, not only beskar). Hits on minions are quiet beats (minionDown) so groups do not spend the call budget. (3) **Cleave** (keeping the advantage after taking a minion out) is NOT built; recorded as a candidate Shii-Cho / Sokan form trait against groups for M6 or later. (4) **Beskar by era** (BESKAR_BY_ERA, rolled per Mandalorian; the curve is lore, the rates design defaults): Old Republic eras through the Great Galactic War 0.6 (also the years up to the New Sith Wars); New Sith Wars 0.45; the late Republic and the Clone Wars 0.15; the Empire through the Yuuzhan Vong War 0.05; after the Vong war 0.35. Non-beskar Mandalorians keep heavy armor and their grade but are not saber-resistant, so cut-down applies. Named Mandalorians use canon armor (CanonNpc.armor; Jango and Boba Fett: durasteel, when their era files exist), else a seeded roll by era. (5) Saber-resistant armor and weapons for anyone else (cortosis weave, personal beskar, phrik) belong to the M8 equipment system; until then STUB_WEAPONS gives saber resistance only through the Mandalorian rule (architecture §15, M8 row). *Measured* (Jedi at 50, 2,000 fights each, win % / wounded %; target in brackets, +/-10 points):
  | Group | Djem So (reference) | Soresu | Shii-Cho |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 10 B1 at -30 (99+ / <=10) | 98.3 / 18.5 | 99.9 / 7.3 | 99.2 / 12.3 |
  | 10 militia at -30 (99+ / <=10) | 99.6 / 15.7 | 100 / 6.3 | 100 / 10.3 |
  | 6 line troopers at -20 (85-95 / 25-40) | 93.7 / 29.1 | 99.3 / 14.1 | 98.4 / 19.1 |
  | 6 elite troopers at -10 (55-70 / 55-75) | 61.7 / 66.0 | 88.9 / 38.5 | 79.7 / 49.0 |
  | 4 Mandalorians at -10, all beskar (32 / 89) | 34.9 / 87.2 | 76.2 / 57.9 | 58.1 / 73.7 |
  | 4 Mandalorians, mixed at the Old Republic rate 0.6 | 58.1 / 71.8 | 88.7 / 42.9 | 77.0 / 55.0 |
  | 4 Mandalorians, all durasteel | 95.9 / 35.5 | 99.7 / 17.4 | 99.0 / 24.8 |
  | 6 B2 at -20 | 90.3 / 23.3 | 96.0 / 10.9 | 93.2 / 15.7 |
  | 4 droidekas at -20 | 90.5 / 21.3 | 97.3 / 7.8 | 94.4 / 11.9 |
  | 3 beasts at -10 | 100 / 10.6 | 100 / 11.5 | 100 / 8.5 |
  | 6 thugs at -30 | 100 / 9.7 | 100 / 4.1 | 100 / 6.0 |
  | 3 Sith acolytes at -20 | 90.9 / 31.1 | 92.4 / 28.2 | 93.9 / 24.9 |
  Form bonuses apply on top (Soresu and Shien vs blasters, Shii-Cho and Saberstaff vs several opponents). health.md §9 and npc-generation.md §6 are amended.
- **D-159 Lifetime fights are real duels; the closed-form comparisons keep the yearly mission (D-143 A/B; in progress).** tests/support/lifetime.ts runs each scheduled fight through the duel engine against a generated opponent at the scripted gap (a Force-using duelist, or a minion group of a MINION_KINDS kind), with a rolled outcome; wounds and Vitality go through healthSynced, a Dying profile is stabilized, Order and military profiles get 30 days of medical care after a wounding fight, and combat XP, E and CI come from the duel log through fightResolved. The scripted-combat stub is retired. First schedules (to be retuned on fight counts, gaps and kinds only): Average one mission a year from 12 (4 line troopers at -20); Obi-Wan spars plus 3 missions a year from 13 and the Clone Wars at 35-38 mostly droids (30 B1 groups, 10 B2 groups, 4 duels a year); Anakin 25 missions and 4 duels a year from 10, the Clone Wars 19-22 (40 B1 groups, 12 B2 groups, 3 elite squads, 6 duels a year), then 25 missions a year; Luke 30 stormtrooper fights a year at 19-24, the Vong war 44-50 (14 elite fights, 6 duels a year). *Why the comparisons changed:* with E only from sparring (capped at 15) the drill ceiling holds every form at 40, so the training-only runs of NPC test 2 and the archetype reachability test stalled at about 43 against the closed form's 52; they now run with the Average's one real mission a year from Padawan (its combat XP is about 1% of the yearly main-form XP). Baseline before real duels (ceiling + Order-class E only): Average 41 at 17.0 y, Obi-Wan 41 at 14.0 y (target 10.8), archetypes 43 vs 52 at 25 years.
- **D-164 Lost and prosthetic limbs cannot be severed again (Jared, 2026-11-18; bug fix).** *Problem (measured):* the hit-location roll and the limb rule ignored limbs already gone, so a limb severed earlier in a fight could be severed again (Anakin's step-9 run had two lostArm leftArm lasting injuries on the same day) and a cybernetic limb could be severed into a new lasting injury. *Rule:* a limb severed earlier in the same fight leaves the location roll (a lost arm takes its hand); a limb lost before the fight carries its prosthetic, and a hit there costs Vitality only (no wound, no sever) until M8 makes it equipment damage (architecture §15 M8 row). engine limbState (lasting.ts) feeds damageFighter (duel.ts). Regression test in tests/engine/duel.test.ts (fails without the fix). health.md §5 amended.
- **D-165 The day projection starts from the actual meters (Jared, 2026-11-18; bug fix, live game too).** *Problem (measured):* planDay projected the day from afterSleep(current meters), but the day loop plans at wake, after sleepCompleted has already applied the night; exertion earlier that day (a fight at dawn) was slept off a second time, and the Average's fight days ended at f 1.34 against the standard limit 1.1 (0.91 without fights). *Rule:* the projection uses the meters at planning time; only a look ahead (the routine preview's typical day, planDay opts.fromSleep) starts from a night's sleep. training-routines test 5 has a dawn-fight case; training-routines.md §9 test 5 amended.
  *Addendum (same day):* with the honest projection, a Padawan's fixed Order blocks alone could still end a fight day at f 1.34. When the fixed blocks alone pass the limit because the day started tired, they are trimmed the same way as the fill, down to the level they reach on a fresh day (the Order excuses the exertion, never an ordinary day's requirements); player-locked blocks are never trimmed. *Pre-existing, reported:* a 13-year-old Padawan's Order day alone ends near f 0.97 against the standard limit 0.9 even when fresh (the M4 requirements); that is not cut.
- **D-162 Bacta in war years (Jared, 2026-11-18; a lifetime-harness input).** In a profile's war years (LifeProfile.warAges: Obi-Wan 35-38, Anakin 19-22, Luke 19-24 and 44-50) a wounding fight puts the profile in a bacta tank (x4) long enough for the worst new moderate-or-worse wound (a moderate 42 h); the tank days replace the routine (no training). Bacta was standard in the Republic's military medical care in the Legends Clone Wars. Outside war, Order and military profiles keep 30 days of medical care (x2).
- **D-163 Sparring a stronger partner, group sparring (Jared, 2026-11-18).** *Problem (measured, step 9):* with sparring E capped at 15, a peacetime swordmaster with no missions hit the drill ceiling at 40: main form 47.3 at 40 and 53.9 at 60, 61 at age 91, bank share 33% (more peer spars changed nothing). *Rules:* a partner at least 10 levels above the student's form level (a master counts only then) is a stronger partner: +0.4 E a session, sparring E rising toward the partner's main-form level - 25 (never capped below 15), scaled by (1 - E/120); anyone else is a peer (+0.2, sparring E capped at 15). Group sparring: 2-3 partners at once count as one at the strongest's level + 10 per extra partner (at most 3); the bout is harder by the numbers modifier. Order classes counted as sparring (D-143 A) follow the same rule by the teacher's level: the instructor stub (10 ahead) is stronger but its level - 25 stays under 15 below level 40; the stub master (30 ahead) lifts sparring E to the student's level + 5 (so the Initiate class now gains at 0.4 a session up to 15; *accepted by Jared as an amendment to D-143 A*). Creation mirrors this (starting E = the Padawan-end form level + 5 for a Knight). Engine: sparringE, sparGroupLevel, eGainFor (appliedExperience.ts); E_GAIN.sparMaster renamed sparStronger; the fightResolved master flag removed (the partner's level decides). *Recorded for M7 (not built):* instructors get 35% instead of 25% of drill XP above the ceiling for the forms they teach (architecture §15). *Measured (fixed seed):* the Cin Drallig case (the Average, no missions, no group spars) now: main form 64.0 at 40, 74.5 at 60, 61 at 33.3 y (age 37, on target), bank share 23%; it slows again from about level 71 (sparring E stays at its Padawan-end value, about 46): a peacetime Knight who spars only peers trickles at about 25% of drill XP above that level (Average with no fights: about 110 XP a year from age 50 against 460). *Accepted (Jared):* the wall is above Master level and the trickle continues; group sparring or missions lift it. The Order's best duelist (the Average from Knight on, no missions, 50 group spars a year against three Masters 10 below him, which count as one partner 10 above): 65.7 at 40, 85.0 at 60, 88.3 at 64; 1.16 levels a year at 25-40, 0.96 at 40-60, 0.84 at 60-64; he gains every year (tests/lifetime/swordmaster.test.ts). combat-xp.md §3 and progression-numbers §7 amended.
- **D-167 Force meter isolation (Jared, 2026-11-18; no rework of the numbers).** Everything in M5 that touches the Force meter goes through src/engine/forceMeter.ts: forceCapacity and forceLimit (the current limit), forceFraction and forceWithin, forceLoadPerUse (the load for an ability use), applyForceLoad (backlash), addFixedLoad (precognitive defense, shatterpoint), rollCollapse (now reads FORCE_METER.collapseChance instead of a literal 0.05), and trainingForceStep, restForce and sleepForce (recovery). Combat (combatForce.ts, duel.ts: Force actions, shatterpoint, precog; sequences use Force actions), the fatigue stepper and the health and training displays call it; no Force number is used outside config and this module. tests/engine/forceMeter.test.ts fails if Force-meter arithmetic or a Force config number appears in any other source file. Behaviour is unchanged (same RNG draws). The M6 redesign replaces this module; the tests that depend on Force-meter values are listed in the M5 close-out.
- **D-161 Wound severity by weapon; non-saber wounds by margin with banded overflow (Jared, 2026-11-18, option C).** *Problem (measured):* the health model almost never produced minor wounds: even saber duels gave 0% minor, 5% moderate, 71% severe (27% of all wounds severed limbs), 3% critical, 21% mortal; blaster hits wounded a Jedi only through Vitality overflow (14/25/61 minor/moderate/severe+). *Rules:* lightsabers keep health §4 (a clean saber hit maims or kills; grazes Vitality-only; overflow straight into the wound): the rolls §8 duel tables and health tests 4 and 5 are unchanged (Jared: the 50/35/15 target was wrong for sabers). Every other weapon and hazards: W = weapon base x NONSABER_WOUND_BY_MARGIN(margin) (margin 7 or less 0, 8: 0.1, 12: 0.5, 17: 1.0, 25: 2.5), scaled with D by armor; hits below margin 8 Vitality-only. *Overflow rule change:* non-saber overflow is banded too: past an empty Vitality the wound stays the margin's W, at least a minor wound (W 1). Hazards: severity hint 1/2/3 read as margin 10/15/21. Per-kind minion accuracy (a new MinionKind tunable on attack rolls): B1 -3 (Legends: notoriously poor shots), elite troopers +0.5. Grade and coordination could not move the elite row (six elites already reach the +3 numbers cap; grade sets the level, which the row fixes at -10), so the elites use accuracy as well. *Tuning steps (Djem So, 1,500 fights):* curve C0 (8: 0.05, 12.5: 0.5, 17.5: 1.0) rifle split 63/25/13, B1 98.5/25.1, elites 77.9/69.2; C1 (chosen) 55/31/15, B1 98.5/31.1, troopers 95.3/43.6, elites 74.0/74.5; C2 63/26/11. B1 accuracy -1/-2/-3/-4: wounded 20.3/12.3/8.9/5.5; elite accuracy +1/+2/+3: Djem So wins 51.8/29.3/14.9 (+0.5 chosen). *Final (level 50, even duels, 3,000 each; wounding hits minor/moderate/severe+):* saber vs saber 0/6/94, A wins 50 (unchanged); rifle on a Jedi 55/31/15 (Jedi wins 86); saber on the rifleman 0/8/93; blaster vs blaster 60/26/14 (51); pistol on a Jedi 68/20/11 (94). *Minion rows (Jedi at 50, 2,000 fights; win % / moderate-or-worse wounded %; Djem So | Soresu | Shii-Cho; D-158 target in brackets):* 10 B1 at -30 (99+ / <=10): 99.7/8.9 | 100/3.9 | 99.9/5.5. 10 militia at -30 (99+ / <=10): 99.0/21.4 | 99.8/10.2 | 99.4/15.7 (**+11.4, outside**). 6 line troopers at -20 (85-95 / 25-40): 95.2/43.7 | 98.3/21.0 | 97.6/32.5. 6 elites at -10 (55-70 / 55-75): 62.6/80.8 | 85.8/60.1 | 78.1/69.5. 4 Mandalorians all beskar (32 / 89): 44.6/86.6 | 84.7/61.0 | 70.8/72.4 (**win +12.6, outside**). Mixed at 0.6: 46.5/83.5 | 86.6/57.2 | 77.2/68.0. All durasteel: 97.0/41.1 | 99.6/23.8 | 99.1/28.7. 6 B2 at -20: 92.6/34.7 | 97.5/17.8 | 95.2/27.0 (wounded +11.4 over the D-158 measurement; no target). 4 droidekas at -20: 94.1/30.7 | 97.2/15.6 | 95.8/23.4. 3 beasts at -10: 100/11.8 | 100/11.2 | 100/8.3. 6 thugs at -30: 99.8/13.9 | 99.8/6.0 | 99.7/9.6. 3 Sith acolytes at -20: 90.5/31.4 | 91.1/30.0 | 93.3/24.1 (sabers, unchanged). health.md §4 and §10 test 3, npc-generation.md §6 amended.
- **D-168 A spar is a session of bouts (Jared, 2026-11-18).** *Problem (measured, step 9):* combat-xp §6 prices an even spar at 34 exchanges, but a sparring bout ends at the first point (controlled live blades) or the first side Spent (training sabers), about 5-10 exchanges, so a spar earned about 1.7 XP and Anakin could not reach 61/81/101 with any credible schedule (best: 41 at 5.0 y, 81 at 22.5 y vs the limit 16.2). A temporary 34-exchange session in the harness moved Anakin's 41 to 2.4 y at the same count. *Rule:* a spar is a session of bouts up to 34 exchanges in total (combat.config SPAR_SESSION). Each bout ends by its mode's rule; between bouts each side gets back 50% of its maximum Vitality (design default; fatigue and wounds carry); the session ends at the exchange count, or early when a side is still Spent after the recovery, on a real injury, or when the player stops. Numbering and the log run on across bouts; the outcome is the bout tally (won / lost / truce). Engine: src/engine/sparSession.ts (afterBout, runSession, sessionOutcome); the combat feature keeps the session on the active fight and in the fight summary. *Live play:* by default the session resolves in code like a montage with one narration call for the whole session (bouts won and lost, the highlights, any accident or injury); Play this bout plays the current bout out with the normal beats (any tactical action does too), a narrated boutEnded beat follows, and the next bout waits for the same choice; Stop sparring ends it. Fixture tests (tests/turn/combatPipeline.test.ts): a default session uses one narration call; a played bout then resolves the rest; Stop ends the session. rolls.md §3 and combat-xp.md §6 amended.
- **D-169 Militia and Mandalorian accuracy (Jared, 2026-11-18).** The two D-161 minion rows outside +/-10, fixed with the per-kind accuracy tunable. Militia (poorly trained, like the B1s) -1: sweep -1/-2/-3 gave Djem So wounded 14.1/9.7/5.5 (target <=10 + 10; -1 chosen). Mandalorians (elite marksmen) +0.5: sweep +0.5/+1/+1.5/+2 gave all-beskar Djem So wins 30.1/21.6/14.4/9.2 (target 32 +/- 10; +0.5 chosen). *Final rows (Jedi at 50, 2,000 fights; win % / moderate-or-worse wounded %; Djem So | Soresu | Shii-Cho):* 10 militia at -30: 99.4/14.1 | 100/6.0 | 99.9/10.2. 4 Mandalorians all beskar: 30.1/90.8 | 76.1/68.8 | 56.3/81.6. Mixed at 0.6: 35.4/89.6 | 78.6/64.8 | 66.3/73.9. All durasteel: 93.9/48.3 | 98.7/29.0 | 97.8/36.7. Every other D-161 row unchanged (saber vs saber 0/6/94, A wins 50; rifle on a Jedi 55/31/15). npc-generation.md §6 amended.
- **D-159 results (2026-11-18; retune done).** Tuned on the fixed seed and 5 more (median), fight counts, gaps and kinds only, K 11.683. *Schedules (tests/lifetime/profiles.ts):* Average unchanged (one mission a year from Padawan). Obi-Wan: 8 Initiate spars a year at +5, then 10 a year with his master at +15 (a stronger partner), 4 trooper missions a year, the Clone Wars at 35-38 (30 B1 groups, 10 B2 groups, 10 elite squads, 10 duels at 0 in the first year and 12 after), war years with bacta. Anakin: sparring sessions with his master at +15, 55 in the first year (so 21 stays within the 4.0x pace, 1.25 y) and 155 a year to 19, 15 trooper missions and 4 serious duels at -5 a year, the Clone Wars at 19-22 (57 B1 groups, 20 B2 groups, 8 elite squads, 8 duels at 0; the first year +6 B2 and +2 elite), then 240 group spars a year with three partners at 0, 10 trooper missions and 2 duels; war years with bacta. Luke: unchanged fights, both wars with bacta. *Fixed seed (all lifetime tests pass except Y_avg at age 80, -5.5%: D-166):* Average 4.19 / 16.03 / 33.44 / 55.11 / 82.05 y; Obi-Wan 3.24 / 12.22 / 23.61 / 39.76 / 59.98, Clone Wars shares 26.9 / 28.8 / 25.2%, peacetime max 8.3%; Anakin 1.27 / 4.37 / 8.57 / 15.74 / 22.80, war shares 25.9 / 27.3 / 26.1%; Luke 1.94 / 6.18 / 12.75 / 21.63 / 34.10. *5-seed spread (medians of 5; range):* Average 4.19 / 15.97 / 33.27 / 54.79 / 81.86 (41: 15.85-16.03); Obi-Wan 3.26 / 12.19 / 23.70 / 39.91 / 60.11 (41: 12.08-12.28), war shares 25.8 / 30.0 / 27.0 (one seed 33.2 / 33.8 in its first two war years) (*accepted by Jared as seed spread*); *After D-206 (2026-11-19):* Obi-Wan's last war year is 11 gap-0 duels; on 5 extra seeds one year reaches 32% (seed 3, age 36: 23 / 32 / 22), *accepted by Jared as seed spread*; Anakin 1.31 / 4.50 / 8.71 / 15.66 / 22.33 (41: 4.14-4.69; 81: 15.44-15.85), war shares 26.6 / 27.0 / 29.3; Luke 1.93 / 6.30 / 12.82 / 21.74 / 34.43. *D-143 J reports (fixed seed):* main-form E at 41 / 61: Average 61 / 82, Obi-Wan 25 / 111, Anakin 115 / 120, Luke 117 / 117; drill XP banked: Average 14.4%, Obi-Wan 10.4%, Anakin 6.8%, Luke 0%. Bacta days: Obi-Wan 455-530, Anakin 366-458, Luke 705-811. *Peacetime Average Knight (no war years; Jared):* main form 63.8 at 40 and 81.8 at 60, bank share 14.4%; with no missions at all (the Cin Drallig case) 64.0 / 74.5 (D-163); the Order's best duelist with weekly group sparring 69.9 at 40, 91.6 at 60 (1.35 levels a year at 25-40, 1.09 at 40-60). *Named duelists (Jared):* a Jedi at 50 against 2 / 3 named duelists, win % / wounded %: 2 at -10 Djem So 22/91, Soresu 25/90, Shii-Cho 34/85; 2 at -20 41/82, 44/82, 54/74; 3 at -10 4/99, 3.5/99, 8/98; 3 at -20 11/97, 11/97, 21/93 (before D-161; not an automatic loss at -20 against 2; 3 at -10 is close to one).
- **D-160 Built in M5 steps 5-9, logged now.** The drill ceiling applied at session resolution (the card, the event and the save agree); starting E and CI at creation (Order sparring as D-163, war years E +3 / CI +1, Knights +1 E per mission year, at most +15); Vitality stored as damage below the derived maximum (vitalityLost); a severed limb is a lasting injury at once (aid from stubProsthetic), and losing the dominant hand switches it; named teachers use their generated NPC levels (the teacher-level stub is removed); NPC levels reuse creation's closed form with per-skill variance and a rank fallback when the role's rank does not fit the age; minion Vitality multipliers; hits on minions are quiet beats (minionDown); the fight summary keeps the final duel for the last turn's cards; a fight intent starts a serious-stakes duel; heal intents: stabilizing is a moderate Medicine / Force Heal check, default care spans medical 7 days, bacta 6 h, Force healer 1 h; deferred hazardOccurred stored in health.pendingHazards; occupation 'combat' (no recovery); the creation form has an era picker and no default year; the Jedi Order seat by date (src/data/orderSeats.ts) is in the state block. *Design defaults for Jared to check (D-152, D-155, D-158):* canon birth and snapshot years marked estimate in canonNpcs.ts (rosters cover whole eras from the campaign date); the undated Order-seat boundaries (return to Coruscant about 3,600 BBY; Ossus from 36 ABY); minion kinds brawler (coordination 0), B2 (0, Vitality x1.2, heavy weapon and armor), droideka (0.5, Vitality x1.5, no shield model), beast (0, Vitality x1.8), Sith acolyte (0).
- **D-166 Y_avg re-measured; not adopted (stopped for Jared).** The M4 table dates from the scripted-combat stubs. Re-measured after the retune as Jared chose: the Average with its yearly mission, training XP only, median of the fixed seed and 5 more: 4: 582 (was 580), 8: 607 (608), 12: 557 (545, +2.2%), 16: 534 (538), 20: 551 (562), 24: 444 (449), 30: 395 (407), 40: 468 (474), 50: 453 (463), 60: 485 (484), 70: 438 (444), 80: 439 (453, -3.1%), 90: 266 (254, +4.7%), 98: 194 (206, -5.8%). *The 4.0x clamp check changes a test outcome:* with the new table Anakin's 41 moves from 4.37 to 3.92 y, under the 4.0x floor (Average 16.0 / 4), and his "never beyond 4.0x" test fails; the other profiles move by 0.06 y or less and the Average's Y_avg test passes. progression test 24 also pins yAvg(20) = 562. *Adopted (Jared, 2026-11-18):* the new table is in progression.config Y_AVG; progression test 24 now pins yAvg(20) = 551. Anakin retuned by fight counts only: 150 Padawan spars a year (was 155) and 280 group spars a year after the war (was 240). Fixed seed: 1.27 / 4.61 / 8.76 / 15.82 / 21.80 y, war shares 26.5 / 26.4 / 28.5%, every 4.0x floor met (41 >= 4.0). Medians of 5 more seeds: 1.43 / 4.30 / 8.95 / 16.08 / 22.70 (41: 4.20-4.61; 81: 15.82-16.32), war shares 25.5 / 29.1 / 28.6. Every lifetime test passes (36/36).
- **D-170 No numbers bonus on surprise strikes (Jared, 2026-11-18).** A defender caught by a surprise strike gets no numbers modifier for that strike (the others cannot cover them); it applies again from the next exchange (duel.ts resolveExchange). *Measured (Stover opening, level 160 vs Tiin at 73):* before, the surprise strike on Tiin missed about 28% of the time with Tiin defending at +3 numbers. The rolls §8 tables have no surprise and are unchanged (all four duel-table tests pass; every lifetime milestone identical). rolls.md §5 amended; test in tests/engine/duel.test.ts.
- **D-171 Aimed strikes (Jared, 2026-11-18).** A strike or a sequence strike step can aim at the head, the torso or a limb: the strike roll takes -2 (combat.config AIMED_STRIKE, design default) and the chosen part replaces the location roll (a part already lost falls back to the roll); the aim is spent on the next strike. Fighter.aim; the combatAction 'aim' with a body part; the sequence step strike { aim }; the intent field aim (and on sequence strike steps), mapped from typed input such as "go for his head" (intent prompt); Aim buttons in the duel panel. rolls.md §5 and health.md §5 amended; test in tests/engine/duel.test.ts.
- **D-172 Sith sparring stakes (Jared, 2026-11-18).** Routine sparring uses the rank default (a Sith master and apprentice: controlled live blades); Sith-academy stakes apply to spars at an academy and to trials; a master can call academy stakes for one session as a story event (combatStarted sparringMode, spar only; Sith path only, otherwise controlled with a note). SPARRING_MODES comment, pendingFight.sparringMode. *Why:* the step-9 Sidious-like profile at academy stakes was wounded 300+ days a year from 17 to 40, so only Juyo grew. Tests in tests/features/combat.test.ts.
- **D-173 Shii-Cho gets the fundamentals transfer (Jared, 2026-11-18; bug fix).** *Root cause:* Shii-Cho basics needs a teacher present until Shii-Cho 15 (supervisedOrLevel), and the unlock check ran before the transfer, so a Knight or Lord training with peers was refused and Shii-Cho never started (the Sidious-like profile's Shii-Cho stayed 0 while every other planned form started). Now the transfer is worked out first and level gates read the level after it. Regression test in tests/features/formTransfer.test.ts (fails without the fix); every lifetime profile unchanged (Average and Obi-Wan learn Shii-Cho as Initiates; Anakin and Luke do not plan it).
- **D-174 Unused planned forms warning (Jared, 2026-11-18).** The routine preview warns when a form in the form plan in force gets no hours in the previewed week (the priorities leave it out, so it never starts or grows; the Anakin Ataru and Luke second-form case). routines selectPlanForms; training-routines.md §5 amended; test in tests/features/routines.test.ts.
- **D-175 Sidious-like path and Stover 4v1 (reports, 2026-11-18; nothing adopted beyond D-170..D-174).** *Sidious-like profile (Sith Warrior, exceptional combat / Force / mind, trained from 17 under a master 20 above, a few lethal trials a decade, own apprentice and group sparring from 40):* at academy stakes wounded 300+ days a year to 40, only Juyo grows (94.2 at 63, other forms 0). With controlled master sparring and fair priorities, at 40 / 60 / 63: Juyo 94.6 / 113.1 / 115.0; Soresu, Ataru and Djem So 0 / about 82 / about 82 (from the fundamentals transfer, then frozen at the ceiling); Niman 41, Makashi 31, Shien 15, Shii-Cho 0 (D-173); Lightning 20 / 51 / 54, Force Output 20 / 43 / 45, Force Control 55 / 63 / 64, Force Sense 46 / 49 / 50; Juyo E 114 / 116 / 116, ceiling 139-141, bank share 0 / 16 / 18%, no 4.0x discards; CI 0 in the harness (no Force use logged), Lightning 54 fights at 39 (CI 0), 49 (CI 20), 54 (CI 60). *Secondary forms (all careers):* second forms reach about 40-53 and gain about 3 levels in 20 years (architecture §15 M6). *Stover 4v1 (Masters estimated: Windu 92 Vaapad, Kolar / Tiin / Fisto 73; opening feint under surprise, then a sequence striking Tiin then Kolar; cap 4):* before D-170/D-171 both were out in the opening 0-2% at every level; with D-170 and head-aimed surprise strikes (D-171): profile 1%, level 115 3%, 130 5%, 145 7-8%, 160 10-12%. At 160 per strike: missed 26-30% (d20 spread against the margin-4 strike minimum after -2 aim), mortal 29-39%, solid (severe) 13-17%, graze 12-14%; the feint is seen through 19%. 2v1 median: profile 92-98 exchanges, 115 77-81, 130 65-68, 145 52-57, 160 38-41 (29-61 beats); Fisto falls first 28-33% (115), 66-68% (130), 87% (145), 97% (160). Windu 1v1 (after a clean opening; few cases): 17-34 exchanges, Sidious wins 42-69% at 115, 82-89% at 130, 97% at 145, 97-100% at 160. Free form switch: no consistent effect. *Weapon hand lost (item 6, measured, not adopted):* rolls §8 win rates stay within +/-3, but the medians shorten 4-7 exchanges (gap 0: 37 -> 31, gap 5: 35 -> 28, gap 10: 36 -> 29, gap 20: 31 -> 26; gap 5 and 10 below 30); in even duels the loser had lost the weapon hand or arm 17.0% (current) / 22.9% (rule); lifetime with the rule: Anakin 81 at 16.41 y (limit 16.20), Obi-Wan war year 36 at 38.3% (band 20-30), Average and Luke unchanged. Outside the gates: recorded for M6 (architecture §15). With the rule, the Stover 2v1 shortens by about 10-15 exchanges; the opening is unchanged.
- **D-176 E and CI above 100 break save loading (found in step 10; open question for Jared).** The gain formulas scale by (1 - E/120) and (1 - CI/120), so E and CI approach 120 (measured: 200 serious fights give E 120; the lifetime runs reach E 108-120), but the progression schema (and progression-numbers §7: "E (0-100)") caps both at 100, and saves are validated on load (src/persist/envelope.ts): a save with E or CI above 100 is rejected. Options: cap E and CI at 100 in gainE / gainCI (the spec range; the drill ceiling then stops at 125 and the CI line at 125), or widen the range to 0-120 in the schema and the spec (the formulas' limit; behaviour unchanged). *Decided (Jared):* widen E and CI to 0-120 in the save schema and the spec (progression-numbers §7, §8; architecture §2); behaviour unchanged. *And loading is made robust:* when a save loads, a number outside its schema range is clamped to the range with a logged warning (LoadResult.warnings, console.warn on resume and import) and the load goes on; only a structurally broken save (wrong types, missing or unknown fields, failed migrations, newer versions) is refused (src/persist/envelope.ts; architecture §13). tests/persist/rangeClamp.test.ts: E and CI near 120 save and reload unchanged; a save with one out-of-range number loads clamped with a warning; a structurally broken save is refused. The persist test that expected an out-of-range clock second to be refused now expects it clamped (and a wrong type refused).
- **D-177 Typed combat input plays a sparring session out (Jared, iPad test, 2026-11-18; bug fix).** *Diagnosis:* the Initiate's "parry the strike using Shii-Cho fundamentals" was in a sparring session (a combatStarted with stakes spar, resolved at the next step b). In that same turn the combat action came from the intent, which had no sequence, tactic or aim (parry is not a tactic), so it was continue; continue on a session not in play-out mode resolves every remaining bout in code (D-168). The fight began and ended within the turn, so the duel panel (shown only while a fight is active) and its Play this bout button were never on screen: not missing on iPad, never offered. *Rules:* in a session not in play-out mode, any player input that is not a request to spar as an activity (an intent whose activity is a sparring activity) plays the bout out with the beats (resolve.ts); routine and Order sparring blocks are training sessions and unaffected; after a session resolves like a montage, the fight summary keeps a rematch (the named partners and the mode, unless someone was really hurt) and the duel panel offers Play the next bout the same day, which starts the next bout in play-out mode. Fixture tests (tests/turn/combatPipeline.test.ts): a typed specific action opens the duel panel and makes no auto-resolve; sparring asked as an activity auto-resolves and Play the next bout steps back in. rolls.md §3 amended.
- **D-178 Dev-only test start (Jared, 2026-11-18).** Starting rank and age are already in normal creation (character-creation.md §1, built in M3 with E and CI from M5): the rank list is filtered by the age the birth date gives. For testing, a dev-only start behind ?dev=1 (src/turn/testStart.ts, a section of the new-campaign form): rank (Initiate, Padawan, Knight, Master) and age; an Average Human Jedi Guardian (training from 4, no talents, the default plan) with closed-form levels from computed creation (the same growth NPC generation uses), creation's starting E, CI for every known combat Force ability by rank (design default: Initiate 0, Padawan 10, Knight and Master 20, the sparring cap), a story exception when the rank's minimums are not met; marked testCharacter in the save (character v5, older saves migrate to false) and "[TEST]" in the campaign name. tests/turn/testStart.test.ts.
- **D-179 Natural combat words map to mechanics (Jared, iPad test, 2026-11-18).** When the intent call names no combat move (no sequence, tactic, aim or Force ability), the player's typed text (or a choice's label) is read against a configured word list (src/data/combatWords.ts; whole words and phrases, the earliest stance word wins): parry, block, defend, hold, guard (and deflect, give ground, fall back) -> defensive stance; press, attack, push forward, overwhelm -> aggressive stance; dodge, evade, sidestep, roll (duck) -> defensive stance plus a take-position attempt; circle, reposition, high ground, get behind (flank) -> take position; a form name, number (Form I-VII) or a well-known technique (Sun Djem, Hawk-bat swoop) -> switch to that form at the form-change cost, or stay in it (an unknown form is ignored with a note). The combat feature applies them together as a posture action, then runs to the next beat. Continue words (continue, keep fighting, fight on) keep Continue. Anything else asks back: a clarify beat (quiet, no exchange, no AI call) with a short line, and the beat buttons. In a sparring session these count as playing the bout out (D-177). The intent prompt (intent.md) teaches the same mapping. Fixture tests for each group in tests/turn/combatPipeline.test.ts. rolls.md §3 amended.
- **D-181 The duel panel and the choice list agree; a bout's end keeps the session open (Jared, iPad test, 2026-11-18; bug fix).** *Diagnosis:* in a Knight test start's sparring session the duel panel closed after one stance tap while combat choices stayed. The engine did not end the session: reproduced on 20 seeds, a stance tap resolves 2-5 exchanges to the next beat, and a bout that ends there (a point, about 1 in 5 taps) starts the next bout with the fight still active. The panel shows exactly when combat.active is set, so the fight left the slice during the narration: the only path there is the narrator's combatEnded, legal in any fight. The RESOLVED block said only "Narrate the bout that just ended", and the combat prompt listed "a point in a spar" among fight end reasons, so a narrator reading the point as the end of the spar sends combatEnded (truce): the session concluded, the panel closed, and the same reply's tactical choices (written for a fight in progress) filled the choice list, which showed the last narration's choices without looking at the combat slice. Not confirmed from the device (no save was shared); the debug panel's applied events for that turn would show combatEnded with source ai. *Fix:* (1) combatEnded during a sparring session is refused except interrupted (the story stops it), which ends the session through the same path as Stop sparring, with the bouts so far; (2) the RESOLVED block says on a bout's end that the session goes on and the next bout begins, not to send combatEnded; mid-bout it says the bout is in progress; when a fight is over it says the choices are story choices again; combat-narration.md says the same; (3) one source of truth, the combat slice (src/turn/fightChoices.ts fightView): while a fight is active the choice list is empty and the narrator's tactical suggestions show inside the duel panel ("Or ..."); when it is not, the list shows the choices; (4) a turn that ends a fight drops tactical moves (fight intents at no known NPC) from the narrator's choices and fills the list to two with code choices (Catch your breath, Take stock of what just happened), logged in the turn report; (5) the panel shows the last bout's result and the next bout while a session goes on, and after a fight or session ends a result summary (outcome and reason, or the bout tally and any injury, the opponent's choice, each fighter's status) with a Close button, above the fate, review and Play the next bout choices. Tests: tests/turn/fightView.test.ts. rolls.md §3 amended.
- **D-182 A stance is a setting, not an action (Jared, iPad test, 2026-11-18).** The stance buttons send stanceSet (player only, outside the turn pipeline, through the same no-turn path as the routine editor): the stance changes and is saved; no exchange, no dice, no AI call, no time, no turn record. The stance stays selected until changed, including across a sparring session's bouts, and does not start or resolve a session. stance is removed from the combatAction actions (a beat button can no longer send it). Exchanges resolve only from an action: Continue, Fight on, a tactic, a maneuver, a Force action, an aimed strike, a form change, Narrate, Resolve to end, or typed input (typed stance words still resolve to the next beat, D-179). A clarify (unreadable typed input) no longer starts a session's play-out either. Target selection already made no exchange and is unchanged. Tests: tests/features/combat.test.ts, tests/turn/fightView.test.ts. rolls.md §3 amended.
- **D-183 Selected buttons keep readable text (Jared, iPad test, 2026-11-18; bug fix).** *Cause:* beat buttons use the primary style (solid accent fill, dark ink) and the selected rule set the text to the accent color: accent on accent, unreadable. *Fix:* the duel panel's settings and pending choices (stance, next-strike maneuver, aim) are toggles: unselected outlined on the box color with the normal text color; selected keeps the normal text color on a tinted ground (accent-soft) with an inset accent outline, a check before the label, bold, and aria-pressed. Never a solid fill. Contrast by the tokens: selected about 11:1, unselected about 13:1. The rest of the app was checked: tabs (accent text on accent-soft) and the side-panel icon toggle (accent text on the box color) were already readable; no other selected style exists. The app has one theme (the dark datapad, D-100; color-scheme dark), so there is no light mode to break; the toggle uses only tokens, so a future light theme inherits it. Aim buttons read "right arm" rather than "rightArm". Checked at iPad width (820 x 1180, headless Chrome on the real renderDuelPanel output: the panel mid-spar with a stance and a maneuver selected, and the end-of-session summary): every label readable, the rows wrap without overflow. The narrator's suggestions in the panel keep their own capitalization.
- **D-180 Dev sandbox (Jared, 2026-11-18; dev mode ?dev=1, test campaigns only).** Built after D-181..D-183. *Scenario:* the dev test start has an optional free-text scenario; it is stored on the campaign record and sent to the opening narration as a DEV SCENARIO block that overrides the generated opening ("start exactly in this situation"); the narrator still proposes normal bounded events (combatStarted with minionGroups, terrainTags and stakes; npcIntroduced; locationIntroduced), validated as usual. /scenario <text> mid-campaign does the same for the next narrated turn (the pending scenario is read from the transcript: the latest /scenario since the last narrated turn, so it survives a reload and needs no state). *Commands* (src/turn/devCommands.ts), handled in code before the intent call, no AI call, each through applyEvents: /fight <count> <kind or level> [at <gap>] [terrain <tags>] [stakes ...] [mode ...] (a fightBegun at once, so the duel panel opens; kinds: duelist or a level, a minion kind with aliases such as b1, b2, elite, mando, acolyte, or canon:<name> matched against the canon rosters, refused before the figure's birth or after the death; "at" is the gap to the player's main form;
