(2) **In-world dev places** (turn/devCommands.ts DEV_PLACE_NAMES, DEV_DEFAULT_PLACE): a /fight place word sets an in-world place on a planet that exists in every era ("terrain ruins" -> "ancient ruins on Dantooine", hangar -> "an abandoned hangar on Nar Shaddaa", ice -> "an ice field on Hoth", ...); a /fight with no place word keeps the scene's place (the scenario's) and only a scene with no place, or a leftover "(dev sandbox)" place, gets "an abandoned hangar on Nar Shaddaa"; dev opponents are described in-world ("an armed duelist who means to fight", "a sparring partner", a canon figure "here to fight"), never "a dev sandbox opponent". Pass 2's narrator read "Ruins (dev sandbox)" as a combat simulation. (3) **/fight weapon <kind>** for a minion group (scattergun, slugpistol, slugrifle, pistol, rifle, heavy, melee, saber): every minion of the group carries it, overriding the kind's weapon and the Mandalorian slugthrower roll (PendingFight minionGroups[].weapon); refused for named duelists. (4) **/truce [terms]**: the opponent offers a truce (truceOffered, now also a dev source), answered by the Accept truce beat. (5) **/fall**: the fight's terrain accidents are rolled now (devTerrainRolled, rollBoutHazards as at the start of a block of exchanges, D-185), refused when the fight has no hazardous terrain. *Tests:* tests/turn/devTools.test.ts (all eight archetypes valid on their paths; a Sith knows Lightning and Choke and is a Sith Lord; a smuggler has no Force and fights with a blaster pistol; a Twi'lek; four Mandalorians with scatter guns in the ruins; bad weapon words and a weapon on a duelist refused; /truce then Accept truce ends as a truce; /fall on ice; /fall with no hazard refused); tests/turn/devCommands.test.ts (the place-word case now in-world, the no-place default, a later /fight keeping the place).
- **D-216 Combat pass 2's minor issues (14-27) (Jared; built overnight).** Each issue, fixed or left with the reason. **14 fixed:** narration check rule 12 (stepNotReached): "two / three / both ... cuts, strikes, blows" when fewer strike steps were made and the rest were NOT REACHED (the pass-2 sentence is a test). **15 fixed** by D-211 (rule 10). **16 partly fixed:** the block has an Armor line ("Armor (the only armor in this fight): Kael Voss: none; Mandalorian warrior 1: heavy (beskar: turns a lightsaber)"); rule 11 (gearNotInBlock) catches "your armor / beskar / plating / vest" on a player with none; the melee weapon text says it cuts, bruises or pierces and does not burn (the amphistaff "burns"). Left: "spinning blades" (no narrow pattern without false alarms; the Weapons line and the ABSOLUTE RULE cover it), the utility-belt cord (items are M8), "rely on the Force to defend" (flavour). **17 fixed:** OBJECT_WORDS (rock, stone, log, branch, debris, ice, crate, machinery...) set a throw step's objectTag from the player's words, also from the clause just before "and hurl it"; the intent's own objectTag wins. **18 fixed:** a surrender gets the opponent's choice from the same fate table as a downed player; a killing strike becomes capture (an offered surrender is taken; overnight design default; alternative: allow the killing strike in lethal fights). **19 partly fixed:** a Force hit that does no damage (Lightning scales with Force Output; pass 2's Knight had Force Output 0) reads "X is struck but unharmed (no damage)" in the step line and the block instead of "hits: stamina only". Left as per the rules: a hit whose damage bypasses Vitality into a wound (health §4 bypass) leaves Vitality full, and a killing strike adds a mortal wound with no Vitality loss (health §3: Vitality never causes death). **20 partly fixed:** the health card says the aid in words ("quality cybernetic"). Left: a severed limb's aid is the faction default at once (data/stubs.ts, D-164: Jedi, Sith and Republic get a quality cybernetic); making it "none until fitted" would end every later duel at once (cannotStand) and move the lifetime gates: an M8 equipment question. Adaptation pace is in the balance list. **21 partly fixed:** a dev command that moves time or starts a fight clears the previous story choices (pass 2: capture choices offered 35 days later). Left: the montage's "brief encounter with Duelist 1" (world encounters draw on known NPCs; whether a hostile NPC may appear is an M6 relationship rule), the medic week staying at "Forest (dev sandbox)" (D-215's in-world places) and the /scenario jump (dev only). **22 mostly fixed:** in a ysalamiri bubble every Force button reads "(no Force here)" and is disabled; against a foe outside the Force, Push, Choke and Lightning read "(immune)" (Throw and Saber throw stay); each opponent's weapon shows with its status ("minion · scatter gun"); the out-of-fight mind trick card says "immune, no roll" for an immune target and shows the resisting Willpower dice and a Force user's added resistance. Left: the first, generic "Use the Force" check (the intent named no ability; the second, explicit attempt rolled Mind Trick correctly) and the sparring button names (D-177's). **23 fixed:** ability names and body parts in words in Force notes, wound notes, healed-wound notices, terrain notes and /wound; "+3 more: 2 still fighting, 1 down". Left: "lost: sparring session: stopped" (accurate) and the montage's rest-day count (the day list includes injury-rest days the count calls training days; a montage-report question, not combat). **24 fixed:** QUIET_LINES_RANGED when either side fights with a ranged weapon (no "blade on blade", no blows from a shooter), and no "it" for a person. **25 fixed:** typed speech in a fight (quotes or a speech verb) gets the quiet line "Words alone do not stop a fight. To end it: Disengage or Surrender, or accept a truce if the other side offers one. To fight on: a stance, a feint, a strike, a move." (the intent call itself is still needed to read the input). **26 fixed:** core-gm.md bans Earth idioms in anyone's mouth ("parlor tricks", "Hail Mary", "by the book", miles, guns); dev Sith duelists are of the Sith Empire only in its years (SITH_EMPIRE_YEARS: the Great Hyperspace War, Revan's empire, 3,681-3,628 BBY, the Legacy era), else of the Sith. **27 partly fixed:** the Force meter's over-limit text is one short line on its row ("over limit: 5%/use", the full text as a tooltip), so it no longer pushes the stance row out of a wide panel; at 900 px and narrower the turn count is hidden and the model picker is narrower, giving the date and place their room. Left: 30 px buttons (44 px would push the pinned rows past the 324 px panel body at 820 x 1180; a layout decision for Jared with the iPad in hand). **Also found and fixed:** the stored fight context's schema had no cover or forceNegated (D-192), so a reload in the middle of a fight dropped a ysalamiri bubble and cover; both are kept now (default false). *Tests:* tests/turn/minorFixes.test.ts (14, 16, 17, 18, 19, 23, 24, 25, 26 with the pass-2 cases).
- **D-208 amended: the physical hard limit is 4.0 (overnight, after the lifetime check).** npm run test:all with 2.0 failed one gate: Anakin's Clone Wars combat share at age 19 fell to 24.5% (band 25-35%), because his war days chain fights and carry the physical meter, so some fights ended in collapse. With the limit off the gate passed (25 / 26 / 26%); at 3.0 the share passed but his 81 milestone came out at 16.21 y against the 16.2 limit; at 4.0 every Anakin and Obi-Wan gate passes with D-207's numbers exactly (Anakin 41 4.18, 61 9.26, 81 15.82, 101 22.35; Obi-Wan 81 39.41, 101 59.65): the collapse never fires in a lifetime. The pass-2 case (26.4 / 5.1 = 5.2x) still collapses, and the safety cap bounds a single fight. No gate was amended. Alternatives for Jared: a lower limit with a lifetime retune, or a chance per exchange past 1.0 like the Force meter.
- **Jared's calls on the overnight decisions (2026-11-19, combat pass 2 follow-up).** (1) **D-208's 4.0 hard limit: accepted.** Jared asked whether fatigue penalties keep growing past 1 x capacity, so that a fighter at 2-4x is heavily debuffed. *They do not* (measured from engine/duel.ts fighterEffectiveLevel and engine/fatigue.ts efficiency): the level factor is 1 - 0.25 x (1 - e(f)) with e(f) = 1.0 to f 0.4, falling linearly to 0.3 at f 1.0, then a flat 0.1. So the factor is 1.0 to 0.4x, 0.913 at 0.7x, 0.825 at 1.0x, then a step to 0.775 just past 1.0x and flat 0.775 all the way to the 4.0x collapse (x 0.9 more when Spent, D-208's other path). A fighter at 3.9x fights at 77.5% of their level, the same as at 1.1x. *Proposal (not built; waiting for Jared, CLAUDE.md rule 7):* see D-234. (2) **The narrator's hazard as an interruption cause: kept**, and it now does damage when it ends the fight: D-217. (3) **B1s and droidekas from 32 BBY (the Naboo invasion): accepted**; D-213 amended below. (4) **A test Sith gets Force Lightning (D-215): accepted.** (5) **A surrender's killing strike:** capture by default, with an execution chance by archetype from config: D-218. (6) **Anakin's 4.0x floor on 8 of 20 seeds:** explained in D-159 (spread note) and docs-v2/testing/lifetime-spread.md; report only. (7) **The war-share gates stay on the fixed seed**; the 20-seed natural spread is recorded in D-159 below.
- **D-213 amended: B1 battle droids and droidekas from 32 BBY (Jared, 2026-11-19).** The overnight range (32-19 BBY, the Naboo crisis included) is accepted: the Trade Federation's droid army invaded Naboo in 32 BBY. B2s stay 22-19 BBY. No code change (data/npcGen.config.ts already has eras [[-32, -18]] for both).
- **D-159 amended: the natural spread of the war-share gates (Jared, 2026-11-19).** The war-share gates stay on the fixed seed (and the fixed seed plus 5 more for retunes). The 20-seed run (docs-v2/testing/lifetime-spread.md, pass-2 code with the 4.0 hard limit) gives the natural spread to expect, 10th to 90th percentile of the yearly combat share of the main-form XP: **Anakin** (band 25-35%) age 19: 22.6-27.4% (median 25.4%), age 20: 26.0-29.4% (27.4%), age 21: 25.5-30.0% (27.6%); **Obi-Wan** (band 20-30%) age 35: 21.1-26.5% (24.0%), age 36: 23.8-32.3% (27.4%), age 37: 22.5-27.2% (25.6%). A seed outside the band but inside these ranges is expected spread, not a regression. *Anakin's 4.0x floor (8 of 20 seeds fail at tier 41):* the D-48 clamp never binds for him (no XP discarded; his main-form XP of 1,400-2,220 a year stays under the share-0.5 cap of 4.0 x Y_avg, 2,200-2,380, and well under his own at focus share 0.7, x 1.4). The gate's floor is the Average's target / 4: tier 41 is 7,761 XP and the Average's highest form takes 16.03 y (484 XP a year), while Y_avg averages about 556 at ages 4-20, so the clamp allows about 4.6x the pace the gate assumes (6.4x at his share). A seed fails when its years 2-4 average above about 1,940 XP; the seed-to-seed spread is in the sparring XP with his master. Report only: no tuning, no gate change.
- **D-217 A narrator's hazard that ends a fight is rolled for everyone in it (Jared, 2026-11-19; replaces D-212's no-damage rule for that case).** When combatEnded (interrupted, environmentCollapse) has a hazardOccurred in the same reply as its cause, the engine rolls that hazard for every fighter still in the fight (the player, the named opponents, the minions) by the normal hazard rules: hazardDamage at the reply's severity band, a rolled location (the fall weights for a fall), the wound by its W (engine/terrainHazard.ts hazardOnAll). The narrator names the hazard and its band; it never sets damage. The wounds reach health and the NPCs (syncEmits) before the fight concludes as interrupted, with "hazard: X is caught by the explosion (...)" notes. A hazard sent during a fight with no interruption still does no damage (D-212). Tests: tests/engine/balanceRows.test.ts.
- **D-218 A surrender is a capture by default; some captors may execute (Jared, 2026-11-19; replaces D-216 18's overnight default).** A surrender no longer rolls the downed player's fate table. It is a capture, except that an archetype with an execution chance in data/combat.config.ts CAPTOR_RULES kills a surrendered foe at that chance (by stakes): Sith Warrior and Sith Inquisitor 0.3 in a lethal fight, bounty hunter 0.2 in a lethal fight (standing in for dead-or-alive contracts until contracts exist), 0 in a serious fight for all; every other archetype 0 (design defaults). The killing strike is the usual mortal wound with the dying timer (D-154). Tests: tests/turn/minorFixes.test.ts (a Jedi always captures; a Sith in a lethal fight gives capture and killing strikes).
- **D-219 A strike step that only holds passes the chain on (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 1).** engine/sequence.ts: a strike step whose build-up exchange holds (the tie band; the track where it was, no hit on the player, no natural 1) is DONE with "holds; no ground gained, no hit; the chain goes on", and the next step is rolled without building pressure (pressure resets to 0). Lost ground, a miss or a natural 1 still stop the chain (FAILED). Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-220 A first use gains at most 4 track steps (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 2).** combat.config FIRST_USE_MAX_GAIN = 4: a pattern's first use (D-146) still jumps toward the strike, but by at most 4 steps in one move, in engine/combatForce.ts (Push, Choke) and engine/sequence.ts (tactics). A first-use Push from -3 now lands at +1 (was +3); from 0 or above it still reaches the strike. Tests: tests/engine/duel.test.ts (the Push card -3 -> +1), balanceRows (a feint from -3 and from 0).
- **D-221 A state of mind counts once on a build-up roll (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 4).** engine/duel.ts netMods: when a state of mind's attack and defense values have the same sign (fear, despair, grief -1 / -1; pride 0 / -1), a build-up roll counts it once, at the larger value; values of opposite sign (anger, hatred +1 / -2) still net, like the stance (D-205). Despair is now -1 on every roll (was -2 on build-ups). Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-222 Precognition's Force load is on the card (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 6).** No number change. Each use of precognitive defense adds a card-only note to the exchange ("precognition (the strike still lands): 0.4 Force load", or without the parenthesis when it avoided the strike; engine/duel.ts CARD_ONLY_NOTE, precogCardNote), shown on the roll card and dropped from the narrator's block (turn/combatFacts.ts). The duel panel's Force meter row reads "precognition 0.4/use" while under the limit, with a tooltip saying why the meter rises with no Force action. Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-223 Droid kinds dodge no better than they shoot (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 7).** features/combat/build.ts buildMinionFighter: a droid minion kind's Agility is min(Agility, weapon level), so its dodge of a thrown object or a Force action is no better than its shooting (a B1 dodged at +3.6 and shot at +1.4 - 3). The -3 accuracy (D-161) stays on its attack only. The lifetime harness builds its minions without Agility rolls and is unchanged. Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-224 A Yuuzhan Vong warrior archetype (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 8; done now, not in M6).** data/archetypes.ts yuuzhanVongWarrior: career path, main skill melee, the soldier's paces with melee in place of the blaster and no Force Control (the Vong are outside the Force, D-192); generated NPCs only (not in PATH_ARCHETYPES). Loadout (data/weapons.ts): melee (the amphistaff), heavy armor, saber-resistant: Characters_15 :34 has both amphistaffs and vonduun crab armor resisting lightsabers (Legends). data/npcGen.config.ts SPECIES_ARCHETYPE: a Yuuzhan Vong in the role soldier, officer, pilot, bounty hunter, criminal or other is a warrior, whatever the faction; scholars, healers and the other civilian roles (shapers, priests, workers) stay civilian until the M6 catalog. engine/npcGen.ts rollNpcInputs takes the species. Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-225 Captors stabilize a dying prisoner (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 13).** features/combat conclude: when the player's fate is "captured" and the player is dying, the captors stabilize the mortal wound (woundsStabilized, "by the captors"), unless the captor's archetype captures to execute (CAPTOR_RULES capturesToExecute): the Yuuzhan Vong warrior, who takes captives for sacrifice (Legends; design default, the only one set). "Left" keeps the dying timer. Tests: balanceRows (soldiers stabilize; Vong do not).
- **D-226 The usage meter counts 2xx replies and 429s only (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 14).** turn/pipeline.ts countsOnMeter: a failed call is recorded on the meter only when the API answered 2xx (an empty, cut-off, blocked or malformed reply) or 429. A 5xx or other HTTP error, a timeout, a network error or a missing key is not. Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-227 Surrender XP factor 0.5 (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 15).** progression.config COMBAT_XP.surrendered = 0.5 replaces the lost factor (1.2) for a surrender; fightResolved carries surrendered: true; engine/combatXp.ts uses it. Below fled (0.8), so surrendering is never the XP-best way out of a fight. Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-228 At most one terrain accident per roll for the whole fight (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 19, pass 1 number 8).** engine/terrainHazard.ts rollBoutHazards: every fighter still in rolls each hazardous tag as before (the bout start, and every 10-exchange block of a real fight, D-185), but the fight takes at most one accident per roll; when several fighters roll under a chance one of them is picked at random (the player is not favoured by rolling first). Tests: tests/engine/terrainHazard.test.ts.
- **D-229 A missed sequence strike against a minion loses at most one step (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 20, pass 1 number 4).** combat.config FORCED_MISS_MAX_LOSS = 1: D-191's forced strike against a minion that misses by more than a tie moves the track at most one step toward the minion (was the build-up step for the margin, up to 3). Tests: balanceRows.
- **D-230 A lost leg's adaptation measured (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 16; report only, nothing changed).** progression-numbers §18 adapts a lasting injury by +1% per effective hour of training the skills it affects, at most 3% a day. A lost leg's penalties (health.config LASTING_PENALTIES.lostLeg) are on Agility (0.35) and Endurance (0.15), and engine/lasting.ts lastingAffects counts a skill only when one of its own groups is named, so **only Agility and Endurance practice hours count**; form drills do not, though forms are footwork. The hours are practice hours (engine/computeXp.ts: effective hours, and a secondary stat's share / 0.75 of them), and fights add none (fightResolved emits no adaptationTrained). *Measured* (a Knight at 30 on the default routine, a lost left leg, 30 days): 147.5 training hours, of which Endurance 22.2 and Agility 14.2 practice hours reach the leg (Soresu 51.3 and Ataru 25.1 do not); adaptation 6.0% at day 6, 12.8% at 12, 20.5% at 18, 27.1% at 24, 36.4% at 30, about 1.2% a day, under the 3% cap; the aid does not change the rate (a quality cybernetic caps at 100%, none at 70%). Pass 2's 17% after 18 days fits. At this rate a quality cybernetic leg reaches 100% in about 82 days, against the hand's 30-40 day reference (a lost hand affects every form, so it meets the 3%-a-day cap). *Options for Jared (not built):* count form hours toward a leg at a fraction (forms are footwork), or a leg reference of its own in §18.
- **D-231 A 5% collapse chance per exchange past 1.0, measured against the 4.0 hard limit (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 17; report only, not adopted).** *Measured* (a temporary patch, reverted; each lifetime profile on its fixed seed through the tests/lifetime gates). Today the lifetimes do pass 1.0x capacity: 7,530 of Anakin's 473,552 exchanges (1.6%), 869 of Obi-Wan's 15,131 (5.7%), 723 of Luke's 6,389 (11%), none of the Average's; the 4.0 limit never fires. With a 5% chance per exchange past 1.0 (the 4.0 limit kept as a backstop) fighters collapse 160 times in Anakin's lifetime, 14 in Obi-Wan's, 10 in Luke's, 0 in the Average's, and three gates fail on the fixed seeds: Anakin within 4.0x (41 at 3.87 y; his milestones all come sooner, 61 at 8.16 y against 9.26, 101 at 19.64 against 22.35), Anakin's Clone Wars share (21.1% at 19, band 25-35%) and Obi-Wan's (17.3% at 35, band 20-30%). Luke and the Average pass. Adopting it would need a lifetime retune; the 4.0 hard limit stays (Jared).
- **D-232 The weak-minded modifier moves to M6 (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 2 balance row 9, pass 1 number 6).** A line trooper's Willpower (+3.7) beating a Knight's Mind Trick (+2.3) is settled in the M6 catalog with a weak-minded modifier by minion grade (Legends: mind tricks work on the weak-minded). Nothing changes now.
- **D-233 Combat pass 2 balance rows with no change (Jared, 2026-11-19).** Row 3 (the unarmed Knight for 322 exchanges: fixed by D-208), 5 (duel lengths of 81, 53 and 5 exchanges: dice, inside D-206's table), 10 (a minor wound healing in a day: health §3 and §7), 11 (10.67 combat XP for the 322-exchange loss: cannot recur after D-208), 12 (a Jedi's Lightning and Choke with no consequence: alignment is M6), 18 (the Mandalorian row moved by the 2.0 hard limit: back at 4.0). As intended or already resolved.
- **D-234 Proposal: fatigue penalties that keep growing past 1x capacity (for Jared; not built, CLAUDE.md rule 7).** *The current curve* (first entry above): the level factor 1 - 0.25 x (1 - e(f)) is 1.0 to 0.4x, 0.825 at 1.0x, steps to 0.775 just past 1.0x and stays flat at 0.775 to the 4.0x collapse. *Proposal:* keep it to 1.0x and continue it smoothly past 1.0x, one formula and no step: factor = max(0.40, 0.825 - (0.425 / 3) x (f - 1)), so 0.825 at 1.0x, 0.68 at 2x, 0.54 at 3x and 0.40 at 4x, where the fighter collapses (D-208). A fighter at 3x would fight at about 54% of their level (x 0.9 more when Spent) instead of 77.5%. *Measured* (a temporary patch, reverted; fixed seeds): the Average, Obi-Wan and Luke pass every gate (Obi-Wan 81 at 39.44 y, 101 at 59.69; Luke 101 at 34.09); Anakin fails three: milestones +/-20% and <= 1.2 x target at 81 (16.43 y against the 16.2 limit; 101 at 23.30) and his Clone Wars share (24.8% at 19). The Knight-50 sweep hardly moves (4 Mandalorians balanced win 97.0 -> 96.3%, 10 B1 heavyForce 68.7 -> 70.0%, every other row equal). Adopting it needs an Anakin retune: his near-daily sparring and his war days are the lifetime exchanges past 1.0x. Waiting for Jared; nothing built.
- **D-234 adopted: the fatigue curve past 1x capacity, and Anakin's retune (Jared, 2026-11-19).** engine/fatigue.ts fatigueLevelFactor, used by engine/duel.ts fighterEffectiveLevel: 1 - 0.25 x (1 - e(f)) up to 1.0x (unchanged), then a straight line from 0.825 at 1.0x to health.config FATIGUE_PAST_CAPACITY.atHardLimit 0.40 at the 4.0 hard limit (0.68 at 2x, 0.54 at 3x), never below; no step at 1.0. Fights only: checks out of a fight keep rolls §1's factor (rolls.ts), the case Jared asked about being fighters at 2-4x. rolls.md §1 notes it. *Anakin's retune* (fight counts only, tests/lifetime/profiles.ts; on the fixed seed and the median of anakin-s1 to -s5, 26-year runs): spars 150 -> 160 a year at ages 10-19, the first war year's extra B2 fights 6 -> 9, group spars from 22 on 280 -> 300. Before: the fixed seed failed 81 (16.43 y, limit 16.2) and the age-19 share (24.8%); the median failed the age-19 share (24.5%). After, every gate passes: fixed seed 21 1.25, 41 4.61, 61 8.86, 81 15.33, 101 21.90 y, shares 25.5 / 26.4 / 26.2%; median of s1-s5 21 1.31, 41 4.17, 61 8.56, 81 15.63, 101 21.82 y, shares 26.2 / 27.7 / 27.3%. Tests: tests/engine/fatigueLegFloor.test.ts.
- **D-230 amended: form drills count toward a lost leg (Jared, 2026-11-19).** health.config LEG_FORM_SHARE: a lightsaber form's practice hours count toward a lost leg's adaptation at this share, beside Agility and Endurance at full rate (features/health adaptationTrained). Jared's 50% gave a quality cybernetic leg 100% on day 41 of the default routine (Knight at 30), under the 45-60 day target; tuned to 0.3 (the share only, as allowed): 100% on day 51 (16.6% at day 10, 38.3% at 20, 59.3% at 30, 78.0% at 40; with no aid the 70% cap on day 36). A lost hand or arm already counts every form hour in full (its penalty is on the forms), so nothing changed there; a lower share for hands would have broken the hand's 30-40 day reference. Tests: fatigueLegFloor.
- **D-235 The lifetime 4.0x floor uses the clamp's Y_avg table (Jared, 2026-11-19; replaces the Average's target / 4).** tests/lifetime/checks.ts paceFloorYears: the fastest a tier can be reached at PACE_CAP x Y_avg(age) XP a year (the D-48 cap at the Average's main share 0.5) from the profile's start age, integrated day by day; expectWithin4x and the spread runner use it (LifeResult carries startAge). New floors (years since the start; old floor in brackets, the same for every profile): Average (start 4) 21 1.03, 41 3.28, 61 6.56, 81 11.08, 101 16.65 [1.25, 4.00, 8.03, 13.18, 19.38]; Obi-Wan (3) 1.04, 3.31, 6.56, 11.00, 16.59; Anakin (9) 1.02, 3.38, 6.98, 11.59, 18.06; Luke (19) 1.10, 3.70, 8.08, 14.19, 20.95 (stricter than before from 61, since Y_avg falls in the 20s). *Seeds passing* (the overnight 20-seed run): Average 20, Obi-Wan 20, Luke 20 (both floors); Anakin 12 of 20 with the old floor, 20 of 20 with the new; the closest seed is 1.125 x its floor (Average 4.0x, Obi-Wan 3.1x, Luke 1.5x). *On the final code* (D-234 and Anakin's retune, a fresh 20-seed run, docs-v2/testing/lifetime-spread.md end): all 80 lifetimes pass the new floor; closest seed average 4.01x, anakin 1.15x, obiwan 3.10x, luke 1.50x. The other gates on 20 seeds: Anakin's war share fails on 5 (was 8), Obi-Wan's on 3 (was 4), one seed each misses a milestone band (anakin-s1, obiwan-s8), the Average's K and Y_avg checks 2 each. *Does it let a profile pass that the clamp should not allow?* No case found: the only profile that newly passes is Anakin, and the clamp never binds for him (no XP discarded; his focus share 0.7 gives a cap 1.4x the floor's reference). The floor is the clamp's limit at share 0.5, so a profile whose clamp share is below 0.5 could in principle beat its own clamp and still pass; none of the four profiles comes near (closest 1.125x). Tests: fatigueLegFloor.
- **Accepted (Jared, 2026-11-19): the design choices made inside D-218 to D-228.** The held strike step resets the pressure to 0 (D-219); a state of mind counts once only when its two values share a sign (D-221); the execution chances (Sith 0.3 and bounty hunters 0.2 in lethal fights, D-218); the Yuuzhan Vong capture to execute and wear saber-resistant armor (D-224, D-225); timeouts do not count on the usage meter (D-226); the terrain accident's victim is picked at random among those who rolled under (D-228).
- **D-159 amended again: the natural war-share spread after D-234 (2026-11-19).** The 20-seed rerun on the final code replaces the ranges above: Anakin age 19 24.3-29.6% (median 26.4%), 20 25.2-28.6% (27.4%), 21 25.7-28.9% (26.7%); Obi-Wan age 35 21.4-27.4% (23.5%), 36 22.7-29.9% (26.7%), 37 22.2-27.4% (24.0%). The gates stay on the fixed seed.
- **D-236 Narration check precision: the slip rule and wording repair (Jared, combat pass 3 issues 3, 4 and 20).** *Problem:* rule 9 fired 8 times in pass 3 and 6 were false alarms ("your focus slips", "the concentration slips", "stumbling off balance" after a Choke the block calls "thrown off balance", a mortally wounded man who "stumbles backward"); and D-211's repair dropped the flagged sentence, which three times was the one carrying the turn's fact (a killing blow, a failed recall, a Choke's opening), with no second check. *Rules* (turn/narrationCheck.ts, turn/pipeline.ts): (1) **Rule 9 counts only the body.** Footing terms (FOOTING): footing, footwork, balance, feet or grip that falters, slips, gives way or fails; losing footing, balance or grip; "slips on <something>"; trips; a misstep; a stumble on or over something. Reaction terms (REACTION): stumbles, "you / he falls to the ground / hard / backward", drops to the knees. Never: "focus / concentration slips", "a slip of focus", "the moment slips away", a blade or a fighter slipping past, away or out of reach, "off balance" or "staggers" alone ("he catches you off balance" is being outpaced), "silence falls". (2) **What explains it, by fighter** (slipCauses; the clause's subject is the last "you" or "he / it / a name" before the term): a footing term needs a terrain accident on that fighter, a knockdown, or a Push or thrown object that succeeded on them; a reaction term is also fine after any hit, Choke or lightning on them. A terrain accident on one fighter no longer excuses a slip of the other (D-211 skipped the rule whenever the block had any accident). (3) **Wording repair only swaps terms** (repairWording): each severity label keeps its severity in natural words (minor light, moderate deep, severe grievous, critical terrible, mortal deadly); no sentence is ever removed. (4) **Order** (pipeline): issues that a swap alone settles are reworded in code and checked again, with no retry call ("reworded in code, no retry"); anything else is retried once as before; a reply that still has issues (or the first reply, when the retry call fails) is reworded when that is enough and re-checked, else a fight gets the D-209 summary, which carries every hit, wound, step and the end state. Outside a fight the offending sentences (an invented treatment, a repeated line) are removed, as before. (5) **Log lines** say what was done: "reworded in code, no retry", "still contradicting RESOLVED (...); code-written summary used instead", "the first reply stands and contradicts RESOLVED (...)" after a failed retry; the quoted sentence of an omission is its correction text, not an empty string. *Left out on purpose:* "overextends" (D-201 lists it; it is not a footing term in Jared's list, and it produced no false alarm or catch in passes 2 and 3; one pass-1 sentence, "as the warrior overextends", is no longer flagged), and a stumble that stands beside a real hit on that fighter ("advancing as it stumbles, then cleaving its right leg clean off": allowed, since the block has a hit on it). *Measured* (every AI narration saved in the browser from passes 1 to 3: 16 campaigns, 195 narrations, 116 of them in fights; the saved text is the final one, after any retry or repair; the code-written fallback of pass 2 left out): the old check fires on 34 narrations (47 issues: rule 9 21, rule 10 15, others 11), the new one on 30 (40 issues: rule 9 14, rule 10 15, hitNotInLog 2, minionNotHit 2, repeated 2, healingNotApplied 2, sever 1, woundOmitted 1, stepNotReached 1). Read one by one: 38 of the 40 are real contradictions and 2 could not be judged from the save (a sparring "touch", a "bacta-infused gel" out of a fight); none is a known false alarm. Rule 9: old 21 firings = 15 real, 3 false (a blade slipping past a guard, a fighter slipping out of reach, a dying man stumbling), 3 beside a real hit; new 14 firings, all 14 real, with 1 real one no longer caught (the "overextends" above). On the pass-3 engine-log quotes (first replies, not saved): old 8 firings, 2 real; new 1 firing (Mira's "your footing slips"), real. *Tests:* tests/turn/narrationCheckPass3.test.ts (every slip quote of passes 1 to 3, both kinds; causes by fighter; the repair never removing a sentence; the re-check; the pass-3 kill sentence); tests/turn/forceCombat.test.ts (through the pipeline: reworded with one call; a failed retry gives the summary, not a cut narration); three older fixtures changed where "his stumble" follows a hit the block has.
- **D-237 Narration errors the check missed (Jared, combat pass 3 issues 5, 6 and 7).** Five more rules in turn/narrationCheck.ts, each with the block line the narrator needs to get it right. (1) **Rule 13, a shot never taken** (stepNotReached; combatFacts.playerShotNotReached): when the player character carries a ranged weapon, a strike step is NOT REACHED and no strike of theirs was resolved, the build-up line reads "X and Y maneuver for position: NO HIT, and X gets no shot off" (it read "trade blows", which for a blaster is firing), and a sentence in which they fire ("fire back", "return fire", "squeeze the trigger", "your bolt ...") is a contradiction. (2) **Rule 14, a failed Force action shown working** (stepFailed): a Force kind that failed in the passage with no success of that kind may not show its effect (a Push that throws him back, a choke that leaves him gasping, lightning that strikes him, a thrown object that hits, the saber leaping back to the hand). The attempt and the failure are fine; a throw and a recall share the saber-in-hand picture, so one succeeding excuses both. (3) **Rule 15, ground given to the wrong side** (groundReversed): when the track never moved the player's way in the passage (no exchange gained, no hit, no successful Force action or maneuver), the opponent does not "give ground", "yield an inch" or get "driven back", and the player does not "claim ground". Not when it is an aim ("to drive him back") or the player is the one driven. (4) **Rule 16, survivor counts** (survivorCount): the block has "Opponents still standing: N (names)", and "the surviving X", "the last X", "the final X", "the only one left", "the lone survivor" are a contradiction while more than one opponent stands (opponents fighting an ally are not counted). **Rule 16b** (gearNotInBlock): a scatter gun or a slugthrower in the prose when no fighter carries one (pass 3: the scatter gun of the fight before). (5) **Rule 17, lasting state** (lastingState; engine/lasting.ts lastingState): a body's lasting state is its limbs severed and not replaced: fresh severed wounds, and lost limbs with no aid or scar tissue only; a limb with a prosthetic on record is whole for this purpose (health gives the faction's aid at once, D-164), and a lost arm includes its hand. In words: "cannot stand: severed left leg", "no right arm (severed)". It is in the fighter view (CombatFighterView.lasting), on the block's "Now:" line with the rule ("A fighter who cannot stand stays down ..."), and on every NPC's line in STATE, so a spared or captured fighter keeps it in later scenes. The check (lastingIssues) flags a fighter who cannot stand standing up, rising, walking, stepping, kicking, charging or being helped to their feet, and a missing arm or hand in use ("his right hand" when it is gone, "both hands"); not an attempt that fails ("tries to stand and collapses"), not the injury itself (stump, severed, missing), not dragging or crawling. Who a sentence is about: the one listed fighter it names, or for a pronoun-only sentence the one named just before; a sentence that names anybody else is left alone. In a fight the state is the one at the start of the passage (a limb severed in this passage is the hit rules' business); outside a fight it comes from the state before and after the turn (context.lastingStates: the player, the NPCs present, the NPCs whose fate is open). *Fallback:* all five are factual, so a reply that still has one after the retry gets the D-209 summary in a fight; outside a fight the sentence is removed (an invented action, as for an invented treatment). *Measured* on the saved narrations of passes 1 to 3 (195, as in D-236): 32 narrations flagged, 45 issues; the five new ones are the pass-3 sentences (the Mandalorian's two, the smuggler's three) and nothing else; four false alarms found on the way (an opponent "driving you back to gain ground", "as Line trooper 1 claims ground", "to drive Duelist 5 back with the Force", a recall's success beside a failed throw) were fixed and are fixtures. *Not built:* the captors named wrongly after a dev fight ("the remaining war droids" for line troopers): the block cannot know which minions a scene may hold; the fate narration now gets the NPC lines with their lasting state, and D-215's dev fights still give the narrator no scene for their opponents. *Tests:* tests/turn/narrationMissed.test.ts (the report's exact sentences, the honest tellings, the block lines, the state line).
- **D-238 One RNG stream: the fight's dice continue the campaign's (Jared, combat pass 3 blocker).** *Problem:* three duels in a row in one campaign had the same d20s for 60 exchanges, and the same arm was severed at exchange 5 twice. *Cause* (features/combat/index.ts): startFight copied meta.rngState into the duel and wrote it back once, at the start; every exchange then advanced only the duel's copy, and conclude never wrote that copy back. The player's fate roll also drew from meta, the state from before the first exchange. So the next fight began where the last one began. *Rule:* there is one stream. Every combat handler that draws dice (combatAction, combatEnded, devTerrainRolled) starts the duel from meta.rngState and returns the stream after its last draw (settle and conclude always return it; the fate roll draws from the duel's stream after the last exchange; the disengage path, which dropped it, returns it). The duel's stored rng is only a mirror between turns. Determinism is unchanged: the same save and the same input give the same dice. *Other dice checked* (all already write their stream back to meta): checks and the mind-trick contest (turn/resolve.ts), training (progression/training.ts, via resolve and advanceRoutine), healing's lasting-injury rolls and hazard locations (features/health), world and chapter events (features/world), minion grades and fight-start rolls (startFight, returned). NPC generation is seeded per NPC by design (D-143 G: npcSeed of the campaign and the NPC id; the beskar and slugthrower rolls by the NPC's seed), so the same NPC is the same NPC; every dev duelist has a new id. The sweep and the lifetime harness build their duels on their own seeds and are unaffected. *Tests:* tests/engine/rngStream.test.ts (two fights in a row differ; a save replayed with the same input gives the same dice, also mid-fight; 10 fights in one campaign share no run of 5 identical d20 pairs and the natural-1 rate stays under 10%; three of the four fail on the old code). npm run test:all 924/924.
- **D-239 Yuuzhan Vong (and other species with rules) from the narrator's words (Jared, combat pass 3 issue 2).** *Problem:* the narrator introduced "Yuuzhan Vong warrior" with species unknown; generation made him a soldier with a blaster rifle, the Force worked on him, and he stabilized his dying prisoner, so D-224 (archetype), D-192 (outside the Force) and D-225 (captures to execute) never applied. *Rules:* (1) **Species from the words** (data/speciesWords.ts inferSpecies; features/npcs npcIntroduced): when the narrator's species is unknown, other or human, the name decides ("a Yuuzhan Vong warrior" is one even when sent as human), then, for unknown or other only, the description (vonduun, amphistaff, coralskipper; a human may wear a trophy). A table covers the species with rules (Yuuzhan Vong, Hutt, Toydarian, droid) and the other species names in the list. The note says what was read ("species yuuzhanVong from the name (the narrator sent unknown)"). **Era:** a species is inferred only in the years it can be met; the Vong from 30 BBY on (Legends: their scouts met Vergere in 29 BBY and Outbound Flight in 27 BBY; the invasion began in 25 ABY; design default). Out of era the species is kept and the note says none are met then. (2) **A weapon the description names** (weaponFromDescription; features/combat/build.ts): an NPC whose role gives a ranged weapon but whose description names a melee one (an amphistaff, a vibroblade, a sword, a staff, an axe, a knife, claws) fights in melee; a description that names a blaster or a saber too keeps the role's weapon. *Read of the brief:* "an unknown species never gets a soldier blaster by default; it is generated from the role and era". A named weapon now always beats the role's default, for any species. When the description names no weapon, the role and era still decide, and a soldier's role in any era carries a rifle; I did not invent a weapon the role does not give. (3) **A Vong minion kind** (data/npcGen.config.ts yuuzhanVongWarrior): the D-224 archetype as minions (melee, heavy saber-resistant armor, outside the Force, coordination 0.5, Vitality x1.2, grades centred on 3, melee at the soldier's blaster pace), from 25 ABY on; out of era a brawler with the D-213 note. The narrator's combatStarted can name it. (4) **Captors who are minions** (features/combat conclude): a minion winner's captor rules and fate table are its kind's archetype's, not "soldier" for every kind, so Vong warriors do not stabilize a dying prisoner. An execution on surrender stays a named captor's choice (D-218); minions only capture. *Side effect, for Jared:* the fate table of a minion winner now follows its kind (thugs underworld, militia and beasts other, Sith acolytes Sith, troopers and droids military as before). *Tests:* tests/engine/vongFromNarrator.test.ts (the pass-3 event gives yuuzhanVong, the warrior archetype, an amphistaff, saber-resistant armor, Force immunity and no stabilizing; named and minion; the era fallback; the inference and weapon rules).
- **D-240 Gemini overload: retries, the next model, then Retry (Jared, combat pass 3).** *Problem:* about 100 of 166 requests in pass 3 came back as HTTP 503 ("high demand"); each one stopped the turn at a Retry button, and the narration check's own retry failed nine times, leaving the check's fallback in place. *Rules* (turn/pipeline.ts withOverloadRetry and callModel; ai/models.config.ts OVERLOAD_RETRY; ai/fallback.ts nextModelAfter): (1) An **overload** is an HTTP 5xx or a timeout (isOverload). Not a 4xx, a 429 (the daily-quota fallback, D-80, as before), a truncated, blocked or malformed reply, or a network error. (2) On an overload the same call is retried on the same model up to **3 times**, waiting 2 s, 4 s, 8 s (base 2,000 ms x 2^(n-1), capped at 10 s) with +/-25% jitter; then it is tried **once on the next model** in the call type's fallback order that is not exhausted today; if that fails too, or there is no next model (intent, summary and OOC have one), the turn fails with the last error and the UI shows Retry as before. Worst case for a narrative call: 5 requests and about 14 s of waiting. (3) The **same request** every time: the turn's draft (dice, exchanges, events) is resolved once before any call (prepareTurn), so a retry never rolls again. (4) The status line shows "Resolving: ... · Gemini is busy, retrying… (n of 3)", then "trying <model>…", cleared when the call ends (TurnDeps.onStatus). The engine log has a line per retry ("gemini-3.8-flash: busy (503); retry 1 of 3 in 2.0 s") and one for the next model. (5) The usage meter still counts only 2xx and 429 replies (D-226): none of the overload retries count. The jitter comes from the wall clock's milliseconds (pacing, not game dice; Math.random is banned by the lint rule; tests pass their own). *Changed tests:* a summary failure test now fails with a 400 (a 500 is retried now); the D-236 pipeline test whose retry call fails gives five 503s. *Tests:* tests/turn/overloadRetry.test.ts (two 503s then a reply; four then the next model; the next one busy too gives the Retry error and nothing on the meter; a timeout retried, a 400 not; the backoff numbers; the next-model choice).
- **D-241 Dev commands for the items pass 3 could not reach (Jared; dev mode, test campaigns only).** turn/devCommands.ts, with three dev-only events. (1) **/sever <limb>** (out of a fight; the duel owns its wounds): a severe, severed wound on an arm, hand or leg through the normal healthSynced path, so it becomes the lasting injury with the path's prosthetic (D-149, D-164) and adapts as D-230 says. (2) **/fatigue** up to 5x capacity (was 3), past the physical collapse at 4x (D-208); a value out of range is now said ("at 5 (asked 9; the range is 0 to 5)"; pass 3: "/fatigue 3.9" answered "at 3"). (3) **/exchanges <n>** (devExchanges): up to n exchanges with the current stance, as Resolve to end runs them (the saber recall first, D-208), stopping when the fight ends, with the roll cards in the turn. **/exchanges to <n>** moves the exchange counter to n with no rolls first, then fights on to the end: real duels end long before the 200-exchange cap (16 probe duels of a Master at 60 against duelists at gap 0 and +30 ended in 7 to 141 exchanges), so "to 199" is the way to reach it. A sparring session is refused (its bouts have their own budget). (4) **/hazard <type> <1-3> [end]**: the narrator's hazardOccurred exactly as a reply carries it (source ai), and with end in a fight its combatEnded interrupted (environmentCollapse) in the same batch, so D-212 (no damage while the fight goes on), D-217 (rolled for everyone when it ends the fight) and D-29 (deferred out of a fight) can be tested without the narrator. (5) **/stakes <serious|lethal|skirmish>** (devStakesSet): the current fight's stakes and the duel's dying-risk flag; not to or from a spar. **/archetype <id>** (devNpcArchetypeSet): the current named opponent's generation archetype, which the captor and fate rules read at the end (D-218); the fighter keeps its levels and weapon; a minion is refused (its kind decides). Together, /stakes lethal and /archetype sithWarrior force the surrender execution branch. *Tests:* tests/turn/devPass4.test.ts (a severed leg's lasting injury and its adaptation over /skip 20 days; fatigue 5 and the message; the collapse at 4x through /exchanges; /exchanges to 199 ending as the 200-exchange stalemate; /hazard with and without end and out of a fight; 30 lethal surrenders to a Sith Warrior: some killing strikes, the rest captures).
- **D-242 Combat pass 3's minor issues (8-24) and the captors after a fight (Jared).** Each issue fixed or left with the reason. **Captors (added by Jared):** a capture records who took the player (features/combat conclude captorsOf: a named winner by name; a minion winner as its kind's group still standing, "4 line troopers", with the names, the time and the place; FightSummary.captors). The surrender turn's block has "Captured by: 4 line troopers (Line trooper 1-4, still standing). Name these captors and no others."; STATE carries "CAPTIVE: the player character was captured by ..." in the following scenes while the player is still there and for 24 game hours (CAPTIVE_LINE_SECONDS, design default; context.selectCaptors). The check's **rule 18** (captorsWrong): a sentence about taking or holding the prisoner (seize, grip, drag, bind, binders, captor, prisoner, close in, march you, ...) that names another minion kind ("the remaining war droids") and not the captors' own noun; only for minion captors. **8 partly fixed:** "taking a light blow to your own ribs" with no hit on the player is now a hitNotInLog (PLAYER_IS_HIT). Left: grazes given a body part the block does not have, since the block gives grazes a part and rule 7 checks a fighter only when the narration names a wound word and a possessive; narrowing it further gave false alarms in the saved narrations. **9 partly fixed:** rule 16c, blades locked or clashing when no opponent carries a blade (a B1 "locking blades"). Left: "reinforced plating" on a droid with light armor (the block lists the armor) and a crate in a forest clearing (scenery). **10 fixed:** labels on more nouns (stroke, impact, slug, shot, bolt, thrust, slash; "mortal thrust"), stamina wording ("drains your stamina": the repair swaps it for "wears you down", so no retry), the step list in prose ("your intended strikes", "your remaining sequence"). **11 fixed:** a passage of 10 exchanges or more gets "Key events (tell each one, in order, however briefly)" listing its wounding hits and the hits that put someone out. **12 left:** the stock sentences sit under rule 8's thresholds (80% word overlap, 60% of sentences); lowering them flagged ordinary repeated phrasing in the saved narrations. **13 fixed:** core-gm.md: bolts and blades, never "energy beams", "laser beams" or a "magnetic field"; only a lightsaber turns a bolt aside; the unarmed weapon text says "dodges bolts and blows, cannot turn them aside". Left: "neon" (Legends prose uses it for Nar Shaddaa) and B1s dodging (the dice). **14 left:** the player's feelings: the prompt rule stands; a check for "heart pounding" and the like would also catch sensations the rules allow ("gritting your teeth against the agony"). **15 fixed:** a training saber only in a spar: in a real fight an initiate or acolyte carries a lightsaber (buildNpcFighter live). **16 fixed:** a fate choice that lapses (past FATE_CHOICE_EXPIRY or once the player has left) tells the player: "X was left where they fell", or for a fighter left dying "X was left dying and did not survive" with npcDied (design default: a lapse is "Spare (leave dying)"). **17 fixed:** a player who is dying cannot fight: a fight that begins while they are dying ends at once as lost (dying) and the opponent's choice follows, typed or dev (/fight says "fight begun and over at once"). **18 fixed:** a stance step the intent added when the words name none (no stance word of D-179's groups, no "aggressive", "defensively", "stance") is dropped ("dive behind a stack of crates", "keep low"); "press" and "sidestep" keep their D-179 stances, and a stance stays set as D-182 says. **19 fixed:** an intent ref that names a minion of the fight ("npc:line-trooper-1") is the fight's, not a stranger: noted, not sent to review (turn/refs.ts fightMinionRef). **20 fixed** by D-236 (the log says what was done; an omission's quote is its correction). **21 fixed:** "AI calls this fight" counts as the usage meter does (no 5xx, timeouts or network errors). **22 partly fixed:** the XP line shows the surrender factor ("serious, lost, surrendered x0.5"); a severed limb's note is in words ("right arm severed: a lasting injury (a quality cybernetic)"); a minion shot down by a shooter is "shoots down", and a person is "they" (a droid "it"); the dev fight line names the kind fielded ("4 line troopers" over the era note); "+N more" counts an opponent fighting an ally apart; the end card shows the captors' stabilizing ("Dying" no more after "stabilized by the captors"). Left: the engine log's raw reasons (allOut, truce (truce): the engine log is a dev view), "Healthy" at part Vitality (health §6 labels by wounds), the save list's branches (UI; for the save-list pass), the hold line after a natural 1 (the reason is on the card). **23 partly fixed:** at 900 px and narrower the date drops its weekday (a span of its own, still in the tooltip) and the place takes the room left. Left: 30 px buttons (D-216: Jared's call with the iPad in hand). **24 left:** the carry-overs are dev-made (a captor still "here now" through /fight, a montage encounter, M6) or the narrator's own events (metCouncil, a relationship +5 for a captor, pronouns); a capture taking the player's weapon is equipment (M8). *Measured* on the saved narrations of passes 1 to 3 (195, as in D-236): 22 new firings from rules 8, 10, 16c and 18, of which 21 are real (the stamina wording, the labels, the step list, two blades with no blade, the pass-3 captors sentence) and one was the noun "a drain on his stamina", which no swap reads well: excluded. *Sweep after:* the 21 Knight-50 rows unchanged; the 38 rows against Sith acolytes moved (issue 15: they fought with training sabers and never wounded anyone; now they carry lightsabers, e.g. a Padawan at 16 against an acolyte 10 below wins 75% and is wounded 50%, as against other duelists at that gap). Balance list row 11 in combat-pass-3.md. *Tests:* tests/turn/minorFixesPass3.test.ts; tests/engine/backlashAndLabels.test.ts (the hit notes) and tests/engine/balanceRows.test.ts and vongFromNarrator.test.ts (a dying player's fight ends at its start).
- **Jared's calls on the pass-3 balance list and readings (2026-11-19).** *Row 2, heroicAct:* a won lethal fight against a Sith Lord or higher counts as a heroic act whatever the gap: the original definition (flag-registry §2), which HEROIC_ACT already is (ranks lord and master); no change. *Row 3, precognition's Force load in long duels:* no change; the M6 Force redesign revisits it. *Accepted:* a weapon the description names wins over the role's weapon (D-239); a lapsed fate choice leaves a dying fighter to die (D-242); Sith acolytes armed with lightsabers in real fights, and the 38 sweep rows that moved with it (D-242 item 15, balance row 11); a dying player's fight ending at once (D-242 item 17). Rows 4 and 5: D-243 and D-244.
- **D-243 Cover against ranged attacks (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 3 balance row 4).** engine/duel.ts sideMods; combat.config COVER_DEFENSE 2. In a fight whose terrain has cover (the cover tag), a fighter with a ranged weapon defends at +2 ("cover" on the card) against a ranged attack. A fighter with a blade is not in cover (closing with the shooters), the same reading as D-192's inCover for the Force; so cover counts between shooters, both sides. *Sweep, new rows (300 fights each):* a smuggler at 30 (blaster pistol, balanced) against 4 line troopers: flat floor 64.3% won, 81.7% wounded, 78.6 exchanges; ruins with cover 77.3% won, 69.0% wounded, 87.6 exchanges. A Knight at 50 against 6 troopers in cover: 99.7% / 7.3% (balanced), 100% / 6.0% (mixed) (flat: 99.7 / 5.0, 99.7 / 5.7; the Knight is not a shooter, so the difference is the ruins' footing and the seeds). *For Jared:* a smuggler at 30 losing a third of fights to four line troopers on open ground, wounded in four of five, looks hard for a career fighter of that age; not tuned. *Jared (2026-11-19): accepted as is.* End-of-production character traits (architecture §15, future work: a gunslinger trait, for example) are where blaster specialists improve against these odds.
- **D-244 The weapon-arm rule adopted, and the duel-length retune (Jared, 2026-11-19; pass 3 balance row 5; lore).** *Rule* (health.config WOUND_FIGHTING.weaponArmLost true; engine/duel.ts weaponArmState): a fighter whose weapon arm or hand is severed is out of the duel, unless they have an off-hand weapon (Fighter.offHandWeapon), which they then fight with at the off-hand penalty (0.4). A limb that already carries a prosthetic is whole. Nobody has an off-hand weapon yet (dual blades are items, M8; Jar'Kai cannot be fought in until then), so today the loss ends the duel. D-198's other half, the forced hand switch after a severe arm wound (weaponArm), stays off: Jared's call named only the loss. *Effect:* the rule ends 17% of even duels; medians fell to 30 / 26 / 28 / 25 / 21 / 17 / 12 at gaps 0 / 5 / 10 / 20 / 30 / 40 / 60 (gaps 5 and 10 under the 30-40 band; wins all within +/-3). *Retune, D-206's tunables only* (a grid of pressure x precognition at 4,000 duels per gap: pressure 3 with D-206's precognition gave 33 / 29 / 30, gap 5 still short; pressure 2 with min(0.5, FS / 200) 31 / 28 / 30; pressure 2 with min(0.4, FS / 175) 32 / 30 / 32 / 28, passing at the edge): **MISSED_STRIKE_STEP 3 (was 2) and PRECOG min(0.5, Force Sense / 200) (was min(0.4, FS / 250))**, the original precognition of D-156. *Rolls §8 table 1 at 10,000 duels per gap (win % / median; target):* gap 0 49.8 / 34 (50 / 34), +5 59.6 / 32 (60 / 33), +10 68.7 / 33 (68 / 34), +20 82.6 / 29 (84 / 29), +30 91.2 / 24 (92 / 24), +40 95.7 / 19 (96 / 20), +60 99.0 / 14 (99 / 13); tests 2, 3 and 5 pass. *Lifetime:* every milestone gate passed; two war-share gates failed on the fixed seed: Anakin at 19, 20.4% (band 25-35%), and Obi-Wan at 36, 32.3% (band 20-30%). *Retune, fight counts only* (tests/lifetime/profiles.ts): Obi-Wan's second war year 12 -> 10 duels (9 gave 21.1%, 10 gives 23.5%); Anakin's first war year + 25 sparring sessions with his master (more war fights did not lift the share, since the daily combat cap of 12 XP binds: +7 B2 fights gave 19.6%, +18 B1 fights 21.9%, +4 duels 20.3%; +60 sessions 36.2%). After: Anakin 27.1 / 27.5 / 25.2% at 19-21 (milestones 21 1.30, 41 3.99, 61 8.39, 81 15.27, 101 21.42 y), Obi-Wan 25.1 / 23.5 / 25.6% at 35-37 (81 39.73, 101 59.96 y); npm run test:all 965/965. The 5-seed median check of D-234 was not re-run (the spread runner is not in test:all). *A test rewritten:* duel.test.ts's D-199 case started at -5 and relied on a missed strike leaving the track at -4; with pressure 3 a miss from -5 goes to -2, so the case now reaches the rule through a build-up from -2. *Sweep after* (Knight at 50, 21 rows): within 2 points of before (e.g. 10 militia heavyForce 70.7 -> 68.7%, 4 Mandalorians heavyForce 87.3 -> 89.0%), no issues. *Tests:* tests/engine/woundFighting.test.ts (a severed weapon arm or hand ends the duel, the other arm does not, an off-hand weapon fights on at the penalty, a prosthetic is whole, a left-handed fighter mirrors it).
- **D-159 amended: the lifetime gates run on a representative seed, and the war shares are tuned to the 20-seed median (Jared, 2026-11-19).** *Why:* the fixed seeds are arbitrary; the D-244 retune, done on them, put Anakin's fixed seed at the bottom of his age-19 spread (27.1%, the lowest of 20) and the retune pushed the median to 33.9% with six seeds above the band, and Obi-Wan's age-36 median to 21.4% with five seeds below it. *The gated seed:* the four profile tests (tests/lifetime/average, anakin, obiwan, luke) run on `<profile>-s7` (profiles.ts GATED_SEED_SUFFIX, gated()). Chosen from the 20-seed spread after D-244 as the seed index closest to the median across every gated quantity of all four profiles: the sum over the profiles' milestones, war shares, peacetime share, Y_avg deviation and physical peak of |value - median| / (p90 - p10). s7 scored 4.89 (Average 0.66, Anakin 1.34, Obi-Wan 2.41, Luke 0.49) and passed every gate; next s6 5.50, then s12 7.58; the old fixed seed ranked 7th at 8.20. The spread runner still runs the profile seed and -s1 to -s19, so the 20 seeds are the same set. The closed-form, swordmaster and archetype lifetime checks keep their own seeds. *Retune, fight counts only* (tests/lifetime/profiles.ts): Anakin's master sessions 25 -> 11 in the first war year and + 10 in the third (age 21); Obi-Wan's second-war-year duels 10 -> 11. *20-seed results* (docs-v2/testing/lifetime-spread.md end): war-share medians Anakin 29.4 / 27.9 / 29.6% at 19 / 20 / 21 (band 25-35%), Obi-Wan 22.1 / 24.8 / 24.7% at 35 / 36 / 37 (band 20-30%); every milestone median inside its gates. Seeds failing the war-share gate: Anakin 1 (the old fixed seed, 21.5% at 19), Obi-Wan **4** (s16 19.1% at 35; s2 30.4% at 36; s9 and s18 30.7% at 37). The target was at most 2 of 20; Anakin meets it, Obi-Wan does not. s7 passes every gate of all four profiles; npm run test:all 965/965. *Obi-Wan, not settled (waiting for Jared):* his three medians are within 0.3 points of the band middles at 36 and 37 and his 10th-90th ranges (21.4-25.5, 22.4-28.7, 22.0-28.7%) lie inside the band, so the four failures are tails beyond the 10th-90th range at three different ages, two of them high and one low at 37 alone. A fight count moves the whole distribution, so it trades a tail on one side for one on the other. See the options in the progress line of the same date.
- **D-159 amended: the war-share gate is the mean over the war years (Jared, 2026-11-19; option (d)).** For both Anakin (ages 19-21, band 25-35%) and Obi-Wan (ages 35-37, band 20-30%), the gate checks the mean of the war years' combat shares against the same band; the per-year shares are printed by the tests and kept in the spread rows and in lifetime-spread.md as information, not gated (tests/lifetime/anakin.test.ts, obiwan.test.ts; tests/spread/spread.ts, which also records warMean). On the 20-seed results above: Anakin's mean 28.9% median (10th-90th 27.7-29.8%, min-max 25.0-31.6%, s7 29.7%), no seed failing; Obi-Wan's 24.2% median (23.0-26.5%, 21.9-27.4%, s7 25.1%), no seed failing. *Accepted as seed spread (Jared):* `anakin-s18` reaching 81 at 16.21 y against the 16.20 limit (milestones +/-20% and the 1.2 x target bound). npm run test:all 965/965.
- **D-245 A fitted cybernetic leg stands, and the leg's penalty reaches the duel (Jared, combat pass 4 issue 1).** *Problem:* after /sever left leg (a quality cybernetic fitted at once, D-164) every fight ended after one exchange with no line saying why, still at 69% adapted 40 days later. *Cause:* engine/duel.ts cannotStand counted any severed leg wound in the fighter's wounds, and the healing wound is carried into the duel; the weapon-arm rule (D-244) already skipped a fitted limb, the leg rule did not. *Rule:* cannotStand is lastingState(...).cannotStand, the reading the narrator's block and the check already use (D-237): a severed leg with a prosthetic on record is whole; a leg severed in this fight, or lost with no aid or scar tissue only, is not. *The reason in the log:* a player who cannot stand (or has no weapon arm) when a fight begins loses it at once, like a dying one (D-242 item 17): "the player character cannot fight (cannot stand: severed left leg): the fight ends at once"; an opponent who comes into a fight unable to stand gets "X cannot stand: severed left leg: out of the fight" on the card. *The leg's penalty in a fight:* progression-numbers §18 gives a lost leg Agility 0.35 and Endurance 0.15, x (1 - adaptation), and no form penalty; until now the Agility penalty never reached a duel except for an unarmed fighter. fighterAgility applies it where a fight rolls Agility (dodging a Force action, a disengage). A fighter striking with a form is not slowed by the leg, as §18 has it. *For Jared:* if a lost leg should cut form strikes too (forms are footwork, D-230), that is a spec change to §18 (e.g. forms at LEG_FORM_SHARE x the Agility penalty); not built. The Endurance penalty still does not reach the meter capacities (selectCapacities reads Endurance performance with no lasting penalty), in or out of a fight; recorded, not changed. *Tests:* tests/engine/cyberneticLeg.test.ts (the rule with and without an aid; the Agility penalty by adaptation; a fitted leg fighting on; 40 days later at partial adaptation; no aid: the fight ends at once with the reason).
- **D-246 Generated levels for every generated fighter: the Yuuzhan Vong warrior (Jared, combat pass 4 issue 2).** *Problem:* the narrator's Vong warrior fought at level +0 with 20 Vitality and was worth 0 XP; dev-made Vong minions had +3.5 and 24. *Cause:* features/character/creation.ts refused an archetype not on the creation form ("not an archetype of the career path"), and yuuzhanVongWarrior (D-224) is NPC-only, so npcLevelsFor returned an empty level table and every stat read 0. The minions had the same empty table: their melee level is the kind's profile (D-195), patched in on top, so they fought, but with no Endurance (Vitality the empty floor x 1.2 = 24 at every grade), no Agility and no Willpower. *Rule:* data/archetypes.ts NPC_ARCHETYPES lists the generator-only archetypes by path (career: yuuzhanVongWarrior); creation accepts them when the input is an NPC's (CreationInput.npc) and still refuses them for a player. *Effect:* the pass-4 warrior (30, trained from 28.5 by his seeded start age) gets melee 13.8, Endurance 40.7, Vitality 57, the level of a grade-1 dev warrior (14.0); Vong minions grade 1-5: melee 14.0 / 23.5 / 34.7 / 43.5 / 51.0 as before, Vitality 62 / 70 / 72 / 74 / 75 (was 24). *Checked for the same gap:* every role, faction and species override the narrator can send (ROLE_ARCHETYPES, FACTION_ARCHETYPE, SPECIES_ARCHETYPE, the civilian default) and every minion kind at every grade: only the Vong archetype was refused. *Not changed:* a named Vong's start age is the archetype's seeded range [16, 30], so a 30-year-old may have trained only a year or two; that is the generator's spread for every career NPC. The sweep has no Vong rows. *Tests:* tests/engine/npcLevelsEveryArchetype.test.ts (every narrator path and minion kind has levels; creation refuses the NPC-only archetype for a player; the pass-4 event against dev-made warriors of each grade; a dev Vong group's Endurance and Vitality).
- **D-247 Rule 17 names a fighter by the full name only (Jared, combat pass 4 issue 3).** *Problem:* after Duelist 37 lost a leg, the Spare narration's "Duelist 9 steps forward slightly from the shadows of the ferns, ..." was flagged as Duelist 37 standing: a retry call and a true sentence removed. *Cause:* turn/narrationCheck.ts lastingIssues matched a listed fighter by any word of four letters or more of the name, so every "Duelist", "Mandalorian", "warrior" or shared family name matched. *Rule:* a fighter is matched by the whole name, case-insensitive, not inside a longer word or number ("Duelist 3" is not "Duelist 37"). A sentence that names somebody else still drops the pronoun referent, as before. *Left out on purpose:* a canon fighter named by the surname alone ("Malgus" for Darth Malgus) is no longer matched; a pronoun sentence after the full name still is. IDs never appear in prose, so the full name is the only key. *Tests:* tests/turn/narrationMissed.test.ts (the exact pass-4 sentence must not fire; "Duelist 3" against "Duelist 37"; two Mandalorian warriors).
- **D-248 Combat pass 4's minor issues (4-15) (Jared).** The report lists 12 minor issues (4 to 15); each is fixed or left with the reason. **4 fixed:** a capture records the captors' weapon (FightSummary.captors.weapon: "slugthrower rifles", or the kind's own weapon name), and STATE's CAPTIVE line says "armed with slugthrower rifles and no other weapons" and "A blow from a captor is a story event only: it does not wound the character" (pass 4: "a heavy blaster" and an armored backhand next scene). **5 partly fixed:** the cards' "out of the fight" and "is / are outpaced" are engine terms with natural swaps ("unable to fight on", "pressed hard"), reworded in code with no retry; "intended maneuver" and "unreached" join the step-list words. Left: "outpaced pressure" and "outpacing your guard" (ordinary prose), and "no wound" (ordinary prose too: "the blow leaves no wound"). **6 partly fixed:** the block's build-up line says "trade blows" only between two fighters who strike up close; a shooter's line is "maneuver while X fires", two shooters "trade fire" (an unarmed Jedi "traded blows" with a blaster droid because the block said so). Left, as D-242 left them: graze places, and the stock sentences under rule 8. **7 fixed:** the block has "Cover: the shooters on both sides fire from behind cover" when the terrain has cover and both sides shoot (D-243); "recover / regain your footing / balance" is a rule-9 reaction term (fine after a hit, Choke or Push on that fighter, an invented slip otherwise). **8 partly fixed:** core-gm.md: a lightsaber cauterizes (burns sealed as they are made, a seared stump that does not bleed) and catches lightning on the blade; a datacron is a store of knowledge, messages come by comlink or holomessage. Left: "plasma" for a blade (Legends uses it), "the Galactic Capital" for Coruscant. **9 fixed:** canThrowSaber compares with a tolerance of 1e-6 (/set telekinesis 15 read back 14.9999... from its XP), and the Saber throw button shows only when the engine would allow the throw. **10 fixed:** the character keeps naturalHand when a loss switches the dominant hand; /heal all restores it (dominantHandRestored). Left for Jared: Q39 switches the hand on any loss of the dominant arm or hand, even when a prosthetic is fitted at once and D-244 counts that limb whole; keeping the hand when an aid is fitted would match D-244 (the lasting penalty on forms is the same either way). **11 fixed:** a choice's targetRef that is a temp key of this reply becomes the minted ID, as the reply's events do. **12 fixed:** the end card says "a killing strike", "taken captive", "left where you fell"; interruption causes in words ("the place came down"); the player's status after a killing strike shows the mortal wound ("Dying", not "Healthy"); the opponent's line shows the weapon's own name and the armor with its resistance (the brief's armor item: "amphistaff · vonduun crab armor (resists lightsabers)"; Fighter.weaponName and armorName, from the description or the Vong caste), and the narrator's block has them too. Left: "Healthy · Healthy" after /exchanges to 199 (no rolls were made: the dev counter only). **13 fixed:** dev command turns do not count toward a chapter's length (memory.current.devTurns), so a test pass does not pay a summary call every 30 dev turns. **14 fixed (unmeasured):** at iPad width the date and the place stack in two short lines (styles.css .tb-where) instead of one cut line; pass 5 measures it. Left: 30 px buttons (D-216, Jared's call). **15 partly fixed:** the narrator cannot introduce a fight's minion as an NPC (npcIntroduced refused for a minion name of the active or last fight); earlier dev duelists leave the scene at the next dev fight unless a fate choice about them is open; a smuggler's /fight gap counts from the weapon skill he fights with (playerFormLevel falls back to the fighting level; the line says "your level"). Left: a Jedi's dev opponent is a Sith Lord by design (D-180: the other path), whatever the era. *Tests:* tests/turn/minorFixesPass4.test.ts (each fix with the report's case); three older fixtures changed for the new wording (the armor line, "out of the fight" now reworded, a duelist's "recovers his footing" after a hit stays allowed).
- **D-249 The test start's rank list by the archetype's own ranks; the pass-4 balance flags (Jared).** *Varn's rank (pass 4):* the dev form's rank list was the four path-free tiers labeled initiate / padawan / knight / master for every archetype, so a Sith Warrior had no "Sith Lord" to pick (the Lord is the knight tier, RANK_ON_PATH) and a pick of a missing option left the acolyte tier. Not an order problem: the archetype applied, the labels did not follow it. *Rule:* turn/testStart.ts testStartRankOptions labels the tiers with the path's ranks (Jedi: Initiate, Padawan, Knight, Master; Sith: Academy acolyte, Apprentice, Sith Lord; a career: its one rank), and the form refills the list when the archetype changes, keeping the tier where it exists. *Balance flags (combat-pass-4.md issue 16):* one is a bug and is fixed: flag 6, fatigue left at 20x after /heal all made every later dev fight end at exchange 1; the fix is the help line ("Fatigue is separate: /fatigue physical 0"), since /heal is health and fatigue its own meter. The other nine are not bugs; recommendations for Jared are in the pass-5 handoff (progress.md). *Tests:* tests/turn/testStart.test.ts (the three lists; the Sith Lord option gives rank lord).
- **D-250 Dev /simulate 503 n, and tests for what pass 4 could not reach (Jared).** *The command:* ai/simulateOverload.ts wraps the page's provider; "/simulate 503 n" (0 to 10; dev mode, test campaigns only) arms it so the next n model requests fail as HTTP 503 before any network call, and the D-240 path follows as for a real one: the retries and their log lines, the "Gemini is busy, retrying… (n of 3)" note, one try on the next model after the third retry, Retry when that fails too. The count lives in the page only, never in a save (the command's turn records the line; the state is unchanged); 0 clears it. A simulated 503 is never on the usage meter (no request was made). 5 is a full narrative fallback (3 retries, the next model, its failure). *Covered by tests instead of the browser* (tests/turn/pass4Unreached.test.ts): the D-209 summary on a double factual contradiction (both replies invent a severed arm: the summary is shown, says how the fight stands, and passes the check's factual rules); a failed saber recall (the block's "saber recall: FAILED", rule 14 flags "the saber leaps back into your grip" and passes "your focus falters and the hilt stays"); a hold passing a typed chain on in the game (step 1 DONE "holds ... the chain goes on", step 2 rolled, the block tells both; the engine case was already in balanceRows); /simulate 503 2 in a fight turn (two busy notes, the third try answers, one call on the meter, and the same dice, exchanges and rngState as the same turn with no 503); /simulate 503 4 (three retries on the first model, the answer from the next). The Vong's armor on the card is D-248 item 12.
- **D-251 A lost or prosthetic leg slows form strikes (Jared, 2026-11-19; amends D-245).** health.config LEG_FORM_PENALTY_SHARE 0.5 (design default): a lost leg's lightsaber-forms P is 0.5 x its Agility P, 0.175, x (1 - adaptation), with any aid (a cybernetic leg included; full adaptation clears it). It enters the duel level through the lasting penalty like a lost hand's (fighterEffectiveLevel). *Kept:* D-230's adaptation pace. Forms now count as "affected" by a leg, which would have made form practice full-rate adaptation training and put the forms on the routine's adaptation list; both read the leg's forms at LEG_FORM_SHARE as before (features/health adaptationTrained, selectInjuredSkills), and the D-230 test (fatigueLegFloor) is unchanged. Specs: progression-numbers §18 and health §5 amended. *Tests:* tests/engine/legPenalties.test.ts.
- **D-252 A lost leg's Endurance penalty cuts the fatigue meters' capacity (Jared, 2026-11-19).** progression selectors capacityEndurance: the Endurance the capacities read is x (1 - 0.15 x (1 - A)) for a lost leg, for the player (selectCapacities) and for an NPC fighter (build.ts), in and out of fights. Only the leg (CAPACITY_ENDURANCE_KINDS): burns or lung damage also has an Endurance P (0.4) and still does not reach the capacities; for Jared. Max Vitality still reads Endurance without lasting penalties. *Tests:* tests/engine/legPenalties.test.ts.
- **D-253 A prosthetic fitted at once keeps the dominant hand (Jared, 2026-11-19; amends Q39 and D-248 item 10).** The hand switches only when the dominant arm or hand is lost and not replaced (no aid or scar tissue; engine/lasting isFittedAid). Today health always fits the path's prosthetic at once (the stub), so the player's hand never switches; the switch and /heal all's restore (D-248) stay for a limb with no aid. Health §5 amended. *Tests:* tests/features/health.test.ts (the Q39 case now keeps the hand), tests/engine/legPenalties.test.ts, tests/turn/minorFixesPass4.test.ts.
- **D-251 to D-253 measured (2026-11-19).** *Sweep:* every one of its 197 result lines identical, the D-158 rows included (sweep fighters carry no earlier lost limb; a leg severed in a duel already ends it). *Lifetime (gated seed s7, every gate passes; old -> new, years from the profile's start):* Anakin 41 4.03 -> 3.74, 61 8.28 -> 7.87, 81 15.20 -> 14.39, 101 21.87 -> 20.31; war-year shares 31.1 / 26.5 / 29.0% -> 25.5 / 27.0 / 30.0% (mean 28.9 -> 27.5%, band 25-35%). Obi-Wan 81 39.35 -> 39.26, 101 59.49 -> 59.42; war shares 22.1 / 27.8 / 25.5% -> 23.6 / 24.4 / 24.8%. Luke 101 33.76 -> 33.69. The Average, the closed-form, swordmaster and archetype checks did not move. *Who loses a leg:* every gated profile, more than once: at 70 years Anakin carries both legs, both arms and the right hand lost (quality cybernetics, all fully adapted), Obi-Wan all four limbs and the right hand, Luke all four limbs (basic prosthetics, at their 80% cap). *What moves Anakin* (each rule toggled on his seed): with only D-253, 101 at 21.82 (no move: his hand never switched in the harness); without D-251, 22.26; without D-252, 21.71. Both leg rules make him faster, not slower: a penalty lowers his duel level, so each fight's gap to the opponent is larger and its combat XP higher, and the run's injuries and dice diverge from the old path from the first leg loss on. Not retuned. *For Jared:* the lifetime harness's duels take all four limbs from every profile over a life (it never stops a fight early or limits severs per year); for Obi-Wan (Legends: no lost limb) and Luke (one hand) that is far from the lore, and every lasting-injury rule now reaches the gated profiles through it.
- **D-254 Burns and lung damage cut the fatigue meters' capacity (Jared, 2026-11-19; extends D-252).** health.config CAPACITY_ENDURANCE_KINDS now lostLeg and burnsOrLungs: the Endurance the capacities read is x (1 - P x (1 - A)) for each (0.15 and 0.4), combined multiplicatively, for the player and NPC fighters, in and out of fights. Every lasting kind with an Endurance penalty is now on the list. *Tests:* tests/engine/legPenalties.test.ts.
- **D-255 Max Vitality uses the lasting Endurance penalty (Jared, 2026-11-19; design default).** engine/vitality maxVitalityWithLasting: max Vitality x (1 - the combined lasting Endurance penalty), each injury's P x (1 - adaptation) (a lost leg 0.15, burns or lung damage 0.4); the player's selectMaxVitality and every NPC fighter (build.ts). Spec: health §3 amended. *Tests:* tests/engine/legPenalties.test.ts. Measured with the morale rule (D-256 measurement entry).
- **D-256 Morale (Jared, 2026-11-19; design defaults).** *Rule* (engine/duel.ts resolveExchange, combat.config MORALE, MORALE_BY_ARCHETYPE, MORALE_BY_MINION_KIND): an NPC opponent, named or minion, checks morale when it takes a severe or worse wound in the exchange (a sever included), when it becomes Spent, and when the exchange puts half or more of its side out (every standing member of a group checks). A check breaks with its group's chance, early 0.45, average 0.20, late 0.06, never 0; a break is a surrender with the profile's share, else the fighter disengages and flees. Groups by archetype: civilian (militia, brawlers) early 0.4 surrender, smuggler and bountyHunter (thugs, mercenaries) early 0.3, soldier average 0.6, mandalorian late 0.3, the Jedi archetypes late 0.8 (they yield), the Sith archetypes late 0.2 (they run), yuuzhanVongWarrior never; by minion kind: the droid kinds never, beasts early and never surrender. A broken fighter is out of the fight (Fighter.yielded); the exchange's card says "X's morale breaks: surrenders / disengages and flees, out of the fight"; the last one breaking ends the fight won, reason yielded ("the opponents broke"); a named opponent's surrender opens Spare / Capture / Kill like an incapacitated one; one who fled is gone. The narration block has a "Morale:" line for each break and the status Surrendered or Fled. The player never checks; a spar has no morale; Force actions and thrown sabers do not trigger a check (only exchanges do). The dice continue the duel's stream (D-238). Dev /archetype now sets the fighter's morale too. *Not modelled:* morale from the player's reputation, a leader's presence, or the stakes; willpower does not change the chance. *Tests:* tests/engine/morale.test.ts.
- **D-256 measured (2026-11-19).** *Sweep* (9,840 fights, no issues; D-158 rows, Knight at 50, old -> new win / wounded / exchanges): 10 militia balanced 100 / 0.7 / 18.4 -> 100 / 0.3 / 14.5, mixed exchanges 43.0 -> 36.9, heavyForce 68.7 -> 68.3%; 6 troopers balanced 99.7 / 5.0 / 16.5 -> 99.7 / 6.3 / 15.0, heavyForce 86.0 -> 85.3%; 6 elites balanced 97.0 / 23.3 / 25.1 -> 96.7 / 22.3 / 22.8; 4 Mandalorians balanced 97.3 / 20.7 / 46.5 -> 97.3 / 17.0 / 44.9; troopers in cover 17.4 -> 15.7 exchanges; the droid rows (10 B1, 6 B2, 4 droidekas) unchanged (droids never break). The smuggler at 30 against 4 line troopers: flat 64.3 / 81.7% -> 71.7 / 72.0% (won / wounded), cover 77.3 / 69.0% -> 81.3 / 66.7%. *Duel tables* (rolls §8, tests/lifetime/duelTables.test.ts): unchanged and passing; their fighters carry no morale.
