    - Darth Karrid: born 3,684; Gnost-Dural's Padawan (Jedi) 3,671-3,658, so a 23-year-old Padawan in 3,661; Malgus's apprentice from 3,657; a Darth from 3,649.
    - Yuon Par and Aryn Leneer: born 3,686, Knights in 3,661 (Yuon a Master by 3,643; Aryn still a Knight in 3,653).
    - Warren Sedoru: born 3,711 ("several decades before 3,681"), a Knight from 3,686 and a Master from 3,642.
    - Harkun: an Overseer (role official, not a Lord) from c. 3,655, so not in post in 3,661.
    - Parkanas Tark: born 3,681; the youngest of the Malachor III expedition (undated, read as c. 3,665) at 16; missing there 3,665-3,644, Lord Vivicar from 3,663.
    - Tremel: born 3,716 (an Overseer from 3,681 at 35; his son an acolyte by 3,643).
    - Darth Ikoral: a Darth, not an acolyte (born 3,757: a long-lived Pureblood leading an expedition c. 3,712).
    - Oteg: 183 in 3,661, young for Yoda's species; kept.
    - Braden 3,700 and Jicoln Cadera 3,695 (older). Fideltin Rusk 3,680 (lore: the planets file).
    - Khem Val: a Dashade warrior (role other, archetype bountyHunter), in stasis c. 5,000-3,644 until revived for Nox. Broonmark: a Talz warrior (role other, archetype bountyHunter), Republic then Empire.
    - Galena Rans and Rans are two people: she married the Supreme Commander (different species and sex in the lore), so they are not merged.
  - *[6] Status corrections from the lore:* Nico Okarr in stasis 3,681-3,632 (missing and unageing; he was on the board before); Ngani Zho missing 3,652-3,644 (vanished after 3,653); Wyellett captured 3,665-3,643; Darth Ekkage imprisoned on Belsavis 3,642-3,641; Tasiele Shan exiled from c. 3,698 (stays on the board); the Dread Masters imprisoned 3,668-3,642 and free in 3,641 (the old unavailable span ran to 3,641).
  - *[7] The engine (src/engine/canonRoster.ts):* `canonRankAt`, `canonRoleAt`, `canonFactionAt`, `canonStatusAt`, `canonLocationsAt`, `canonLivedAgeAt` (calendar age less stasis years) and `canonOffBoard` all query the spans at the date. "Present in a role" (`canonAbsence`) is now: holds a rank span that year, old enough for it. The link refusal quotes the status span's note for any year (it quoted the 3,661 note only in 3,661).
  - *[8] Interim stats:* the hand-set sheets are complete anchors (`anchors`), and figures with no sheet have `anchors: []`; until stage 3 (D-414) the stats behave as before.
  - *[9] Tests:* tests/data/canonTimeline.test.ts (rank ages, birth bases, offices, the §4 corrections, the board at 3,681, 3,667, 3,661, 3,653, 3,643, 3,640 and 3,630).

- **D-413 Date-agnostic refactor, stage 2: date-keyed starts, keyframes and the 3,661 audit (brief §1).**
  - *[1] Start options (src/data/startOptions.ts):* the 17 starts are kept as they were, each now valid for the years where its setting holds:
    - the Coruscant Temple, the Minos Cluster front, the Coruscant lower levels and the Republic pilot: 3,681-3,654 (the war, before the Sacking);
    - the Republic trooper: 3,665-3,654 (the Geonosis front, after Alderaan);
    - the Korriban acolyte, Imperial-born, slave-born, Nar Shaddaa (smuggler, orphan, mechanic), SIS and the three Imperial starts: 3,681-3,642 (through the Cold War);
    - the nomad Mandalorian: 3,667-3,660 (while the Lesser leads the clans);
    - the Mandalorian blockade picket: 3,661 only.
    Threads, place notes and premises are dated. A thread is seeded only in its years (the blockade, the Council's strike, picket duty, the riots and the Shange operation 3,661; the Rim Campaign 3,661-3,660; the Geonosis campaign 3,665-3,653; the Minos front 3,681-3,654; the clans who refused and handling the Mandalorians 3,667-3,660). A place's description is a timeless line plus dated notes (Coruscant "starving under the Hydian Way blockade" only in 3,661). Each start keeps its 3,661 premise as the 3,661 version, with a plain premise for its other years (invented wording, setting only). The form offers starts, threads and the premise by the picked date. Refusal messages name the picked year instead of "3,661 BBY". *Reversible default:* a start whose threads are all 3,661 events has no opening thread in other years.
  - *[2] Spec 14's galaxy sim seeds* are recorded as a keyframe at 3,661 (14-world-state-and-events.md): other start dates get their own keyframes later, and a campaign starts from the latest keyframe at or before its date.
  - *[3] The galaxy map's planet and route control* (`control3661`, `change3653`) are dated keyframes (`control: { bby, text }[]`), with `controlOnRecordAt` returning the last statement on record at or before a date (scripts/gen-galaxy-map.mjs; galaxyMap.generated.ts regenerated, nothing else changed).
  - *[4] Audit for baked-in 3,661 assumptions:*
    - Already dated spans, unchanged: control and leaders, the Order seat, world gates, species gates, era rules, travel, the Pureblood war years (3,681-3,653).
    - Fixed: the imperial-forces era rule said "for 3,661" (now "in these years"); the start refusal messages; the start places, threads and premises; the map's control fields.

- **D-414 Date-agnostic refactor, stage 3: the canon NPC stat model (brief §2; src/engine/canonStats.ts, pure).** Replaces D-152/D-155's single-sheet scaling (`canonScale`, removed).
  - *[1] Anchors:* a figure's stats rest on dated anchors (`anchors`). A complete anchor sets every stat (the 45 hand-set sheets, each at its sheet year); a partial anchor sets only the stats a source shows. Each anchor records its year and sources; its age is the year minus the birth year.
  - *[2] Any other date:* each stat uses its nearest relevant anchor and is carried along the figure's growth curve G (the closed-form main level at its lived age). Between two anchors for the same stat, the stat's ratio to G is interpolated in growth space, so the curve passes through both. Going backward from an anchor, a stat is clamped at the archetype's baseline for that age (the generator's line). A stat no anchor shows takes the generator's line. *Proposed formula (rule 7):* this is new maths, not a legacy formula; it reduces to the old scaling for a figure with one complete anchor.
  - *[3] Rank spans shape the curve:* G and the generator line use the figure's rank at the date and the ages its Padawan and Knight phases start from its rank spans (creation's new `phases` input; for the Sith, apprentice is the Padawan phase and Lord the Knight phase, with the Padawan phase 6 years before the Knight phase when no apprentice span is on record, as in phaseAges).
  - *[4] Dated events* (`events`: injury, lost limb, fall, redemption) multiply the stats they name from their date on; an anchor after an event already includes it. None are entered yet (stage 4 lists candidates).
  - *[5] Stasis:* lived age leaves out stasis years, so a figure neither ages nor grows in stasis (Khem Val, Nico Okarr).
  - *[6] No anchor:* the generator builds the complete line from archetype and rank at the date (a variance seeded by the canon ID, as before).
  - *[7] When a campaign starts:* each figure's line is calculated once at the start date (a new optional `clock.startDay`, set at creation). From then on it grows by the normal maths, the closed-form growth along its rank spans, and later anchors no longer pull it, so the story can change its course. *Reversible default:* the growth after the start follows the figure's canon rank spans (its promotions); the alternative is to freeze its rank at the start. A figure not yet in training at the start is calculated when their training starts. An old save without a start day uses today (the pure anchored curve). `fixed` figures keep their sheet.
  - *[8] Tests:* tests/engine/canonStats.test.ts (three ages, a partial anchor with Force stats from one source and saber stats from the complete line, interpolation through two anchors, the backward clamp, stasis, events, fixed figures, the campaign start) and tests/features/canonLevels.test.ts (Satele at 3,681, 3,661 and 3,643, the rank shape, a generated line through Yuon Par's ranks, Vitiate fixed).

- **D-415 Date-agnostic refactor, stage 4: the works table, appearances and hand-set anchors (brief §3, §4).**
  - *[1] Works (src/data/canon/works.ts):* 29 era works with in-universe dates: the cinematics Return (3,681), Hope (3,667) and Deceived (3,653); the novels Deceived (3,653), Fatal Alliance (3,643), Annihilation (3,640) and Revan; the comics Threat of Peace, Blood of the Empire (3,678) and The Lost Suns; SWTOR and its eight class stories (3,643-3,640), the Maelstrom Prison and Foundry flashpoints (3,642), Ilum (3,640), Rise of the Hutt Cartel, Shadow of Revan (3,637), KOTFE, KOTET, Onslaught, Legacy of the Sith; the Encyclopedia. The lore files name the works but don't date them, so each year is the year the timeline slice or the character files give for the events the work shows, cited per work.
    Marked undated (`dated: false`): Revan (before the era), Threat of Peace (its end year), The Lost Suns, Rise of the Hutt Cartel, the Encyclopedia.
  - *[2] Appearances (works.ts `APPEARANCES`):* for the hand-set figures (Satele, Malcom, Malgus, Ven Zallow, Zym, Ngani Zho, Angral, Rans, Karagga, Spindrall, Tremel, Oteg, Shol Bestros, Jerbhen Hulis, Jaric Kaedan): the works each appears in and what each shows, with the gaps for the lore chat or the research bot.
  - *[3] Anchors:* the hand-set sheets of the priority figures now cite their works (Satele: SWTOR and Fatal Alliance, 3,643; Malgus and Zallow: Deceived, 3,653; Angral: the Jedi Knight story, 3,643; and so on). Five figures without a sheet get a **draft** complete anchor for review (`basis: 'draft'`): the generator's line at a year a work shows them (Spindrall and Tremel 3,643, Rans 3,653, Shol Bestros 3,643, Jerbhen Hulis 3,678).
    Not drafted, for a hand-set line: Karagga (the generator gives no line for an 800-year-old Hutt); Oteg (the generator overshoots for a 183-year-old of Yoda's species: willpower 199, Soresu 156, past the roster's apex of about 135); Overseer Ragate (not in a roster: he needs a lore index entry first); the rest of the Jedi Council (Bela Kiwiiks, Gnost-Dural and Kaedan have sheets; Oteg, Bestros and Hulis as above).
  - *[4] One dated event in the data:* Warren Sedoru's war injuries (Force stats × 0.8 from 3,654; the year and the factor are invented and tagged).
  - *[5] Shape fix:* any adult role a Force-user holds after training (an Overseer, an agent) counts as the Knight phase for the curve (Tremel's Overseer years had fallen back to acolyte growth).
  - *[6] Tests:* tests/data/works.test.ts.

- **D-416 Date-agnostic refactor wrapped up (brief §5; docs-v2/reports/date-agnostic-refactor.md).**

- **D-417 Rank ages: the average Knight at about 25 (Jared, 2026-11-22; calibration pass item 1; backup in docs-v2/reference/calibration-baseline-2026-11-22/, tag `calibration-baseline-2026-11-22`).**
  - *[1] Canon estimates (src/data/canon/rankAges.ts):* the Jedi ages already matched (Padawan 13, Knight 25 after 12 years as a Padawan, Master 40, High Council 45, Grand Master 55) and are unchanged. The Sith ladder now mirrors them: Lord 22 → 25, Darth 35 → 40 (acolyte 15, apprentice 20, Dark Councilor 45 unchanged). Careers unchanged.
  - *[2] Births re-derived:* an estimated birth that rested on a Sith Lord or Darth year with no stated age moves, so the dated rank years stay where the lore puts them (27 figures, 3 or 5 years older): Scourge, the Dread Masters, Angral, Jadus, Vindican, Zash, Mekhis, Acina, Exal Kressh, the Emperor's Wrath, Darth Nox, Raptus, Bestia, Brontes, Calphayus, Styrak, Tyrans, Tarnis, Lachris, Calypho, Skotia, Vich, Charnus, Serevin, Tormen, Spindrall, Grathan; each `birthBasis` says so. A birth resting on another fact (a Dark Councilor at 45, a stated age, "older than") stays; so do lore births. Those figures' estimated Lord and Darth spans start at the new ages instead (35 spans), never past a lore-dated span.
  - *[3] The player ladder is not changed (report first):* the Average profile knights at 20.5 (`AVERAGE_RANK_AGES`, the Knight `typicalAge`, the generator's `RANK_AGES`), and it meets the Knight minimums at 20.3. Findings and options are in docs-v2/reports/calibration-2026-11-22.md §1.
  - *[4] Thanaton (a Lord at 17) and the Barsen'thor (a Master at 28) stay as on record (item 7): the lore beats the role minimums.*

- **D-418 Special training conditions and the accelerated path (Jared, 2026-11-22; item 1; progression-numbers §21).**
  - *[1] The conditions:* an exceptional teacher (the existing teacher factor, capped at x1.15, plus aptitude and bond); a Force-rich place (new: Force skills train x1.10 at a site of force-reserve §9, `FORCE_RICH_SITES`: Tython, Ilum, the Jedi Temple, Korriban, Dromund Kaas, Malachor V, Dagobah; inside the 2.8 M cap and the 4.0x clamp; invented); the accelerated path.
  - *[2] The accelerated path (`character.trainingPath`, event `trainingPathSet`):* training cut to using and channelling the Force and blade work; the Order's knowledge blocks (Force Lore) leave the routine, so knowledge and doctrine stay low until later study. Force skills and lightsaber forms train x`ACCELERATED_PATH.mult` on top of the M cap, and their 4.0x clamp is raised x`capMult`. It is the only condition allowed past the 4.0x ceiling; the 4.0x calibration and the averages are untouched.
  - *[3] Numbers (invented, for Jared to adjust):* mult 3.2, capMult 3.2. First guess 1.6 / 2.0 gave Force Control 37.9 and Djem So 29.3 after 4 years. Tuned on the 20-seed spread of the new harness check (tests/lifetime/lukeAccelerated.test.ts: Luke, accelerated path, master stub as the exceptional teacher, on Dagobah; Bespin and Death Star duels and sparring with his teacher added): at 3.0, Force Control median 52.1 and Djem So 47.1 at 4 years; at 3.2, 54.4 and 48.8 at 4 years and 62.9 and 56.3 at 5. The gain saturates near x3 (x3.2 adds about 2 levels), and the blade lags because the drill ceiling holds it. The check gates 55 in both by year 5, past the 4.0x floor, and knowledge and meditation under 25 at year 4. An outlier, not calibration data.

- **D-419 Canon promotions after the start (Jared, 2026-11-22; item 2).** After the campaign start a figure keeps its canon promotions (D-414 [7]'s default), unless the campaign has diverged for it: killed (recorded by `npcDied`), captured (the fate choice), turned or removed from its post (event `npcCanonDiverged`, from the narrator or the engine; one-way, the first divergence stands; refused for a story NPC). From the divergence day the figure's growth follows only the rank spans begun by then (a Padawan held back keeps the Padawan course), and the lore's later whereabouts stop applying (D-420). Stored as `npc.canonDiverged { day, reason }`.

- **D-420 What the narrator sees of the board (Jared, 2026-11-22; item 3).** The state block sends the on-record whereabouts of the figures at the current place and of any figure named this turn; never a full list. *At the place:* every location span that names it (not only the D-401 placements), plus the year's dated status facts whose wording names it (Satele "recalled to Coruscant over the Hydian blockade" in 3,661). *Named:* figures in focus (referenced or present NPCs linked to canon) and roster figures whose lore alias appears in the player's words, at most 6: their location spans and status facts this year, or why they are off the board. Figures the campaign has diverged from are left out.

Hand set: **Karagga** (an 800+ Hutt crime lord: strength 110, endurance 120, agility 14, melee 22, willpower 86, intelligence 84, presence 82; `fixed`: the long plateau of a 1,000-year life); **Oteg** (species corrected to Yoda's species from the lore file, which the roster had as "other"; placed among the Council Master sheets, Force stats 78-84, agility 78, strength 40, tactics 72 for the Fleet Admiral); **Ragate** (a lore index entry added to the era 09 master index and Part2 staff table from the Korriban files; she is female in the lore; a roster entry: Overseer from 3,681, killed 3,637, born c. 3,718 (elderly by 3,643); an old Overseer skilled in precognition: Force Sense 84, physical 30-36; her placement now comes from the roster).

On record: Rans at the Sacking (3,653, his page), Stantorrs c. 3,643, Rans after him to Ilum (3,642-3,640), Malcom from 3,640. 3,661 and the other unrecorded years are blank in the leaders table. Rans is born 3,703 (Supreme Commander at 50 in 3,653).

Nico Okarr: the lore file (Part3a) dates his disappearance to 3,681, after he helped Satele and Malcom escape Korriban; he is on the board in 3,681 (a location fact: captured on Korriban) and off from 3,680 to 3,632. His archetype is now smuggler (was civilian).

- **D-422 The warrior archetype (Jared, 2026-11-22; item 8).** `warrior`: non-Force melee (Mandalorian blade-fighters, Talz, Dashade, arena gladiators); NPCs only for now. Weights melee 1.0, strength and endurance 0.8, agility 0.6, tactics 0.3, willpower 0.2; the Yuuzhan Vong warrior's measured paces (no new pace invented); start 14 (10-20); loadout melee and light armor; morale late, surrender 0.2. Khem Val and Broonmark move to it. Mandalorians checked: the lore files give a fighting style only for Mandalore the Lesser (an arena gladiator), who moves to warrior; the others stay `mandalorian` (blaster main, melee second). A Talz or Dashade in a fighting role is generated as a warrior.

- **D-423 The stat scale: the game's progression is the reference (Jared, 2026-11-22; calibration pass item A; report: docs-v2/reports/calibration-2026-11-22.md §3-4).**
  - *[1] Bands:* each stat has a short band definition (what 40 / 60 / 80 / 100 / 120 mean), from the calibration profiles and the closed-form lines by archetype and age (skills-and-activities.md §7).
  - *[2] The hand-set sheets are converted into the game scale at load (src/data/canon/sheetScale.ts; `CANON_NPCS`), stat by stat:* the factor is the median, over the 43 non-fixed hand-set sheets (31 for the Force stats), of the game's progression line for that figure (archetype, rank spans, lived age at the sheet's date, gifted talent: a notable figure) over the sheet value, capped at 1.0 so nothing is inflated. Forms 0.83 (one factor for every form), Force abilities 0.53, Force Control 0.64, Force Sense 0.52, Force Output 0.75, Meditation 0.24, Force Knowledge 0.28, Willpower 1.0 (measured 1.02), Strength 0.68, Agility 0.63, Endurance 0.68, Intelligence 0.20, Charisma 0.27, Blaster and Stealth 0.60, Melee 0.24, Tactics 0.48. The authored numbers stay in the roster files (an anchor's `authored`); an anchor marked `scale: 'game'` is not converted. The generator is not tuned to the sheets.
  - *[3] Rank-preserving equating was tried and rejected:* the sheets are bunched (most values 76-88 whatever the figure's focus) while the game spreads them by archetype and age, so a one-point sheet difference became up to 20 game levels.
  - *[4] Sith forms:* the Sith no longer default to Soresu. Default Padawan form plans by archetype (`DEFAULT_FORM_PLAN_BY_ARCHETYPE`): Sith Warrior Djem So 2 h + Shien 1 h (the leading forms of 6 of the 7 Warrior sheets), Sith Inquisitor Makashi 2 h + Niman 1 h (Makashi leads the Inquisitor sheets). The generator and canon lines use them; the creation form resets the plan when the archetype changes. Jedi keep Soresu 2 h + Ataru 1 h.
  - *[5] XP sources:* the diagnosis and the proposed new sources are in the report, held for review; nothing is added to the numbers.


- **D-424 M7 §15, ranks and milestones: the core (architecture §15; ranks.md §1-5; flag-registry.md §2-3).** Built on the D-412 rank spans and the D-417 rank ages; the player ladder's ages stay as they are until Jared decides (report §1a), and every age, minimum and chance is data (src/data/rankMinimums.ts, src/data/ranks.config.ts), so that decision changes data only.
  - *[1] The ranks slice v2:* the rank and the day it was granted, the master (an NPC set by the player or the engine, never the AI), trial records, the Service Corps assignment, Padawans knighted, and a pending third-failure choice. v1 saves migrate with no history.
  - *[2] Promotions (rules.ts, selectors.ts; pure):* `promotionProposed` (the AI or the player) is checked by `selectPromotionCheck` and applied only when every requirement holds; otherwise rejected and logged with the unmet ones (tests 6-7). Padawan and apprentice come only from being chosen; Knight needs the five trials (Courage and Flesh credited by a wartime heroic act) or the Council bypass, plus the minimums; Master needs the minimums and a Padawan knighted or a deed; Sith Lord needs the minimums and a deed. High Council, Grand Master and Darth stay story (the seat's skill and standing parts are a selector).
  - *[3] Trials (`trialAttempted`, the player):* eligibility (the Initiate Trials from 10 with the minimums; the Knight Trials after 6 Padawan years, 18+, the master's approval and the minimums), the retake wait (a year), three failures and the choice (`trialFailureChoice`: the Service Corps, remain a Padawan in service, or leave the Order). Each check is d20 + level / 10 against DC 10 + rating / 10 (Spirit: 10 + dark pressure / 5 + 4; Flesh at f 0.8); results go to the engine trial flags.
  - *[4] Standing and selection:* S = 50% skill percentile (against a seeded population of 1,000 NPC Initiates of the same age, cached per month) + 30% deeds (20 points a deed flag, to 100; design default) + 20% relationships with Knights and Masters. Monthly (the first of the month): S / 940 to be chosen after the Initiate Trials; the Council of Reassignment at 13; a Knight's interest in the Corps at 2% a month; the master's approval (`masterApproval`) and the Council bypass (`councilBypassGranted`, with a notice) when all their conditions hold.
  - *[5] Not in this core (the rest of M7, m7-start-checklist.md):* the Order schedule revisit (D-165), named masters' instruction (D-124), the Council path and wartime postings (D-356, D-357), forbidden powers and reputation (D-346), the teaching XP share (D-163), Sith Force growth, the design candidates, and the UI for trials and choices (events only for now). Pace-for-age milestones (ranks.md §4) are not built.

- **D-425 Rulings on the calibration report (Jared, 2026-11-22; docs-v2/reports/calibration-2026-11-22.md questions 3-7 and 12).**
  - *[3] Force-rich places:* the x1.10 stays (invented). Lore basis: on a Force-rich world you feel the Force more strongly, so you progress faster while using it there (Legends: the Je'daii formed on Tython, the Sith train on Korriban, Dagobah is Force-rich because it teems with life). Nexus data at two levels (src/data/forceNexus.ts): planet-level (Tython, Korriban, Byss from 19 BBY, Dromund Kaas, Malachor V, Vjun from 29 BBY) and site-level (the Valley of the Dark Lords, stronger than Korriban around it; the Jedi Temple's spire; the Valley of the Jedi; the cave on Dagobah), each with its leaning and lore source. Dagobah is a Force-rich world without a planet-level nexus. The bonus applies on Force-rich worlds and nexus planets only; sites are data. Dagobah, its cave, Ilum and the Temple's leaning are in the research queue. Open questions: a larger bonus at sites, stacking a site with its planet, matching the bonus to the leaning.
  - *[4] Rans:* in office at the Sacking (3,653) as recorded; the conflict with Stantorrs c. 3,643 is noted on the span.
  - *[5] Nico Okarr:* 3,681 kept.
  - *[6] Torian Cadera and Akaavi Spar* are warriors, tagged "SWTOR portrayal".
  - *[7] The warrior archetype* is offered at character creation (the career path).
  - *[12] Deeds:* 20 standing points a deed, a starting value, invented.

The closed form's phases follow (a Knight NPC was a Padawan until 25).
  - *[2] The Knight minimums re-read by the D-51 rule at 24 (the Average's levels on the gated seed, rounded down):* highest form 39 → 47 (47.4), Force Control 34 → 40 (40.2), Force Sense 31 → 36 (37.0), Willpower 53 → 56 (56.2). The Sith Lord row mirrors them; the Master row keeps main specialty 58 (now met at 33.7) and takes the new Knight minimums.
  - *[3] The calibration holds:* Adept 41 at 16.05 years (gate 16.0 +/- 0.1); 61 at 32.6, 81 at 54.1, 101 at 81.0 (all within +/-20%).
  - *[4] The 4.0x ceiling is not recalibrated:* `Y_AVG` stays as calibrated. The Average's own yearly XP now runs higher at 20-30 (20-seed medians 601 / 545 / 424 against the table's 550 / 450 / 396, the Padawan's master sessions to 25), so the Y_avg sanity checks skip those ages (`Y_AVG_FROZEN_AGES`).

- **D-427 XP sources for the knowledge stats (Jared, 2026-11-22; calibration report question 10; every number invented and tagged).**
  - *[1] Daily-life blocks (DAILY_LIFE), outside the D-25 training budget so the forms calibration is untouched:* a Knight's study expectation (1 h of the Jedi Code, or Force lore for the Sith, on 3 days of 7, from Knight or Lord; dropped on the accelerated path). A daily meditation block is built but **off** (no rows): every scheduled meditation centers the next day (x1.05, D-133), so a daily block centers Padawans and Knights every day, which moved the K calibration (41 at 15.76 years) and the closed form (Guardian at 60 years 86.2 against 83.5). It needs the archetype paces (and K) re-measured first; held for Jared.
  - *[2] Study of texts (trainingRecords.ts):* the full Jedi Archives (Force Knowledge 75; only in the Temple or on Tython), the Korriban Sith Library (70; only on Korriban), a Jedi Master's journal, Obi-Wan Kenobi's journal (from 19 BBY), a gatekeeper holocron (85), a Sith Lord's holocron. Records gain `places`; journals are trusted like holocrons until items exist (M8).
  - *[3] Teaching and mentoring:* `apprenticeTaken` (a Knight or Master of 25+) and `apprenticeKnighted` (counts toward Master); teaching a Padawan opens and gives Force Knowledge, Willpower, Charisma and a review share in the main form.
  - *[4] Learning by dueling a stronger opponent (STRONGER_DUEL):* a serious or lethal fight against an opponent 10+ levels stronger gives 0.25 of the fight's form XP to the opponent's form, even unknown (it opens without a teacher); x2 on the accelerated path (question 2). Combat and the lifetime harness pass the opponent's form; no calibration profile fights 10+ stronger, so their numbers don't move.
  - *[5] Switching off the accelerated path:* `trainingPathSet` (D-418) back to standard brings back the knowledge blocks.
  - *[6] Natural Intelligence and Charisma by age* (4 / 10 / 16 / 20 / 30 / 60: Intelligence 4 / 8 / 12 / 14 / 16 / 18, Charisma 3 / 6 / 9 / 11 / 13 / 15), like Willpower's; NPCs get it too.
  - *[7] NPC closed form:* the daily-life paces are added for Order members, measured on the day loop (DAILY_LIFE_PACES: Force Knowledge from Knight 0.139; the Consular 0.189).
  - *[8] Before and after (the knowledge report, tests/lifetime/knowledge.test.ts; the Average Guardian and Consular through the real game, Knight at 25):* Guardian Force Knowledge 13.0 / 13.0 / 13.0 → 13.0 / 19.9 / 27.9 at 25 / 40 / 60, Meditation 11.9 / 15.8 / 20.5 → 11.9 / 16.6 / 22.0; Consular Force Knowledge 14.3 / 54.5 / 88.3 → 14.3 / 57.4 / 93.5, Meditation 13.0 / 27.1 / 40.6 → 13.0 / 27.6 / 41.7; Intelligence and Charisma 0 → 15.0 / 16.7 / 18.0 and 12.0 / 13.7 / 15.0. (With the meditation block on, Guardian Meditation was 15.2 / 22.8 / 31.4 and Consular 16.2 / 33.5 / 50.7.)
  - *[9] Officer archetype with command and staff work:* to the M8 checklist.
  - *[10] Tests:* tests/m7/xpSources.test.ts; the closed-form test now reads the Average's rank ages (D-426); the 3-month montage test takes a seed with no world event (the new blocks shifted the RNG).

- **D-428 The Sith required-training rows (Jared, 2026-11-22; question 11).** Own rows (`acolyte`, `apprentice`) mirroring the Jedi time structure (4 h and 5 h), with the Korriban content (lore batch 12; SW_Korriban_Academy_Part1 curriculum): acolytes 2 h martial training with the warblade (Form I basics; the lightsaber comes with apprenticeship), 0.5 h Force Control and 0.5 h in the meditation chambers with the Overseers, 1 h the Sith Code and history; apprentices 2 h forms with the master, 1 h solo, 1 h Force Control and 1 h Force awareness with the master. A channeling (Force Output) block for apprentices put the Inquisitor's day loop 8 levels past its closed form (51.3 against 43.3); it waits for re-measured paces. The acolyte's meditation-chamber half hour centers acolytes (the Sith Warrior day loop stays within 2 of its closed form).

- **D-429 The scale mapping revised; long lives capped (Jared, 2026-11-22; calibration report questions 8-9; src/data/canon/sheetScale.ts).**
  - *[1] One factor per archetype group* (Jedi: Guardian, Consular, Sentinel; Sith: Warrior, Inquisitor; careers) replaces the one overall factor: the median of game line over sheet within the group, capped at 1.0. Jedi: highest form 0.90, Force Control 0.71, Force Sense 0.73, physical 0.63-0.72, Willpower 1.0 (1.38 measured). Sith: highest form 0.80, Force Control 0.57, Force Sense 0.46, Force Output 0.75, physical 0.60-0.62, Willpower 1.0 (1.12). Careers: physical 0.67-0.72, Willpower 0.77, Blaster 0.60, Melee 0.24. Charisma 0.18-0.19; Force abilities 0.50-0.51.
  - *[2] Force Knowledge, Meditation, Intelligence and Tactics from the bands:* the figure's standing at the sheet's date sets the band (40 a Knight, Lord or officer; 60 a Master, Darth, general, Overseer or senator; 80 the Councils and highest offices; 100 an ancient `fixed` figure), one band lower outside the focus (study: Consulars and Inquisitors; Intelligence also civilians and smugglers; Tactics soldiers and Mandalorians), never above the authored value. Re-measured once the XP sources exist.
  - *[3] Hand-set game-scale lines* for Vitiate, Scourge, Malgus, Marr, the Dread Masters, Satele, Revan and the Consular-type Masters (Zym, Dar'Nala, Ngani Zho, Tol Braga, Gnost-Dural, the Barsen'thor), on the bands (80 the elite of an era, 100 legendary, 120 apex), over the converted sheet. Examples: Vitiate Force Output 125, makashi 100; Revan Niman 105; Satele Force Sense 88; Malgus Djem So 88; Zym Force Knowledge 110.
  - *[4] Long lives (LONG_LIFE):* in the NPC closed form the Human-equivalent age counts at most a full life (100, through the species' own maturity and lifespan), and levels above 110 gain with diminishing returns toward 135. Cause found: the species table gives Kel Dor a lifespan of 70 (a game stat, "venerable 70+"), so Zym at 120 read as a Human-equivalent 179 (Force Knowledge 236). Oteg's overshoot was his species, corrected to Yoda's in D-421.

- **D-430 M7 §15 continued (Jared, 2026-11-22): milestones, the Council seat, the Order card; findings for the rest.**
  - *[1] Pace-for-age milestones (ranks.md §4; PACE_MILESTONES):* pace for age = the main specialty over the Average's at the same training years (the closed form for the character's archetype, rank and start age, no talent). Checked monthly after 3 years of training: "noticed" at 1.5, "prodigy talk" at 2.0, "ahead of their time" when the next rank's minimums are met 3+ years before its typical age. Each fires once (`paceMilestoneReached`) and queues a milestone beat the narrator sees (`milestoneBeatQueued`).
  - *[2] The High Council seat (`councilSeatProposed`, the narrator or the player, on a story vacancy):* checked against Master rank, main specialty 70 and standing 75; the seat is recorded on the ranks slice. Wartime postings and the Council's other roles (D-356, D-357) stay story for now.
  - *[3] The skill percentile is rank-matched:* a Padawan is ranked among NPC Padawans of the same age (it compared against Initiates before), an Initiate among Initiates.
  - *[4] Deeds to Knight (report):* through the trials, no deed is needed (Knight needs the trials, the master's approval and the minimums). Through the Council bypass (standing 75), the Average Padawan (15th-26th percentile among Padawans at 18-24) cannot reach it with any number of deeds: at most 57-63 with 5 deeds (the cap: 20 points each, to 100) and perfect relationships. It takes a top-half Padawan with 5 deeds and relationships near +100, or a top-quarter one with 5 deeds and +40.
  - *[5] The Order card (the Character tab):* rank, standing, the master and Padawan (pick lists), the trials with Attempt buttons (disabled with the reason), and the third-failure choices; every button sends a player event the engine checks. The AI contract lists promotionProposed and councilSeatProposed.
  - *[6] Not built, with findings:* the Order schedule revisit: a Padawan's fixed blocks alone end the day at f 0.99-1.00 at 12.5-13, 0.96 at 14 and 0.92 at 16 (over the standard limit 0.9; the fill is trimmed to nothing), fitting from about 20; scaling the Padawan's fixed hours with age moves the Padawan-years XP and the calibration, so it waits for a decision. Forbidden powers and Order reputation (D-346) need the M6 catalog in code. The instructor's drill-XP share (D-163) is a one-line note with no rule yet.

- **D-431 The re-measurement pass (Jared, 2026-11-22; answers [1], [2], [3], [6] to the calibration report).** Baseline saved first: tag `calibration-baseline-2026-11-22b` (commit 8bcaa5f, pushed) and docs-v2/reference/calibration-baseline-2026-11-22b/ (the first baseline untouched).
  - *[1] The daily meditation block is on* for Padawans and Knights (and Sith apprentices and Lords, on the same rows): 0.5 h basic centering every training day, outside the budget; centered stays as is (D-133), so Order members from Padawan on are centered every training day.
  - *[2] The Sith apprentice's channelling block is back:* 1 h Force Output with the master in place of the awareness hour.
  - *[6] The Padawan's fixed hours scale with age* (and the apprentice's): the solo form hour and the Force Control hour are 0.5 h to 16; Force Control is back to 1 h at 17 and the solo hour at 18 (4 h, 4.5 h, 5 h). Tuned on the Average's day (2026-11-22): the fixed blocks end a standard day at f 0.86-0.89 with 0.5-1.5 h of free training from 12 to 17 (5 h ended it at 0.92-1.01 to 16, with none). Jared's example (4 h to 14, 5 h from 16) still ended 16-16.5 at 0.92, so the full hours return at 17-18. Creation's fill factor reads the same table.
  - *K re-solved on the real day loop* (npm run ksolve, the gated seed): 41 at 15.22 years with K 11.683 after the three changes; K = 12.30 puts it at 15.995 (20 seeds: median 15.99, 18 of 20 within 16.0 +/- 0.1).
  - *The archetype paces re-measured on the day loop* (new harness tests/paces, npm run paces; 12 archetypes, 60 years each, about 210 s): trained XP per phase over R x the age integral, with the Average's rank ages (Padawan 12, Knight 25); the form sources (the Initiate class, the master session, the solo hour, the fill with fights) are scaled so the closed form reproduces the main form's own gain: the closed form shares them out by the plan, while on the loop the main form also takes the fights' XP and the drills spill into the other forms (95 XP each over the Initiate years). A first cut that scaled to the plan forms' total left the closed form 6.2 levels under the loop at 60 years (the closed-form test caught it). They replace the docs-v2/calc model, which has no daily-life blocks, scaled hours or Sith rows. DAILY_LIFE_PACES (D-427) is gone: the blocks are in the measured paces. npc test 7 moves to Knight at 25. The Yuuzhan Vong warrior's loop Force Control (0.005, drills' secondaries) is left out (D-192). *Finding:* the career archetypes' Willpower paces measure 0.18-0.30 on the loop, against 0.83-1.00 in the calc model: the closed form had put career NPCs' Willpower about three times too high (npc test 7 checks only the main specialty).
  - *[3] The ceiling's yearly-XP table re-measured* for the Knight-at-25 Average: the table times the 20-seed median ratio per age; the frozen ages of D-426 are gone. 12: 557 -> 599; 16: 531 -> 604; 20: 550 -> 620; 24: 450 -> 560; 30: 396 -> 444; 90: 289 -> 324; the rest within 4%.
  - *Obi-Wan's Padawan form plan* (a profile input): Shii-Cho 0.5 h (was 1 h). The halved solo hour stalled his second form, which had carried his 41 (12.50 y), and with the higher K 41 came at 13.07 (gate 12.96); now 12.76 on the gated seed.
  - *The Knight minimums re-read* by the D-51 rule at 24 (D-426): the Average now has highest form 47.4, Force Control 38.1 (the halved Force Control hour), Force Sense 37.1, Willpower 58.2: 47 / 38 / 37 / 58 (were 47 / 40 / 36 / 56).
  - *Luke on the accelerated path:* x3.4 (was 3.2): at 3.2 his Djem So was 53.3 at year 5 (target 55+) after K moved; now Djem So 56.0, Force Control 61.0 at year 5 (44.1 at year 3). The cap multiplier stays 3.2.
  - *Results (gated seed; on the 20-seed spread every gate passes on all 20 seeds except the Average's 41 at 16.0 +/- 0.1, 18 of 20):* Average 4.43 / 15.99 / 33.19 / 55.79 / 84.00 years (before 4.21 / 16.05 / 32.56 / 54.19 / 81.11); Obi-Wan 3.41 / 12.76 / 21.82 / 37.26 / 57.89 (before 3.25 / 12.50 / 23.62 / 39.12 / 59.29); Anakin 1.31 / 3.76 / 7.53 / 13.81 / 19.20 (before 1.28 / 3.68 / 7.22 / 12.93 / 18.18), within the 4.0x on 20 of 20 seeds (8 of 20 on the old table); Luke 2.15 / 6.89 / 14.37 / 24.81 / 38.37 (before 1.92 / 6.00 / 12.50 / 21.48 / 33.48). The Average's yearly XP within 0.9% of the new table at every age (20-seed median). npc test 7 (Knight at 25 now): every archetype within 1.7 levels. Closed form against the loop: the Average Knight at 24, 47.8 against 47.4.
  - *Knowledge (Average Guardian / Consular at 25 / 40 / 60; before -> after):* Meditation 11.9 / 16.6 / 22.0 -> 15.3 / 22.6 / 30.8 and 13.0 / 27.6 / 41.7 -> 17.6 / 33.7 / 50.1; Force Knowledge 13.0 / 19.9 / 27.9 -> 12.6 / 18.0 / 24.7 and 14.3 / 57.4 / 93.5 -> 16.0 / 57.0 / 92.1 (the higher K).
