- **D-432 Nexus extras (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [8]).** Nexus sites x1.15 on Force skills (invented, NEXUS_SITE_FACTOR); no stacking (the stronger of a site and its planet wins); the bonus only where the place's leaning matches the character's or is balanced (a mismatch gives none for now; alignment effects come later). Until alignment exists (M6), the character's leaning is the path's: a Jedi light, a Sith dark, anyone else balanced (balanced places only; design default). Consequence: a Jedi in the Coruscant Temple (a light nexus site from 5000 BBY) trains Force skills x1.15 (question for Jared). forceBonusAt in src/data/forceNexus.ts; selectTrainingConditions returns the factor.

- **D-433 The instructor's share (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [10]; replaces the D-163 note).** Teaching a Padawan, the teacher gains 10% of the student's drill XP in the drilled form (the session's form, else the main form), at most 0.25 XP a session hour (invented, INSTRUCTOR_SHARE), inside the 4.0x clamp, counted as teaching. The student's drill XP is modelled: the session's effective hours at a standard drill x the student's age factor x the teacher's instruction factor (the student's level from the NPC). It adds to the review share of D-427.

- **D-434 Kel Dor (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [7]).** Lifespan 150 (house default; was the game stat's 70): Zym (born about 3780 BBY) reads as a Human of about 81 in 3661 BBY and 85 at his death. A Kel Dor lifespan check is on the research queue. The one-full-life cap (D-429) no longer touches Zym; it still binds for Darth Ikoral (a Sith Pureblood of 115 on a 100-year table) and long-lived generated NPCs, so it stays.

- **D-435 Deeds past the fifth (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [5]).** The bypass stays at 75. The deeds score is 20 a deed to 100 at five, then 100 + 30 x (1 - e^(-(n - 5) / 5)) toward 130 (invented). An average Padawan (15th-26th percentile among Padawans) peaks at 72 with perfect relationships; the 60th percentile with relationships +40 reaches 75 at about 13 deeds.

- **D-436 The Jedi Order's rules on Force powers (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [9], the M6 catalog's forbidden class with M7 standing; answer [12]).** src/data/orderPowerRules.ts gives each power an Order status: open; forbidden (D-346: Electric Judgment, Masters' controlled study from 990 BBY, open in the New Jedi Order from about 5 ABY, a design default; Deadly Sight; Morichro from 1000 BBY); dark side (Force Lightning, Force Choke: never taught or permitted; design default until alignment). Only Jedi-path characters are bound. Order teachers (the Temple's instructors, the master, a Jedi teacher, Jedi records, now tagged by tradition) refuse a closed power; a Master's permission (forbiddenPermissionGranted, the narrator or the player; the character's master or a Jedi Master) opens a forbidden one. Any other way is covert: each session is discovered with chance 3% a session hour (at most 25%), and a discovery costs 15 points of standing, fading by 5 a year (invented, COVERT_LEARNING). The three forbidden powers join the code's ability list (drilled by the generic ability drill; combat effects narrator-only until the catalog integration). The narrator sees the rules, permissions and discoveries in the state block. [12] Wartime postings and the Council's other roles stay story-only: M8 checklist item 11.

- **D-437 Master minimum (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [4]).** Main specialty 64, the Average's at 40 (63.9 after D-431), typical age 40 (58 before, met at 33.7). The Sith ladder mirrors it.

- **D-438 Hard rule: lore files hold plain dated facts (Jared, 2026-11-22; the Nov 22 lore update).** A lore file is never built around a specific year or era window unless the file is explicitly scoped to one (the Coruscant and Kaas City era files, SW_Dromund_Kaas_History, the GGW files, the timeline slices). Every other lore file holds plain dated facts that the game pulls as needed: no "in 3681–3600" sections, no era-state framing. Catalogs (holocrons, crystals) are single dated lists, and status is resolved in game from the start year. Applies to every future lore batch and research prompt. Recorded in CLAUDE.md (Lore Files).

- **D-439 The Nov 22 lore update (Jared, 2026-11-22; lore chat; docs-v2/lore/batches/lore-update-2026-11-22.md).** Files in `Docs/SWRPG lore/`:
  - *[1] New:* SW_Malachor_V, SW_Tython, SW_Korriban_Planet, SW_Dromund_Kaas_History, SW_Ossus, SW_Kolto_Bacta, SW_Holocrons, SW_Meditation, SW_Lightsaber_Crystals.
  - *[2] Era framing removed (D-438):* SW_Force_Nexus_Vergence, SW_Temple_of_Eedit, SW_Dagobah, SW_Ilum_Temple_CrystalCaves (first committed here, in their dated form).
  - *[3] SW_Lightsabers_Crystals_Materials_AllEras:* the crystal table is trimmed to a pointer; SW_Lightsaber_Crystals is the single source of truth for crystal types (its gray-area notes moved with it).
  - *[4] Deleted:* SW_Dromund_Kaas_After3653 (the same content, re-framed as SW_Dromund_Kaas_History).
  - *[5] Rulings recorded in the files:* the Tython nexus (balanced, [inferred]) and Kalikori as an illegal settlement; Malachor V a dark nexus before 3960 BBY, a wound 3960–3951; Dagobah's cave a dark nexus from 700 BBY; the Coruscant spire a natural light nexus with no end date; the Ilum Crystal Caves and the Temple of Eedit as light-associated sites; Dromund Kaas a planet nexus with the Dark Temple a stronger site; undated natural crystals obtainable at any date from their source world. The game data for these files is D-440 to D-442.
  - *[6] Open (Jared):* Ossus Force-rich or not (the file leans no bonus); the kolto-to-bacta switch-over year; undated holocrons (default: excluded); holocrons as a teaching source; the Galaxies-only unique crystals; Sith meditation and "centered"; moving meditation. The files are not runtime-indexed (D-81 reads only the character, timeline, species and planets files).

- **D-440 The lore update's date-driven data (Jared, 2026-11-22; lore update C).** Every entry is a dated fact (D-438), resolved from the date:
  - *[1] Malachor V:* a dark nexus before 3960 BBY, a wound 3960–3951, destroyed 3951. `FORCE_NEXUSES` gains `toYear` (malachor-v: -3951): the bonus holds through 3951 and stops after. From 3951 BBY the map shows an asteroid and wreckage field in the Malachor system (`WORLD_STATES`, `worldStateAt` in galaxyMap.ts; the narrator gets "World state here" at the place). Era rules: the wreckage (no nexus; residual taint [unclear], a story hazard only) from 3951, surveyed (pockets of life) and excavated for artifacts from 3640.
  - *[2] Tython:* the 3651 rediscovery gate and the seat from 3650 kept. Era rules: not open to ordinary settlement (from the cataclysm of 25,783 BBY); Kalikori an illegal Twi'lek settlement surviving through hardship (3651–3636, the Order's years there).
  - *[3] Dromund Kaas c. 3636–3631:* control `sithEmpire`, the Sith Empire under Empress Acina, a vassal of Zakuul blockaded by the Eternal Fleet, not occupied; leader row "Sith Emperor: Empress Acina (self-declared), sworn to Arcann" 3636–3631.
  - *[4] Korriban:* era rule from c. 3636: the temples bombarded to rubble by Zakuul, later rebuilt (undated), artifacts still buried.
  - *[5] Ossus:* era rules: an irradiated ruin from 3996 BBY; Gnost-Dural's Jedi colony some time after 3636 (year not on record); the 3628 invasion and evacuation. Both end at 3628, the last recorded year (D-411 [5]). Force-rich status pending Jared (no nexus row added).
  - *[6] The healing fluid by date (health.config.ts `healingFluidAt`):* kolto standard until the switch-over; bacta unknown before c. 4100 BBY, a rare import after it, standard from the switch-over (kolto then a cheaper alternative). **The switch-over year is pending Jared's ruling;** `BACTA_STANDARD_FROM_YEAR` is a -2000 placeholder (kept on review, 2026-11-22: the supported range is 3600–1000 BBY, and kolto is the cheap option by Bane's era), flagged `BACTA_SWITCH_PENDING`. The engine's tank care kind stays `bacta` (x4); the narrator's care line names the date's fluid ("a kolto tank" in 3661, "a bacta tank" in 19 BBY); the intent prompt maps a kolto tank to the same care.
  - *[7] Kolto supply:* `KOLTO_SUPPLY_EVENTS` records the 3625 BBY invasion of Manaan (scarcity) for the economy work; not read by any rule yet. Manaan is `contested` in 3625 (timeline slice Part 2).
  - *Tests:* tests/data/loreUpdate.test.ts; tests/turn/loreUpdateContext.test.ts.

- **D-441 Holocron data (Jared, 2026-11-22; lore update D).** src/data/holocrons.ts holds the SW_Holocrons catalog, all 83 rows: id, name, side (J/S/O), firstAppearance (calendar year, null when undated), statusEvents [{year, holder, destroyed?}], notes, source URL. The file's wording becomes a year by the latest year it guarantees ("before X", "by X", a range's later end, era names by fixed years listed in the file header), so nothing is offered before it surely exists. `holocronsAt(year)`: firstAppearance at or before the year, no destruction at or before it, holder from the latest status event at or before it; the 15 undated rows are excluded by default (`includeUndated`, pending Jared). The narrator sees the start year's list (resolved at the campaign's start day) in full when the player's words name holocrons or one of them, and otherwise only those on record at the current place (token budget; design default). Tests at 4000, 3661, 3630 and 1000 BBY (tests/data/holocrons.test.ts) and in the state block.

- **D-442 Crystal data (Jared, 2026-11-22; lore update E).** src/data/crystals.ts replaces the old crystal table (there was none in code; the lore table moved to SW_Lightsaber_Crystals, D-439): all 75 catalog rows with id, name, kind (natural / synthetic / unique, plus `unknown` for the four rows the file marks "?": Katak, Vexxtal, Amplification, Life Drain), item (type / named), source world(s), origin, effect, dated limits (availableFrom, lostAfter, rare, controlled, extinct), firstDatedUse (citation only), source URL. Rules: an undated natural crystal is obtainable at any date from its source world; non-natural rows are gated by their first dated year (synthetics from 3961 BBY, the end of their development window); the one natural row gated by a date is the Blackwing Crystal (its source, the virus, is dated 1 ABY); Ruusan lost after 1000 BBY; Eralam, Sapith (fossil only), Pontite, Corusca, Phond, Qixoni, Etaan and Solari rare; Rubat controlled. The Galaxies-only named crystals are gated to c. 0 BBY (pending Jared, the file's decision 3). The file has no NOT USED or TYPE ONLY rows; natural and synthetic rows are types, unique rows named items. Two notes for Jared: Windu's Guile is a natural Hurrikaine variant (so ungated) carrying Windu's name; Lambent is a Yuuzhan Vong harvest, ungated by the rule though only reachable from c. 25 ABY. `crystalsAt(year)`, `crystalsFromWorld(world, year)`, `crystalAvailability`. Not yet wired to items or the narrator (M8 equipment). Tests: tests/data/crystals.test.ts.

- **D-443 Ilum opens to the Empire from c. 3651 BBY (Jared, 2026-11-22; SW_Ilum_Temple_CrystalCaves decision 1).** The Empire learns of Ilum and occupies it from c. 3651 BBY [inferred: the Sith hold the temple "over a decade" before the 3640 battle]. The Ilum world gate (galaxyMap.ts, D-402 [8]) now opens to the Empire from 3651, not 3640. No Imperial control span is added for 3651–3641: D-411 [5] allows no inferred Cold War spans, so the gate carries the ruling and control there stays blank until the 3640 battle. Research-queue item cleared. The review of the lore update also approved D-441's narrator context and its "latest guaranteed year" rule.

- **D-444 Crystal table re-synced (Jared, 2026-11-22; SW_Lightsaber_Crystals re-saved, decision 3).** The 19 rows the file marks NOT USED (Star Wars Galaxies and Force Unleashed loot crystals with no story foundation: Katak, Vexxtal, Amplification, Impact, Incineration, Corrosion, Life Drain, Blackwing and 11 Galaxies named crystals) are dropped from src/data/crystals.ts, which removes the `unknown` kind and the Galaxies gate (56 rows remain). The review note said 21; the file marks 19 and its decision 3 says 19. TYPE ONLY rows (Lava, Permafrost, Windu's Guile) are natural types from their source worlds. Windu's Guile is a Hurrikaine crystal, obtainable at any date as Hurrikaine; its name is used from Mace Windu's time (`typeOf`, `crystalNameAt`; from c. 72 BBY, his birth [inferred]). Lambent is gated to Yuuzhan Vong contact, c. 25 ABY on (the file's row). Bane's Heart, the focus crystal and compressed energy crystals stay in full.

- **D-445 The Order's home in the calibration (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [1] to the re-measurement report).** The Average, Obi-Wan and Anakin run with the Coruscant Temple site bonus active (a light nexus site, x1.15 on Force skills, D-432): the lifetime harness takes dated place spans (`places`; the Temple all life, TEMPLE_LIFE in tests/lifetime/profiles.ts; outside a span the life is in the field, no bonus). Luke gets Dagobah's Force-rich x1.10 for his Dagobah months only: three months at 22 (3 ABY; the lore gives 3-4 ABY and no length, design default, on the research queue). Tython's x1.10 applies as normal after the Order's move (a balanced planet; tested); a Jedi there trains Force skills a little slower than the Temple-calibrated Average.
  - *The Jedi archetypes' paces re-measured in the Temple* (npm run paces, 208 s; npc test 7 runs them there too), so a generated Jedi matches a player of the same life: Force Control and Force Sense paces about +15% (Guardian Padawan 0.981 / 0.924 -> 1.142 / 1.067). The Sith archetypes have no home site yet (question). The Guardian's Knight form fill moved 1.543 -> 1.615 (the closed form at 70: 87.5 -> 89.0).
  - *K re-solved:* unchanged at 12.30 (41 at 15.995 years on the gated seed; the bonus touches Force skills only).
  - *The 4.0x table re-measured* (20-seed medians, npm run spread, 2,505 s): 30: 444 -> 405, 90: 324 -> 300, 60: 505 -> 498, 70: 448 -> 452, 98: 208 -> 204, the rest within 0.3%. The dip at 30 is a real loop effect, not the clamp (nothing discarded): Soresu and Willpower gains fall together for about three years once the Force skills pass about 45 (Soresu 441 -> 404 XP a year, Willpower about -200), and the Temple brings that two years forward (28-31 instead of 30-33). The verification spread (2,043 s): worst deviation 0.7%.
  - *The Knight minimums re-read by the D-51 rule* (the Average at 24, rounded down): Force Control 38 -> 41 (41.4), Force Sense 37 -> 40 (40.2; 37 was met at 21.8, outside the +/-2 years); highest form 47 and Willpower 58 stay. Jared had accepted 38 / 37 before the Temple was in; the rule moves them (question). The Padawan minimum (Force Control 14, met at 8.9) stays.
  - *Results (gated seed; 20 seeds: every gate passes on all 20 except the Average's 41 at 16.0 +/- 0.1, 18 of 20, and Obi-Wan's Clone Wars share, 19 of 20):* Average 4.43 / 15.99 / 33.05 / 55.47 / 83.51 years (before 4.43 / 15.99 / 33.19 / 55.79 / 84.00); Obi-Wan 3.41 / 12.75 / 21.75 / 37.14 / 57.82 (before 3.41 / 12.76 / 21.82 / 37.26 / 57.89); Anakin 1.31 / 3.79 / 7.36 / 13.43 / 19.10 (before 1.31 / 3.76 / 7.53 / 13.81 / 19.20); Luke 2.15 / 6.86 / 14.35 / 24.79 / 38.38 (before 2.15 / 6.89 / 14.37 / 24.81 / 38.37). The Average at 24: Force Control 41.4, Force Sense 40.2 (were 38.1, 37.1); at 40 highest form 64.1, Force Control 52.1.
  - *Lore sync:* the Dagobah cave is a dark site only from about 700 BBY (SW_Dagobah); Dagobah's Force-rich source now cites Jared's ruling in SW_Dagobah, and the "teems with life" wording, which the lore file traces to Canon (The Clone Wars "Lost Missions", 2014), is dropped.

- **D-446 Career Willpower on the real loop (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [2] to the re-measurement report; amended 2026-11-23, D-458).** *Amended:* the untrimmed rates after the rest-before-trim fix (D-456) are kept: soldier 0.59, warrior 0.587, bounty hunter 0.712 and the rest as measured (src/data/archetypes.ts); sourced canon anchors still win stat by stat. *Before:* career archetypes' Willpower paces stay at the loop's 0.18-0.30 (D-431). Exception: a canon NPC with a sourced Willpower anchor keeps the anchor. The canon stat model already does this per stat (src/engine/canonStats.ts: a stat with its own anchor takes the anchor, the generator's line fills only stats no anchor shows); all 45 anchors in the roster are hand-set sheets (none is a generated draft), so no anchor carries the old Willpower. Test: tests/m7/followupRulings.test.ts.

- **D-447 The Yuuzhan Vong are outside the Force (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [3]).** Hard block on Force XP and Force powers for a species outside the Force (the forceInvisible trait: the Yuuzhan Vong; Legends, SW_Characters_15, SW_Midichlorians_Force_Potential): `isOutsideForce` and `isForceXpSkill` (the Force skills and the Force abilities) in src/engine/speed.ts; creation's closed form drops every Force skill and power for such a species, whatever the archetype's paces. A player character cannot be a Vong (they are not in the species table), so the block lives where the Vong exist: NPC creation and Force combat (a guard on the character's training and XP was tried and dropped as unreachable code). Force effects still reach them: lightning (with its variants, Electric Judgment among them) now affects a target outside the Force (SW_Force_Powers_Part2b F7: lightning and Force Net were the only powers known to affect the Vong directly); push, choke, grip, mind tricks and sensing still fail; thrown objects and sabers still hit. The duel panel, the narrator's fight line and the choice filter follow (Electric Judgment is narrator-run until the catalog integration). No canon Vong NPC exists yet; Onimi, the lore's one Force-sensitive exception, would need an override when he is added (question).

- **D-448 Covert discoveries escalate (Jared, 2026-11-22; answers [5] and [6]).** Confirmed: dark-side powers are never permitted to a Jedi, even a Master. Accepted: covert learning at 3% a session hour (at most 25%), -15 standing fading by 5 a year. New: the discovery counter (the count of `covertDiscoveries`) with narrator-facing escalation flags (COVERT_ESCALATION in src/data/orderPowerRules.ts; `covertEscalation`, `selectCovertEscalation`): the 1st discovery the Master's reprimand, the 2nd a summons before the Council, the 3rd (and later) the trials held back; any discovered dark-side power the Council's judgment (possible expulsion). The code tracks; the narrator plays: each discovery's note names the step it newly reaches ("consequence to narrate now: ..."), and the state block lists the count and every step reached. Nothing is enforced mechanically (the trials are not blocked; question). Also recorded: [4] the Knight minimums 47 / 38 / 37 / 58 accepted; [8] characters who are neither Jedi nor Sith get a nexus bonus only at balanced places until alignment exists, accepted.

- **D-449 Electric Judgment open from 11 ABY (Jared, 2026-11-22; answer [7]).** Forbidden in the Old Order with the Master's-permission path in every era (the Plo Koon precedent: the Council had him record it on the Great Holocron); Masters' controlled study from 990 BBY as before; open from 11 ABY (Luke's Praxeum), not 5 ABY.

- **D-450 Two checks (Jared, 2026-11-22; answers [9] and [10]).**
  - *[9] The instructor's share and the review share pay on the same student hours:* both come from the same `teachingPadawan` session hours, and with no form named both land in the teacher's main form. So only the larger stays: the instructor's share (D-433) at 0.10-0.13 XP a session hour against the review share (D-427) at 0.01-0.02 (measured: teacher 28 / 40 / 60 at levels 47 / 64 / 80, student 13 / 16 / 20, one hour). The review share (`$mainForm` 0.2 on teachingPadawan) is removed.
  - *[10] Feints:* the existing feint test seeded duel.rng, which the combat action overwrites with the game's RNG (meta.rngState): every pass of its seed loop rolled the same dice, so "200 seeds" never varied anything, and the D-431 change to 200 had only moved which fixed outcome it saw. Fixed to seed meta.rngState (40 seeds again). Success rates over 200 seeds per pairing (tests/turn/feintRates.test.ts): a Padawan of 16 against a Padawan / Knight / Master / Sith Lord 49.0 / 37.0 / 29.0 / 32.5%; a Knight of 30 60.0 / 49.0 / 39.5 / 49.0%; a Master of 50 66.0 / 58.5 / 44.0 / 52.5%. A Sith Lord reads about half of all feints, well under the 90% line, so no rule change. (The generated Lord's Force Sense is low, 18-23, so its Willpower carries the defense.)

- **D-451 The major-nexus tier and the order seats by date (Jared, 2026-11-23; answers [0] and [3] to followups-2026-11-22).**
  - *Tiers:* a major nexus planet x1.15 (`major` in src/data/forceNexus.ts; MAJOR_NEXUS_FACTOR): Tython (balanced), Korriban and Dromund Kaas (dark). Ordinary nexus and Force-rich planets stay x1.10 (Byss, Vjun, Malachor V, Dagobah); sites stay x1.15. The stronger wins, no stacking: the Valley of the Dark Lords no longer counts as stronger than its planet, and the Dark Temple on Dromund Kaas (a dark site from the Emperor's expansion after 4980 BBY, SW_Dromund_Kaas_KaasCity_OldRepublic_Part1) equals its planet. The leaning check still applies (a Jedi on Korriban gets nothing). Mortis is a special exception: story-only (`storyOnly`), no multiplier, and no map access (it is not on the galaxy map). The Mortis arc is 2008 The Clone Wars material, which the lore file marks "borderline Legends".
  - *Order seats by date:* src/data/orderSeats.ts gives each Jedi seat a home place (the Coruscant Temple; its ruins 3,653-3,651; the Temple on Tython from 3,650; Coruscant again from 3,600, a design default; none 19 BBY-11 ABY; the Praxeum on Yavin 4; the Ossus academy) and a new Sith table, SITH_SEATS: the Korriban academy for acolytes and the Lords' seat for apprentices and Lords. Rows: Vitiate's Empire on Dromund Kaas from 4980 BBY (no acolyte seat until Korriban is retaken); Revan's academy and capital on Korriban 3959-3956; the Korriban academy and Kaas City 3681-3637; Kaas City alone after the Republic's 3637 attack and Zakuul's bombardment, to 3,600 (the lore files stop at 3630: design default); the Brotherhood of Darkness's academy 1010-1000 (headquarters on Roon); the One Sith on Korriban from 27 ABY. No seat while abandoned. `homeSeatAt(path, tier, year)` resolves it.
  - *Wired into the calibration (not re-measured in Phase A):* the lifetime harness's `seat` option moves a life to its seat by rank and year, as a building on its world. The Average, Obi-Wan and Anakin read the run's calendar (the Coruscant Temple at the neutral test year, as before). The era profile now moves with the seat (Coruscant, the ruins, Tython from 3,650) instead of placing itself. The Order archetypes (pace measurement and npc test 7) live at their seat; the Sith have no seat at the neutral test year (1,032 BBY), so a Sith archetype's life is read from 3,671 BBY (SITH_SEAT_YEAR): the Korriban academy as an acolyte, Dromund Kaas from apprentice on.

- **D-452 Luke's month on Dagobah (Jared, 2026-11-23; answer [6]).** Three months -> one month, at 22 (3 ABY). Ruling, Jared: no Legends figure exists. The Hoth-Bespin hyperspace distance (about 1,150 light years on the Falcon's class-10 backup hyperdrive) gives under a day of transit, so the time is set by events (the asteroid field, Fett tracking the Falcon, the Empire reaching Bespin first). One month is the accepted figure. The profile changed only; re-measured in the overnight run.

- **D-453 The Onimi exception (Jared, 2026-11-23; answer [4]).** A per-character exception flag to the Yuuzhan Vong Force block: `insideForce` on a canon figure (src/data/canon/types.ts). The species block stays. A flagged figure gains Force XP, may have Force powers, and can be sensed and touched through the Force (`characterForceTraits` drops forceInvisible; mindImmune stays). No Onimi entry exists yet; the flag is ready for him. Root fix found on the way: the NPC generator builds an unknown species (the Vong) on the human body, so creation's D-447 block read "human" and never fired for a story Vong (only the Vong archetype's empty Force paces kept them Force-less). The generator now passes the real species' block to creation (`outsideForce`), with the canon flag.

- **D-454 The trials held back, enforced (Jared, 2026-11-23; answer [5]).** From the 3rd covert discovery on, each discovery delays Knight Trials eligibility by 1 year, counted from the later of the discovery and the hold already running (TRIALS_HOLD; `trialsHeldUntil`). The hold lifts on its own when nothing new is discovered. The trial check refuses while it runs; the discovery's note gives the date, and the state block shows it ("enforced"). The narrator narrates; the code decides.

- **D-455 Further rulings and the dip diagnosis (Jared, 2026-11-23; answers [1], [2], [7], [8]).**
  - *[1]* The Knight minimums stay at Force Control 41 / Force Sense 40 (D-445).
  - *[7]* Obi-Wan's Clone Wars combat share at 19 of 20 seeds is accepted; no retune.
  - *[8]* Jared's design notes on the XP sources for Intelligence, Tactics, Charisma, Melee, Stealth and Blaster, and the officer, senator/politician and scholar archetypes, are filed as M8 checklist item 12 (item 10 folded in); the martial-cultures question is on the research queue.
  - *[2] The dip at 30 (diagnosis; no change in Phase A):* not a Force-skill rule. The Force boost (0.3 x (Force Control - 40), progression-numbers §11) lifts Endurance performance, and with it the physical capacity the planner projects against. The planner takes a conditional 0.5 h rest between physical blocks only when the physical meter has passed 0.7 (training-routines §4 rule 5). As capacity rises, the meter at those rest points slips just under 0.7 (mean 0.68 -> 0.635), so the rests are skipped (about 790 -> 640-655 taken a year at 29-32). The day then projects over the 0.9 limit and the fill is trimmed (68 -> 117-130 h a year), mostly Soresu. The actual day ends less tired (f 0.63 -> 0.58), so Willpower from fatigue (g(f), §9) falls too. Two to three years later the higher capacity clears the limit without the rest, and the dip ends. The Temple brings it two years earlier because it raises Force Control, and so the boost, sooner. Not intended: the spec's rule 5 says to rest "whenever projected f_phys would pass 0.7" (a look-ahead); the code checks whether it has already passed. In either reading, getting fitter should never cost training hours. Proposed fix: a rest before a trim. When the projected day passes the tiredness limit, the planner first takes the skipped conditional rests (they cost no training hours, only clock time), and trims the fill only if the day is still over. The real day loop takes the rests the plan marks as needed. Measured one-seed (gated Average): trimmed days 137 -> 199-234 a year at 29-32.

- **D-456 A rest before a trim; the overnight re-measure (2026-11-23; Phase B of the follow-up answers, unattended).**
  - *The fix (D-455 [2] diagnosed the dip at 30 as accidental):* a day that projects over its tiredness limit first takes every rest between its physical blocks (they cost clock time, not training hours); only a day still over is trimmed (src/features/routines/scheduler.ts; the plan marks such rests unconditional, and the day loop takes them; training-routines §4 rule 5). On the gated Average the fill trims at 22-36 fall from 29-234 days a year to none; the dip is gone (the 4.0x table point at 30: 405 -> 484).
  - *Re-measured within the bounds:* K 12.30 -> 12.45 (41 at 16.005 years on the gated seed; 12.47 gave 16.027); the 4.0x table from the 20-seed medians (16: 604 -> 625, 30: 405 -> 484, 60: 498 -> 480, 90: 300 -> 338, the rest within 1.5%); the archetype paces on the real loop, the Sith archetypes at their seats for the first time (Sith Warrior Padawan Force Control 0.994 -> 1.143, Knight form fill 1.372 -> 1.528). The Vong's measured Force Control pace (0.004) is removed again (D-447). Luke's accelerated-path bonus x3.4 -> x3.5 (at x3.4 Djem So was 55.4 at year 5 after K rose; at x3.5 it is 56.2, Force Control 61.7). Nothing else changed (rank minimums, nexus multipliers, instructor share, covert and Order rules, stat formulas).
  - *Side effects reported, not tuned:* the fix also stops careers losing 230-320 fill hours a year to trims, so their days end more tired and career Willpower paces roughly double (soldier 0.302 -> 0.59; D-446 had accepted the trimmed rates). Obi-Wan's later milestones slow (101 at 64.6 years against a 52.4 target) and his Clone Wars share falls to 15.6%: the 4.0x clamp caps a skill by its share of training hours (D-104), and the old trims had cut his other fill blocks harder than Soresu, raising Soresu's share; untrimmed, the clamp discards about 356 XP a year from him at 26-30 (101 before). Nothing inside the bounds moves it, so the gates stay failed (report questions).
  - *Harness:* the lifetime harness now counts combat XP the clamp discards (`fightStats.combatDiscardedXp`); its `discarded` counter reads clamp notes on applied events, where routine sessions do not put them, so it always read 0 (reported, not changed). A life now moves to its place at the next wake, before the day hook looks (the era profile probed the first day of 3,650 before the move).

## 2026-11-23 — Answers to the overnight re-measure (Jared)

- **D-457 The 4.0x clamp becomes a per-hour rate cap (Jared, 2026-11-23; answer [6]; replaces D-104's share rule, keeps D-109 and D-127).** *Ruling:* the ceiling limits prodigy speed, never focus.
  - *Old rule (D-48, D-104):* cap = 4.0 x Y_avg(age) x share / 0.5, with share = max(focus share, the skill's share of all the character's training hours in the window). Training more of anything else shrank the main skill's cap; that is what cost Obi-Wan about 356 XP a year once the trims stopped (D-456).
  - *New rule:* cap = 4.0 x R_avg(skill, age at the window start) x the character's hours in that skill in the window (this session's included). R_avg is the Average's XP per hour of training in that same skill for the 365 days starting at that age: the 20-seed median of its pace-window XP / window hours (training sessions, their synergy and fatigue Willpower; a session's hours by XP share, D-109). Hours in other skills never change a skill's cap. Focus weights no longer enter the clamp. The accelerated path still raises the cap x3.2 (D-418). src/engine/paceClamp.ts; the table is src/data/paceRates.ts.
  - *Chosen while Jared was away (design defaults, report questions):* (a) the rate counts the drill XP the ceiling banks (D-45), and its overflow past a full bank, with the credited XP: the ceiling is its own rule, and the Average's ceilinged forms read 75% slow otherwise (credited only: Ataru 0.90 -> 0.26 XP/h at 40; banked counted but not the overflow: 0.88 -> 0.27 at 80, once the bank is full), which would cap anyone whose form is not at the ceiling at about 1 XP/h. (b) A skill gets its own row where the Average's median hours in it reach 20 a year; a lightsaber form the Average does not train (Djem So, Makashi, ...) reads the Average's most-trained form that year; any other untrained skill reads the Average's whole-training rate (all window XP / all window hours). (c) The table ages are the Y_avg ages, interpolated, flat outside. (d) *Fights leave the per-hour clamp and its window* (they kept their D-127 exchange time as window hours at first, and the vongPipeline test caught it: a fresh character's first fight had only its exchanges' seconds in the window, so the cap sat near 0 and nearly all its combat XP was discarded; under the share rule a fight-only skill had share 1). Per exchange, combat XP differs from the Average's in the same fight only by M, which is capped at 2.8 under 4.0 (the accelerated path is allowed past, D-418), so the per-hour ceiling holds for combat on its own; counted in the window, a war year's fights would also eat the training's room. Combat XP keeps its own limits (combat-xp.md: the daily diminishing, the stakes), and the lifetime "within 4.0x" floor still counts it. A fight still clears rust (its exchange time as practice).
  - *Y_avg stays,* no longer as the cap: it is the reference of the "within 4.0x" lifetime floor (D-235) and the Average's yearly table gate. The spread now also gates the per-hour table (the 20-seed median within 5% at every point with 50+ median hours) and writes the measured table next to its results.
  - *Found while measuring:* the old share rule clamped the Average itself in its last years: Soresu's share of its hours falls to 0.127 at 98, so the old cap (about 210 XP) sat under its own 245 XP. Unclamped, its year at 98 reads +19% against the Y_avg table, which is re-measured in Phase B.
  - *The training card* now shows each primary's M and its parts (talent, cross-talent, instruction, age, state, species, place, accelerated, review), and the routine's session log carries the card's discarded XP and factors; the clamp report reads them.

- **D-458 Further answers to the overnight re-measure (Jared, 2026-11-23; answers [1]-[5]).**
  - *[6 harness] The discarded counter is fixed:* it reads the clamp's notes where they are written (the routine's session logs for training, the fight's notes for combat) and totals XP, per skill (`LifeResult.discarded`, `discardedBySkill`). Before, it read the applied events, which carry no clamp notes, so it always read 0. Test: tests/support/discardedCounter.test.ts (a shrunk table makes the clamp fire; the counter reads it).
  - *[5] The montage test* switches world events off in its file (WORLD_EVENT_CHANCE 0) instead of hunting for a seed; it runs on the plain seed again.
  - *[2] Career Willpower:* the untrimmed rates after D-456 are kept (soldier 0.59 and the rest; D-446 amended). Sourced canon anchors still win stat by stat.
  - *[3] Sith paces rising more than 15%:* accepted; the ±15% bound applied to profile constants only.
  - *[4] The Mandalorian's 0.02 miss on npc test 7:* accepted for now; re-checked after Phase B.

- **D-459 The Jedi Initiate lightsaber curriculum; the recalibration under the per-hour clamp (Jared, 2026-11-23; Phase B, unattended).** *Ruling (source: The Jedi Path via Jedi training / Legends, Jedi instructor / Legends, Jedi Initiate / Legends):* Initiates take courses in Forms I-VI with training sabers; Form VII is excluded. Post-Ruusan academy practice, the default per lore batch 13's ruling. Every number below is invented (design defaults).
  - *Built (src/data/initiateCurriculum.ts, src/features/routines/curriculum.ts; the Initiate row's 2 h saber block):* (1) the saber gate: the classes open at Force Control 13 and Force Knowledge 5 (natural baselines included), the Average's levels at the design gate age 6 rounded down (Force Knowledge one lower: 6.0 sat on the line); the Average opens them at about 5.7-5.9. Before the gate the 2 h go to Force Control (1 h), physical training (0.5 h, agility / strength / endurance by day) and centering (0.5 h). (2) Form I only from the gate. (3) The survey from 10 (the Initiate Trials' minimum age): Form I keeps 1 h; Forms II-VI rotate a day each through the other hour (Form V alternating Shien and Djem So by week), about 10% each; total hours unchanged. (5) A survey form's first class starts it with the fundamentals transfer (D-119), so from Padawan the plan's main form starts at its survey level; no second transfer. (6) The closed form and the archetype pace measurement follow it for Jedi (no form XP before the gate age, Form I alone to 10, then the survey shares; the Initiate form pace measured over those effective years). The Sith acolyte row is unchanged. The survey classes do not count as the Order sparring class for E (only the Shii-Cho class does, as before).
  - *(4) The Initiate Trials (set from the Average on the curriculum, D-51 style; gated seed, K 11.5):* the dueling test is Form I at rating 20 plus 4 of Forms II-VI at level 16+ (Form V by its better variant; the Average's lowest after a survey year, 16.4 at 11, rounded down); Force Control at rating 20 as before. Eligibility minimums: Form I 24 stays (met at about 11.8, inside +/-2 years of 10); Force Control 14 -> 19 (the pre-gate hours bring 14 to about 6.2; the Average's level at 10 is 19.5). The Padawan rank row mirrors them; the Sith apprentice row keeps 24 / 14. *Reading chosen:* item 4 named "Skill 3 of 4 at rating 45", which is the Knight Trial of Skill; the Initiate Trials were changed and the Knight Trial of Skill left as it is (report question).
  - *Fixed on the way:* the survey rotation's Form V variant index went negative on BBY days (absolute days are negative), so Djem So was never trained; a non-negative modulo now.
  - *The recalibration within the bounds:* the per-hour table re-measured on the curriculum (20 seeds, the clamp open; the gate passes at 0.0%); the yearly table re-measured (4: 0, no form XP before the gate; 8: 550, 12: 662, 20: 595, 90: 275, 98: 226; the gate passes at 0.2%); the archetype paces re-measured (Guardian Initiate Force Control 0.452 -> 0.648, form pace 1.028 -> 1.204 over the effective years; the Vong's Force Control pace removed again, D-447). *K:* the curriculum moves the Average's 41 from 16.0 to 18.51 years (it starts Form I at about 5.7, gives it half the saber hours from 10 and starts Padawan Soresu from a survey level of about 18 instead of a transfer from Shii-Cho 29). The solve wants K 10.21; the bound is 11.5, so K = 11.5 and the Average's 41 sits at 17.30 years (0 of 20 seeds in 16.0 +/- 0.1). Per the stopping rules the bounds were not widened and the design defaults (gate age 6, survey from 10) were not tuned: one full pass, then stop. Luke's accelerated bonus stays x3.5.
- **D-460 Rulings on the clamp and curriculum report; the curriculum fix and the recalibration (Jared, 2026-11-23; unattended run).** Rulings on remeasure-2026-11-23b.md:
  - *Phase A choices confirmed (D-457):* (1) the Average's per-hour rate counts the drill XP the ceiling banks or loses; (2) a form the Average never trains uses its most-trained form's rate; (3) fights stay outside the per-hour clamp (combat has the 2.8 speed cap).
  - *[1] K bound:* K is not lowered below 11.5 and the targets do not move (Obi-Wan's 41 is canon-anchored; a lower K breaks Force Control, Force Sense and Luke). The curriculum mechanics are fixed instead, by levers in order, stopping at the first combination whose solved K is inside 11.5-13.5: (a) the second transfer; (b) plus Form I at 75% in the survey; (c) plus the gate at 5.
  - *[2] The Padawan specialty transfer:* the survey transfer stays; a second one at the Padawan start: the main form starts at max(its current XP, 0.75 x Form I's XP at that time). Fundamentals carry into the form a Padawan specializes in. *Built:* on the main form's first session as a Jedi past Initiate (once in a life, outside the pace window; `progression.specialtyTransfer`, the activity event's `specialty` field; a character created past Initiate holds it already, 'creation'); the closed form gives the Padawan plan's main form max(its XP, 0.75 x Form I) at the Padawan start (Jedi with the curriculum).
  - *[3] The gate covers all of an Initiate's lightsaber training, the Initiate's own free training included.* *Built:* the training rule refuses a form session for a Jedi Initiate before the gate; the scheduler sends a pre-gate Initiate's fill form hours to the pre-gate blocks (Force Control, physical training, centering). Fights (spars) are not training sessions and are not gated. The closed form already started all Initiate form XP at the gate age.
  - *[4] The Knight Trial of Skill is left alone* (confirmed). *[5] The Force Control minimum stays 19.* *[6] Sith acolytes get a curriculum in a later design pass from the Korriban academy lore (open item).* *[7] The Mandalorian's pace fit in this run (below).* *[8] The canon Force Knowledge and Meditation bands: next session, once every goal passes.*
  - *The probes (levels probe and K solve, gated seed, at K 11.5):* (a) K' 10.84 (the Average's 21 at 5.87, 41 at 16.70); (a)+(b) K' 11.11 (21 at 5.87, 41 at 16.40); (a)+(b)+(c) K' 11.66 (21 at 5.03, 41 at 15.84): inside the bounds, so all three levers are taken. For (c) the gate minimums were re-set by the D-459 rule from the Average at 5 (Force Control 9.7, Force Knowledge 4.2, rounded down: 9 / 4; the Average opens the classes at about 4.9).
  - *The full pass:* K 11.64 (11.66 gave 41 at 16.024; 11.64 gives 16.000); the per-hour table re-measured (the clamp open; gate worst -0.0%); the yearly table re-measured (8: 567, 12: 620, 20: 634, 60: 449; gate worst 0.1%); the archetype paces re-measured (the Vong's Force Control pace removed again, D-447); the Initiate Trials' competence floor 16 -> 18 (the Average's lowest of Forms II-VI after a survey year, 18.0 at 11); Form I 24 (met at about 10.3) and Force Control 19 (about 10.9) stay. Luke's accelerated bonus stays x3.5 (Djem So 59.6 at year 5).
  - *Fixed on the way:* the lifetime harness now keeps the specialty transfer out of the yearly and phase XP (as D-119's transfers), without logging it as a D-119 transfer.
  - *[7] The Mandalorian (start 8):* no single pace fits its life: its loop runs at an effective pace of about 1.04 at 9-11 (the youth training budget, 4 h to 10 and 6 h from 15) and 1.43 by 60; the best single pace (1.355) still misses by up to 2.96 levels, so the measured 60-year pace is kept and npc test 7 still misses (59.75 / 61.90). The root-cause fix (a youth pace phase for careers that start before 15) changes the closed form's structure and waits for approval (report question).
  - *Goals still failing (reported, not tuned around):* Obi-Wan's 21 (4.06-4.12 against the 4.08 limit of +20%; the gate over his free Shii-Cho before 5); Luke's 41 (5.33-5.70 against the 5.52 floor; K speeds him, and no in-bounds lever moves him); the Average's Master main specialty 64 at 37.46 (40 +/- 2); the Mandalorian.

The warrior tracks within 0.90; the Vong within 1.99 (+1.68 at year 25, from its adult years). Sith acolytes will use it later.

Not changed (the ruling covered the 41); his milestones pass 12/20.

The Average crosses 66 at 39.21-39.51 (20 seeds). *Found:* the Knight's Force Control 41 now fails the rank-minimum gate on 13 of 20 seeds (crossed at 21.93-22.14, by at most 0.07 years outside 24 +/- 2); not changed.

Set per archetype (`KNOWLEDGE_BANDS`), because the two focus archetypes differ too much to share a band (Force Knowledge at 50: Consular 78.6, Inquisitor 41.5): Force Knowledge Guardian / Sentinel 17 / 23 / 29, Consular 42 / 79 / 107, Warrior 14 / 19 / 24, Inquisitor 25 / 42 / 57; Meditation Guardian / Sentinel 20 / 28 / 36, Consular 28 / 44 / 59, Warrior 14 / 21 / 27, Inquisitor 17 / 27 / 36 (were 40 / 60 / 80 in focus, 20 / 40 / 60 outside). The bands are now curves by age (`KNOWLEDGE_BAND_CURVES`: the same 100 lives, 20 seeds per archetype, read at 20-100, flat outside). Hand-set lines still override; never above the authored value. *Reading chosen:* every roster figure has a birth year, but 144 of 196 are estimates (a design default, D-412), which fall back; 52 come from the lore. Of the 22 figures with a band-read Force Knowledge or Meditation: 5 read their own age (Kira Carsen 26, Syo Bakarn 28, Darth Thanaton 50, Darth Baras 54, Darth Vowrawn 71), 17 fall back. Against the D-461 midpoint reads, Syo Bakarn moves -14 Force Knowledge and -5 Meditation (28, not 32.5), Kira Carsen -2 Force Knowledge and -3 Meditation, Darth Baras +3 / +2; the rest are unchanged. To support it, `humanEquivalentAge` and the canon-to-species-table id mapping moved into src/data/species.ts (engine/species and the NPC selectors use them from there), because the data layer imports nothing else.

- **D-463 The Jedi Path is the baseline academy curriculum in any era (Jared, 2026-11-23; replaces the narrower lore-batch-13 framing of D-378 [1] and D-459's "post-Ruusan default").** *The Jedi Path* is the baseline curriculum for the academy-based Jedi Order in any era: what is taught (the Code and the Three Pillars, Force studies, classroom subjects, meditation, courses in Forms I-VI). Known era differences override it: entry ages (from infancy is post-Ruusan; earlier eras take teens or twenties); strictness ("progress or leave" and one Master, one Padawan are post-Ruusan; earlier Masters could have several students); the Initiate Trials' form (pre-Ruusan, lightsaber construction as the second test; Dantooine, facing the dark side); and eras with no academy stage (the Je'daii, the early Order, the New Sith Wars). Recorded in the lore index (Jedi Order). D-378 [1]'s "later-era default" tags read under this ruling: the baseline applies unless the era's attested practice differs (as D-378 already found for the braid, the selection tournament and the age limit in 3661).
  - *Date-driven detail, built:* the Initiate survey covers the Forms I-VI that exist at the date. Earliest attested years (SW_Lightsaber_Forms_AllEras_Part1; `FORM_FROM_YEAR`): Form IV Ataru 3976 BBY (the Mandalorian Wars), Form V Djem So 4000 BBY ("developed before 4000 BBY"), Shien 3959 BBY ("existed at least by the Jedi Civil War"); Forms I, II, III and VI carry no start date (every lightsaber era). For any date with all six (3959 BBY on, including the default 3661 BBY and the test year) the curriculum is unchanged; earlier dates rotate through the existing groups, Form I keeps 75% and the rest splits across the forms that exist. The day plan reads the date of the day; the closed form reads the year the character reached the survey age; the Initiate Trials' competence counts only existing groups and needs all but one (4 of 5 when all exist). Tests: the 3990 BBY curriculum (no Ataru or Shien; 6.25% each for the four groups), 4100 BBY (three groups), the closed form and the Trials' 3-of-4.

- **D-464 Economy rulings and the economy lore fixes (Jared, 2026-11-23).** *Rulings* (Jared's M8 design notes, "Economy" section: docs-v2/spec/m8-design-notes.md, "Economy"; D-465; to be built with M8 checklist item 5): a stable credit; separate Republic and Imperial credits, with a changer's cut on exchange; Hutt coins; Jedi decline rewards, with consequences only for amassing wealth, and NPC behaviour by alignment; lifestyle tiers; debt; haggling within +/-20%. Every number is set and tagged when it is built. *Lore fixes in the SW_Economy_* set (Docs/SWRPG lore, 17 files):* (1) lines sourced only to *Endless Vigil* (FFG, Oct 2016, Canon era) moved to each file's Not used (Events: the IGBC recession line; Institutions_Debt: the same fact, split from the Muunilinst-backed fact, which stays; Part 9's 64 Endless Vigil items were already in Not used); (2) the [gray] *Nexus of Power* (FFG, Mar 2016) rows moved to their files' Not used, none backed by another source: 5 rows in GamePriceLists Parts 3, 4, 5 and 7 (the economy set holds 5, not 41; the Bardottan Sphere [canon] gap-fill row cites it too and stays as [canon]); Part 1's [gray] count updated (559 -> 554); (3) CanonGapFill: 12 broken rows deleted (price cells holding prose, image captions, quotes or HTML comments, no price: Sig x3, Trimantium x3, Yurfloo x2, Ziroxin-12 x4); its count updated (158 -> 146). The economy files, the Sith academies, weapon classes, named weapons and armour, starship classes and space tactics files, and the two weapons research lists (docs-v2/lore) are committed and indexed.
- **D-465 Answers to remeasure-2026-11-23e: the bands' read age, Nexus of Power across the lore, the early Initiate Trials, the M8 design notes in the repo (Jared, 2026-11-23).**

All 22 figures with a band-read Force Knowledge or Meditation (25 anchors) now read their own age (D-462: 5). Moves of more than 10 against D-462, all Inquisitors whose estimated ages sit far from their standing's midpoint: Ragate (Overseer, read 75: Force Knowledge 42 -> 60, Meditation 27 -> 38), Darth Acina (Darth, 75: 42 -> 60, 27 -> 38), Darth Zash (Darth, 80: 42 -> 63, 27 -> 40), Exal Kressh (Sith Lord, 45: Force Knowledge 25 -> 37). No read age is below the youngest that fits its standing (18 a Knight or Lord, 35 a Master or Darth, 45 the Councils); Oteg (Jedi Master, Yoda's species) is closest at a Human-equivalent 35.1. The estimates to check are in remeasure-2026-11-23f.md.
  - *[2] Nexus of Power (FFG) is not Legends* outside the economy set too. Of its 33 other mentions (lore files): 1 moved (SW_Species_OldRepublic_Part1, the Devaronian Force-sensitivity line, into a new Not used section; item 4 now reads "not stated in Legends"); 4 kept with the citation dropped, each backed by another Legends source (SW_Force_Powers_Part2a, the Je'daii Force weapon, by *The New Essential Chronology*; SW_Lightsabers_Crystals_Materials_AllEras, Vur Tepe, by *Dawn of the Jedi* 0; SW_Jedi_Order_Ranks_Council_OldRepublic, the Eye of Zallow by the *SWTOR Encyclopedia* and SWTOR, and the Archives and Council chamber by SWTOR; their [gray] tags removed). The other 28 needed no change: 18 already under Not used (or Not found), 6 notes stating the exclusion, 3 source-list entries, and 1 not the book (SW_Force_Nexus_Vergence: "a nexus of powerful dark side energy"). The source notes of the Daily Life, Ranks Council and Crystals files record the rule.
  - *[3] The Initiate Trials before 3959 BBY:* "all but one of the existing form groups" is confirmed (D-463 as built; no code change).
  - *[4] The M8 design notes are in the repo:* docs-v2/spec/m8-design-notes.md (exported from Claude Docs, revision 40; the Claude Docs version stays the working copy). D-461 [8] points to its "Sith acolyte curriculum" section and D-464 to its "Economy" section; it is listed in the spec index (docs-v2/spec/00-index.md) with the M8 checklist and the two world-event tag maps.

Exal Kressh 3723 -> 3710 BBY (the Emperor's apprentice early in the Great Galactic War, died 3678: about 32 at her sheet reads better than 45; a design estimate, not lore). Their estimated rank spans are re-derived from the new years (acolyte from 15, Lord at 25, Darth at 40): Acina acolyte 3680-3671, Lord 3670-3656, Darth from 3655; Kressh acolyte 3695-3686, Lord from 3685; both sheets keep their standing (Darth; Sith Lord). Bands at the new read ages: Darth Acina at 50, Force Knowledge 42, Meditation 27 (were 60 / 38 at 75); Exal Kressh at 32, Force Knowledge 25, Meditation 17 (were 37 / 24 at 45). The bandEffect probe now flags only Ragate and Darth Zash.
- **D-466 World events, Phase A: the tag network's engine (M8 checklist items 1 and 8; 2026-11-23).** Built from the M8 design notes ("World events: the tag network and divergence"); spec: docs-v2/spec/world-events.md. No era data yet (Phase B); tested on a made-up fixture. The choices below are proposals for Jared to rule on (docs-v2/reports/m8-world-a.md).
  - *[1] Breaking a tag:* only recorded state counts: a canon figure the engine recorded as dead or captured, who holds a place (new engine event locationControlChanged), a flag no AI source may set (new engine-only flag sceneOutcome:<id>; nothing sets one yet), another outcome, a check the engine rolled for a player action (new engine event worldActionRecorded, from the resolve step: the scene's own roll against the guard, no new dice), or a won fight. The narrator's npcCanonDiverged (captured, turned, removed) does not break a role: canonDiverged now records who recorded it (recordedBy), and the engine's own capture replaces a narrator's report.
  - *[2] Guards (every number `invented`):* low DC 15 and 1 point; medium DC 17 and 3; high DC 19 and 6, with a prerequisite (foreknowledge) first. An action scores by its grade (3 / 2 / 1 / -1), once per condition per day; a fact or won fight scores 1 / 3 / 6 (minor / major / decisive).
  - *[3] The scheduler:* events settle on their date, not when a tag breaks, producers first; a date known only to the year falls on its first day (as the control table); an event with an end date is under way until then.
  - *[4] Substitution:* the same side and role, the same title first, then the nearest age, then a known NPC, else unnamed; never a figure another unsettled event cannot do without.
  - *[5] Divergence:* a crucial role or condition alters its event unless the tag says cancelled; a broken required input cancels it. Outcomes can have their own rule, so an altered event can still bring some about. Broken tags and outcomes are final.
  - *[6] The freeze:* control, leader and seat rows can name the outcomes that start and end them (dependsOn, endsWith); the links are Phase B. Nothing is frozen in this build.
  - *[7] Plot points:* scored alternatives, chosen once when the tracker holds; a data check stops an alternative from producing any outcome of the recorded timeline.
  - *[8] Spec 14:* the Legacy Change framework, its priority tags, galaxyEventQueue, legacyChanges and lineageRecord are superseded (recorded in spec 14). The faction seeds keyframe is not.
- **D-467 Answers to m8-world-a, and world events Phase B: the 3,661-3,640 BBY network (Jared, 2026-11-23).**
  - *[1] Guards:* low, medium and high accepted as proposed for now; revisit after Phase B playtests. A very high guard is added (the prompt calls it the fifth; it is the fourth): DC 21, 10 points, two distinct prerequisites first; one decisive fact alone is not enough (numbers `invented`). networkProblems refuses a very high guard without two distinct prerequisites.
  - *[2] Captures:* strict; only engine-recorded deaths and captures break a role. *[3]* A divergence is decided on the event's date. *[5]* Stand-ins as built, with the rulings on top (protagonists and canon heroes are NPCs; Visz is replaced only if stopped or killed first). *[7]* A lost crucial figure's network consequence is held back to the event's date; the same now goes for a condition broken only by an earlier event's outcome.
  - *[4] Year-only dates:* the 3,661-3,660 and 3,654-3,653 chains have months, all tagged invented: the lore gives none (searched), only "mid-3,641" for the Treaty's collapse. The riots stand at the 3,661 start, as the M7 start has them (D-400), so they come before Devaron. The rows of a year are not dated against each other.
  - *[6] Foreknowledge:* its own step at a medium guard (3 points), or one recorded discovery fact; the same for very high's two.
  - *Tag map:* O.grand_master_vacant is optional for E.3651.tython, and O.grand_master_set depends on it; O.malgus_alive is dropped (Malgus is the crucial role at the Maelstrom and Ilum); very high added to its guard list.
  - *Recorded deaths* are frozen with the same links (src/data/canonDeathLinks.ts, 33 figures): a death whose event diverged does not happen; any other stands.
  - *Phase B:* src/data/worldNetwork3661.ts encodes every row, PP-1, PP-2 and the section 6 rulings. The breaking tests, prerequisites, scenes and their strengths are design calls (listed in docs-v2/reports/m8-world-b.md). Additions to the map: seven outcomes the freeze reads (Korriban taken, the single deaths, the 3,641 fronts, the 3,640 Council deaths); O.shange_imprisoned and O.raxxus_imprisoned are optional for Belsavis; a treaty incident needs a won lethal fight; a delayed collapse waits three years (`invented`). PP-2's "collapse on time" is the recorded path, so nothing is chosen for it.
  - *Engine changes found by the data:* an optional input off the record changes an event by coming about, not by staying away; only a fight of the last day counts; an outcome can change a place's control; a role can name a person the lore index does not have; the told news leaves out what only follows from an earlier event.
  - *Control and leaders:* Coruscant's return and the Grand Master vacancy now start inside 3,653 (their events' dates); links on the rows the events touch, and on the two Order seats.
  - *Maps:* world-event-tags-3640-1000BBY-draft.md committed as a reviewed draft (not encoded). In the 1000 BBY map, E.~990.zannah is E.980.zannah (Ambria, 980) and E.1000.thought_bomb consumes O.ruusan_endgame and O.thought_bomb_known. The schema loads both maps; the gaps are in the report.
- **D-468 Answers to m8-world-b (Jared, 2026-11-24).**
  - *[1] Foreknowledge as protection:* enough; accidental breaks of low and medium conditions are fine. General rule: an investigation or foreknowledge action counts only when it starts from a lead recorded in play (a discovery fact, a met figure, a found item or place); with none it scores nothing, and out-of-game knowledge never counts. Built as `leads` in the network's data, in the spec and in networkProblems. A fact or story thread recorded about a place is a lead; being at the place is not (as built: for Jared to confirm). Found items wait for items.
  - *[2] Quesh, Balmorra's liberation and the Taris invasion* have conditions (design calls; m8-world-c.md), so PP-2's delay is any four of seven.
  - *[3] Invented months* for 3643, 3642 and 3640, in the rows' order; Karrid's assault last in 3640. The Council deaths run from August to December, because Karrid's death is among them.
  - *[4] The occupying navy* waits for ship combat; the ground-fight stopgap stays.
  - *[5] Total defeat* needs the side's capital taken (or its main fleet destroyed, which waits for ship combat) plus three lost leaders or major defeats (`invented`). Nothing in play takes a capital yet.
  - *[6] The Brotherhood of Darkness's headquarters* are on Korriban in src/data/orderSeats.ts (was Roon; D-451 follow-up).
  - *Maps and notes:* world-event-tags-1000BBY-4ABY-draft.md (Jared's update of 24 Nov) committed and the load check re-run; "a fourth level" in the 3640-1000 map; m8-design-notes.md re-exported at revision 41 (one changed cell since revision 40).
- **D-469 World events Phase C: running a blockade (M8 checklist item 4; 2026-11-24).** One general system (spec: blockades.md), with the Hydian Way blockade as its first instance. It replaces the flat refusal of D-405 [2] / D-406 in travel.config.ts (the strip and the detour stay), and its end is E.3660.hydian_battle, so travel and the control line now change together (this supersedes D-411 [2]'s "open from the start of 3,660": 15 January 3,660, or never if it is not broken). Every number, and the answers to the design notes' open questions (checks, costs, consequences, one approach turning into another, renown), are proposals for Jared, not settled (docs-v2/reports/m8-world-c.md).
  - New flags, engine only and with no setter yet: blockadeTactics, blockadePapers:<id>, shipSystem:<system>, fleetCommand.
  - Costs and fines go to a ledger until the economy; fame is recorded for renown.
  - The check engine takes any rating (checkRating), not only the five difficulties.
- **D-470 Answers to m8-world-c (Jared, 2026-11-24).**
  - *[1] Odds:* accepted for now; revisit after playtests. "Talk through" is renamed **undercover** everywhere (approach id, choices, intent wording, spec, tests; v3 saves migrate): the default intelligence and military route (fake IDs, codes, cover stories, through connections). Checks, costs and odds unchanged.
  - *[2]* The Mandalorians count as a trained military. At 60 days and more it changes nothing; it only closes the holes of the first days.
  - *[3]* 60 days on 1 January 3661 is kept, `invented`: a known stretch (Legends gives the year of the launch, no month), a game convenience that keeps the M7 start's hunger and riots.
  - *[4] Leads:* a recorded fact or thread about a place counts, with a limit: learned in a scene, pointing to a place or a person, never a future event's secret stated outright. Built: turn-0 backstory and a start option's threads (until play takes them up) do not count; a text that repeats 60% (`invented`) of the significant words of the event's title, summary or a condition does not count. The narrator's contract says the same. Being there still does not count.
  - *[5]* Sneaking, papers and fleets stay closed until ships, items and command are built.
  - *[6] Captures* go through the fight's captor rules (D-242, D-225): prisoner, ship impounded, cargo seized. New narrator event captivityEnded, legal only for a prisoner the engine recorded and on a later turn. What waits for the ships build is in spec/blockades.md section 5.
  - *Proposals A-I:* accepted as invented starting numbers. Forcing through needs an ace pilot (Piloting 90) by design; a specialised stealth ship will lower it once ships are built.
  - *Maps and notes:* world-event-tags-1000BBY-4ABY-draft.md committed as found on disk (E.16.maul_dies and the plot-point status line); it does not hold the PP-5 rework yet. The M8 design notes re-exported at revision 42, the latest there is (the prompt names 43); both named changes are in it.
- **D-471 Captivity is the engine's; PP-5 and PP-6 reworked (Jared, 2026-11-24).**
  - *Maps:* world-event-tags-1000BBY-4ABY-draft.md committed (Jared's chat, 24 Nov: PP-5 and PP-6 in player-reachable steps; PP-6 alternative 3, Galen survives the Death Star). It loads (tests/m8/otherMaps.test.ts). New gaps 15-20 in m8-world-b.md.
  - *Ruled:* the narrator no longer decides when a prisoner goes free (amends D-470's captivityEnded, which the AI can no longer send). Escape is a check the player chooses; rescue is an engine chance from the player's allies, rolled daily; else release, ransom or transfer at 14 days. Rules: spec/blockades.md section 5; numbers: src/data/captivity.config.ts, all `invented`.
  - *Design calls to rule on:* one record covers fight and blockade captures (a fight's captive is no longer freed by moving place); winning a real fight while held frees the character; release and ransom return the ship, a break-out only on a margin of 8 or more; captors who take prisoners to kill them follow the same limit; one transfer at most. World slice v5.
- **D-472 Species ages: adult, prime and decline (Jared, 2026-11-24; M8 item 2).** Rules and numbers: spec/species.md section 1; src/data/ageing.config.ts (all `invented`), src/engine/ageing.ts.
  - *Three ages:* adult, prime and lifespan on straight lines, read as Human 18, 30 and 100. A species with no sourced prime keeps its old line exactly; only the Wookiee moves (adult 18, prime 200; the house-default maturity of 60 is dropped). `house default` is renamed `invented` across the species data.
  - *Decline:* Strength and Endurance keep the drop the Human caps already had; Agility starts sooner; the trained level now falls as well as the cap. Tracked for the player day by day in the progression slice (`ageing`, optional: old saves need no migration), in closed form for NPCs, and from each sheet's date for canon figures.
  - *Force Control* stretches the years after prime up to +100 Human-equivalent years at 110 (the body of 100 at 200). This is the scaled rule D-399 [5] waited for, so it covers every species (m6-integration item 33). Yoda's species is excepted: its 900 comes from Jedi.
  - *Overrides logged:* D-399 [5]'s "not species with their own lifespan data"; species.md section 1's single maturity; progression-numbers sections 11, 11a and 16 (notes added there).
- **D-473 Species traits for the 33 species of lore batch 21 (Jared, 2026-11-24; M8 item 3).** Rules and numbers: spec/species.md section 3a; src/data/speciesTraits.ts, TRAIT_RESISTANCE in forceResistance.config.ts (all numbers `invented`).
  - *Traits:* six kinds, each citing its species entry (a test opens the file and finds it). The Part 3 Game notes were used only where an entry backs them. The Wookiee gains the drawback the design notes name (cannot speak Basic).
  - *Body scale* from the lore height over 1.75 m, for 27 species; the house defaults go (Talz 1.29, Houk 1.26, Jawa 0.57, Yoda's species 0.4; the Hutt keeps 1.5, invented, having no height). *Override (proposed):* for these 33 species size no longer scales Agility (a Hutt would have had the quickest body in the game); the 12 older species are unchanged.
  - *Override of species.md section 3:* the two Force resistances add 3 to a contest, past the old limit of 1.
  - *Mind-shielded Epicanthix* ("some" in the lore) stay a per-figure flag; nobody carries it yet, for lack of a source. The narrator is told "some".
  - *Exceptions:* ownForceTraits on CanonNpc, beside insideForce.
  - *Text only, no engine yet:* senses, needs and social traits; radiation, cold, disease and vacuum; claws, venom and the bellow; limb regrowth.
- **D-474 Answers to m8-species (Jared, 2026-11-24).** Rules: spec/species.md sections 1, 3 and 3a; progression-targets section 2; progression-numbers section 20.
  - *[1] Luke's late life:* the gate "effective physical performance peaks at 58-68" is replaced by two. The body: Strength, Agility and Endurance, without the Force boost, have each peaked by 68 and are declining slowly (at most 5% off the peak at 75; the 5% is `invented`). The Force: Force Control, Force Sense and Force Speed (the Force skills he trains) are still rising at 63, his last appearance (Fate of the Jedi), with no upper bound. The stretch stays as built. No other profile gate mixes body and Force.
  - *[2] The Average's per-hour table:* the rows at 70, 80, 90 and 98 (every skill's, the forms' fallback and the whole-training fallback) re-measured on the 20-seed medians under the ageing rules; they are the 4.0x clamp's reference. Rows under 70 and Y_avg unchanged.
  - *[3] Training in real years:* new training age: childhood at the species' pace (adult reads 18), then a year a year. The closed form integrates over the years lived; rank ages, training budgets, learning speed outside the physical stats, the mind's natural levels and the canon knowledge bands read it. The body (ceilings, decline, physical training) keeps the Human-equivalent age, and so does the 4.0x table's read age. *Override of D-103 and D-399:* "learning reads the Human-equivalent age". NPCs still accrue no more past a full life (D-429), and are now held to their species' ceilings.
  - *Found with it (fixed):* the generator read an archetype's start age as species years, so a Jedi or Sith Chiss or Hutt (and any species adult early or late) failed its start-age check and had no levels. It is a training age now.
  - *[4] Bodies:* size no longer scales Agility for any species, nor Strength. Ceilings come from cited entries, set directly: Wookiee Strength 120, Agility 100 (both as before); Hutt Strength 120 (as before; its entry gives toughness and 1,500 kg, no word on strength); Houk 115 ("rivalled only by Wookiees"); Trandoshan 100 ("much stronger than Humans"); Mirialan Agility 90 ("great agility and very quick reflexes"). Talz Strength stays +5. Everyone else is at the Human 80 (the Twi'lek and Cathar Agility +5 stay). Every number `invented`. Size still scales the Endurance ceiling, which the ruling does not name (a question in the report).
  - *The +3 Force resistance* is accepted where the lore gives strong resistance, and species.md's limit updated. The Hutt stays immune to mind tricks, as the Force lore says they do not work on Hutts.
  - *[5] Captivity:* every design call of D-471 accepted as proposed. The numbers stay `invented`.
  - *Lore:* the 16 overnight timeline files and lore-overnight-2026-11-24.md committed as drafts for review and indexed. The narrator's index reads every SW_Timeline file, so the drafts reach the narrator beside the old timeline's rows (questions in the report). Vandar Tokare's age queued.
  - *Maps:* the overnight corrections applied to both drafts, citing the new files: the third Galactic War ended before 3600; Corellia beat both sides in 3017 and forced the peace; Rans Chancellor from 3630 (E.3630.rans); Bane at the Academy in 1002 and on Lehon in 1000 (E.1002.bane_joins, E.1000.bane_lehon); the Zeison Sha's origin marked [conflict]; Owen Lars shoots Maul; the Inquisitorius 19 BBY (E.19.inquisitorius); the Declaration 2 BBY (E.2.declaration); the Valley of the Jedi approved after 990 (E.990.valley_memorial, a new row).
